To mitigate this issue, we propose Volumetric J&F, which evaluates mask sequences as spatio-temporal volumes and reduces the dominance of target-absence rewards while preserving sensitivity to segmentation quality and temporal structure. Project page: https://aidaslab.github.io/FaVOS.

</details>


### [37] [TerraVis: Towards Evaluation of World-Grounded Visual Consistency in Text-to-Image Generation via MLLM Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02959)
*Shuai Fu,Jing Gu,Jian Zhou,Zicheng Duan,Gengze Zhou,Qi Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TerraVis引入了一种评估生成图像世界一致性框架，通过多阶段评估发现和量化世界一致性违规，为生成图像的评估提供了新的视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的忠实度、美观度、偏好度或对齐度度量难以充分捕捉生成图像中的现实一致性问题，而这些问题是现有模型的常见缺陷。TerraVis旨在弥合这一差距。

Method: TerraVis定义了一个结构化的世界一致性违规分类系统，并使用多阶段评估框架来识别和量化这些违规情况。首先使用MLLM评估图像的资格；其次检测18种分类定义的违规类型，并将它们分为轻微或严重分类，以得出总体世界一致性评分。

Result: TerraVis在多个开源和专有文本-图像模型上表现最佳，与现有度量的人类判断一致性的相关性最强。进一步表明，即使在传统指标上表现良好的模型也可能存在显著的世界一致性问题。

Conclusion: TerraVis通过提供系统化的方法来量化、诊断和比较生成图像的世界一致性缺陷，实现了世界一致性评估维度的补充。

Abstract: Recent text-to-image models have made substantial progress in photorealism, aesthetics, and text-image alignment. Yet visually appealing images can still violate real-world plausibility, exhibiting malformed object structures, impossible anatomy, physically implausible interactions, or inconsistent spatial relationships. Such failures are not well captured by existing fidelity, aesthetics, preference, or alignment metrics. To address this gap, we introduce TerraVis, a framework for evaluating world-grounded visual consistency in generated images. TerraVis defines a structured taxonomy of world-consistency violations spanning object-, interaction-, and scene-level failures, and employs a multi-stage evaluation framework to identify and quantify them. Given an image, TerraVis first uses an MLLM to assess its eligibility for evaluation, then detects violations across 18 taxonomy-defined types and classifies them as minor or major to derive an overall world-consistency score. Across diverse open-source and proprietary text-to-image models on two widely used benchmarks, TerraVis achieves the strongest correlation with human judgments of world consistency among existing metrics. Our benchmark results further show that models that achieve strong performance on conventional metrics can still exhibit substantial world-consistency failures. These findings highlight world consistency as a complementary evaluation dimension and demonstrate that TerraVis enables systematic quantification, diagnosis, and comparison of such failures. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/ShyFoo/TerraVis.

</details>


### [38] [Post-Training Frontier Text-to-Image Models by Composing Preference and Rubric Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02967)
*Yuanhao Ban,I-Hung Hsu,Anastasios Angelopoulos,Wei-Lin Chiang,Ion Stoica,Cho-Jui Hsieh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种简单有效的后训练策略，用于开放域的文本到图像生成，通过组合互补的奖励信号提高了模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管现有的文本到图像生成模型在视觉质量上取得了显著进步，但在通过后训练进一步提升时仍存在挑战，主要是因为没有单一的奖励信号能够捕捉所有的人类偏好。

Method: 本文提出了一个基于互补奖励信号组合的后训练方法，其中包括一个通过大型人类偏好数据训练的偏好奖励，以及一个评估提示忠实度和其他优质属性的评分体系。此外，通过设计一种简单的奖励组合策略，以有效平衡偏好优化与评分满足。

Result: 在Arena文字到图像排行榜上，基于强化学习训练的Flux2dev达到了比基础模型高69分的Elo评级，而后训练后的Ideogram-4则超过了所有开源模型，达到了1223.5的Elo评级。

Conclusion: 这些结果表明，有效的奖励对于前沿生成模型的训练至关重要，需要广泛覆盖用户意图并具备在优化过程中抗exploit的鲁棒性。为了支持可重复的研究，作者发布了Arena-T2I-Training，一个包含1000个训练数据子集，旨在促进后续关于文本到图像模型后训练工作的发展。

Abstract: Recent text-to-image generation models have achieved remarkable visual quality, but improving them through post-training remains challenging because no single reward signal captures the full range of human preference. In this work, we develop a simple and effective post-training recipe for open-domain text-to-image generation based on the composition of complementary reward signals. Our reward system consists of two main components: a preference reward, trained on large-scale human preference data using a Bradley-Terry objective to capture overall human aesthetic and perceptual preferences, and rubric-based rewards, which explicitly evaluate prompt faithfulness and other desirable properties while providing safeguards against reward hacking. A key challenge is how to combine these heterogeneous reward signals. We show that a naive weighted average leads to suboptimal optimization behavior, and propose a simple reward composition strategy that more effectively balances preference optimization with rubric satisfaction. In the Arena text-to-image leaderboard (https://arena.ai/), our RL-trained Flux2dev achieves an Elo rating 69 points above the base model, and our post-trained Ideogram-4 surpasses every open-source model on the leaderboard, reaching an Elo of 1223.5. (Claims of state-of-the-art performance are based on the Arena leaderboard snapshot as of September 4, 2026.) Our results suggest that effective rewards for frontier generative-model training require broad coverage of user intent and robustness to exploitation under optimization. To support reproducible research, we release Arena-T2I-Training, a 1K subset of training data that recovers some gains of full-scale training, providing a resource that we hope will facilitate future work on post-training for text-to-image models.

</details>


### [39] [Rethinking Fixed Temporal Grids: Frequency-Disentangled Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03012)
*Yunjiao Zhou,Junlang Qian,Gen Li,Xinying Guo,Lihua Xie,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreqMo 是一种可自适应尺度的运动表示方法，它将运动分解为小波频率带，允许不同时间尺度的动力学分离，并保持了时间定位和精确重构。Unified Frequency Residual Quantization（UFRQ）将所有带在单一共享码本中进行编码，实现了三倍的压缩，有利于单一阶段的稳定生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的人体运动生成方法将运动编码为均匀时间网格上的令牌，但这种方法无法准确捕捉运动的多尺度特性，导致慢速和快速动态之间的频率交织。

Method: FreqMo 方法通过对运动进行小波分解，将运动按频率带分离，然后使用统一频率残差量化（UFRQ）在一个共享码本中编码所有频率带，实现了运动表示的尺度自适应。

Result: 实验结果显示，FreqMo 达到了 SOTA 的保真度，同时在高频率保留方面有了显著改善，其分解特性还能适用于连续的扩散模型。

Conclusion: FreqMo 提供了一种有效的解决人体运动多尺度动态问题的方法，并且在模型部署时有助于简化过程。

Abstract: Most human motion generation methods encode motion as tokens on a uniform temporal grid, where every token spans the same fixed time window. Human motion, however, is temporally heterogeneous: slowly evolving global trajectories coexist with rapid transient events such as foot contacts and joint impulses. Forcing such multi-scale dynamics onto tokens of identical temporal resolution entangles motion frequencies, leaving slow regions redundant while smoothing out the rapid details that distinguish realistic motion. We propose \textbf{FreqMo}, a scale-adaptive motion representation that decomposes motion into wavelet frequency bands, separating dynamics across temporal scales while preserving temporal localization and exact reconstruction. Unified Frequency Residual Quantization (UFRQ) then encodes all bands within a single shared codebook, compressing the token sequence threefold and enabling stable single-stage generation. Experiments show FreqMo attains SOTA fidelity with substantially improved high-frequency preservation, and the same decomposition transfers to continuous diffusion backbones.

</details>


### [40] [RYOPO: Bringing End-to-End Category-Level Object Pose Estimation into Real Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03013)
*Hakjin Lee,Junghoon Seo,Jaehoon Sim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种端到端可训练的基于查询的RGB-D集合预测器ours，可以联合检测和分割对象，并估计它们的9-DoF姿态，同时避免了独立训练实例分割器的需求和物体相关的处理成本。该模型在NOCS上显著改进了已发布的RGB-D联合检测和姿态估计结果，并且在REAL275和HouseCat6D上实现了与两阶段方法竞争的表现，同时支持实时全帧姿态估计，帧率为31.8 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有准确的姿态估计方法依赖于外部实例分割、单独的姿态估计阶段以及物体特定的处理成本，这阻碍了实时推理。为了将准确的姿态估计带入实时领域，该研究提出了ours。

Method: ours采用了一种端到端可训练的基于查询的方法，通过共享图像和场景编码避免了重复的对象裁剪编码。它包含了一个查询条件的几何路径，将观察到的3D点和RGB特征与对象查询关联起来，并整合了共享的场景上下文。对象为中心的精炼使用生成的目标点描述符通过姿态条件下的交叉注意力和递归残差修正更新显式姿态状态。

Result: 在NOCS基准上，ours显著提高了已发布的RGB-D联合检测和姿态估计结果。相对于两阶段方法，其在全方位评估中表现竞争，在REAL275和HouseCat6D上，实现31.8 FPS的实时全帧姿态估计。

Conclusion: 该研究提升了实时姿态估计的能力，提供了一种有效减少处理成本的端到端训练方法。

Abstract: Category-level object pose estimation predicts the rotation, translation, and metric size of unseen instances within known categories. Many accurate RGB-D methods rely on external instance segmentation and crop-based pose estimation, introducing separate stages and object-dependent processing costs that hinder real-time inference. To bring accurate pose estimation into real time, we present \ours{}, an end-to-end trainable query-based RGB-D set predictor. It jointly detects and segments objects and estimates their \mbox{9-DoF} poses without explicit CAD-derived shape priors or a separately trained instance segmentor. Shared image and scene encoding avoids repeated per-object crop encoding. A query-conditioned geometry pathway associates observed 3D points and RGB features with object queries and incorporates shared scene context. Object-centric refinement uses the resulting point descriptors to update an explicit pose state through pose-conditioned cross-attention and recurrent residual corrections. On NOCS, \ours{} substantially improves on published RGB-D joint detection and pose estimation results. It achieves competitive performance compared with two-stage methods under all-object evaluation on REAL275 and HouseCat6D, while enabling real-time full-frame pose estimation at $31.8$ FPS on an RTX~A6000. Project page: https://yopo-series.github.io/RYOPO-project-page/.

</details>


### [41] [OmniAct3D: Leveraging Foundation Geometry and Evidence-Grounded Reasoning for Panoramic 3D Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03015)
*Runtong Wu,Fei Teng,Di Wen,Guoqiang Zhao,Kunyu Peng,Kailun Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了OmniAct3D框架，一种将视角训练的Vision Foundation Models (VFMs)检测器适应到全景图像中的方法，通过引入ERP-Ray Geometry Adapter, Visual-Action Reasoning Chain和Appearance-Guided Heading Expert，提高了3D检测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于传统的基于视角训练的3D检测器难以直接应用于全景投影（ERP）图像，本文旨在改进现有方法，提高3D检测精度。

Method: OmniAct3D框架包含三个组成部分：ERP-Ray Geometry Adapter, Visual-Action Reasoning Chain和Appearance-Guided Heading Expert。这些组件分别解决几何匹配问题、在全景场景中进行证据定位并恢复局部线索。

Result: 实验结果显示，OmniAct3D在Spheriverse上的3D检测精度提高了2.96 NDS点，并在PanoMMOcc上相对于未适应的VFMs基线提高了24.87 mAP点。VARC组件在保持相同配置的情况下，保持了约95-98%的3D推理。

Conclusion: OmniAct3D框架展示了在全景投影图像上进行3D检测的有效性，并为未来将VFMs应用于360度场景奠定了基础。

Abstract: Accurate 3D detection is essential for mobile embodied agents, while Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) offer transferable visual and geometric priors. Yet existing VFM-based 3D detectors rely on narrow-view monocular images or discrete perspective views, limiting coherent surround perception; equirectangular projection (ERP) instead encodes a continuous 360 scene in a single image. Direct transfer remains difficult because ERP organizes geometry and visual information differently, making object-relevant cues hard to model, localize, and preserve. We propose OmniAct3D, a framework that adapts perspective-trained VFM detectors to ERP while preserving transferable VFM priors. To resolve geometric mismatch, the ERP-Ray Geometry Adapter (ERGA-Ray) models spherical viewing rays and periodic spatial structure. To localize evidence in scene-wide context, the Visual-Action Reasoning Chain (VARC) grounds each hypothesis in relevant panoramic evidence and converts it into a structured geometric action. To recover local cues lost under fixed token budgets, the Appearance-Guided Heading Expert (AGHE) re-encodes object regions at higher resolution for heading estimation. Experiments show that OmniAct3D improves over the previous best 3D detector by 2.96 NDS points on Spheriverse and over the unadapted VFM baseline by 24.87 mAP points on PanoMMOcc. With target-specific geometry adaptation, VARC retains 95--98% of the same-configuration mAP, indicating reusable object-level 3D reasoning across sensing configurations. The source code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/FeiT-FeiTeng/OmniAct3D.

</details>


### [42] [ReSCUE: Re-translation with Sentence Commitment for Unsegmented Long-Form Simultaneous Sign Language Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03022)
*Sihan Ren,Gaozheng Li,Yuanshang Quan,Yiming Qin,Fuyi Yang,Chang Liu,Lan Xu,Minye Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReSCUE 是一个统一框架，用于处理未分割的长时间手语视频，通过结合推理感知训练、稳定重译和句子承诺机制，在低延迟设置下实现了最佳翻译质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的手语翻译方法主要集中在假设预分割输入的离线句层设置上，无法适应真实场景中的连续、未分割视频流。

Method: ReSCUE 统一框架通过结合推理感知训练处理部分输入、非手语停顿和多句上下文，采用稳定重译机制实现低延迟的可修正预测，并利用句子承诺机制进行在线分句和内存管理。

Result: 在标准句层基准测试中，ReSCUE 在低延迟设置下实现了更低的延迟和最佳的翻译质量。在长时间未分割数据集上，ReSCUE 的翻译质量接近使用真实句子边界的先验离线系统的质量，而操作延迟显著降低。

Conclusion: ReSCUE 证明了其实用性，适用于现实世界的视频流场景。

Abstract: Simultaneous Sign Language Translation (SLT) is critical for real-time communication, yet existing methods remain largely confined to sentence-level, offline settings that assume pre-segmented inputs. These assumptions hinder deployment in realistic scenarios involving continuous, unsegmented video streams. We present ReSCUE, a unified framework for simultaneous SLT on unsegmented long-form sign language videos that aligns training and inference with realistic streaming conditions. ReSCUE combines inference-aware training to handle partial inputs, non-signing pauses, and multi-sentence contexts, stabilized re-translation to enable low-latency yet revisable predictions with reduced output flicker, and a sentence commitment mechanism for online segmentation and memory management. Experiments on standard sentence-level benchmarks show that ReSCUE achieves lower latency and the best translation quality under low-latency settings. On long-form unsegmented datasets, ReSCUE approaches the translation quality of oracle offline systems that use ground-truth sentence boundaries, while operating at substantially lower latency, demonstrating its practicality for real-world streaming scenarios.

</details>


### [43] [CrowdOcc: Monocular Semantic Scene Completion for Quadruped Robots in Crowded Indoor Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03031)
*Feiyang Chen,Jincheng Hu,Yiduo Chen,Jihao Li,Yue Liang,Bingzhao Gao,Yanjun Huang,Yuanjian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CrowdOcc，一个针对四足机器人在拥挤室内环境下的单目语义场景完成数据集和框架，实现了在静态动态解耦注释下的单目语义场景完成任务，性能达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在拥挤的室内环境中，四足机器人面对遮挡的静态几何结构和不完整的空间人物预测，该研究旨在提供一种更加鲁棒的方法来完成这些任务。

Method: 该方法结合了引导表面法线场景几何融合（NGSGF）算法和以人体为中心的稀疏交互（HCSI）算法，以增强场景几何的连贯性和人物交互的理解。

Result: 这种方法在CrowdOcc环境下实现了最先进的语义场景完成性能， IoU为15.80，mIoU为11.40，人体IoU为46.23。

Conclusion: 该研究促进了四足机器人在复杂室内环境下的单目语义场景理解能力，为未来的相关研究提供了坚实的基础。

Abstract: Monocular semantic scene completion (SSC) for quadruped robots remains underexplored in real crowded indoor environments, where human-scene occlusion disrupts static geometry and human occupancy predictions are often incomplete or spatially misplaced. We present CrowdOcc, an RGB-D dataset and monocular SSC framework for this setting. CrowdOcc contains 25.1K frames from 11 indoor scenes, with semantic occupancy annotations constructed through static dynamic decoupling. Our framework combines: (i) Normal Guided Scene Geometry Fusion (NGSGF) to complement depth-aware lifting with surface-normal cues for occlusion robust geometry; and (ii) Human-Centric Sparse Interaction (HCSI) to selectively model human-human and local human scene relations in 3D. Our method achieves state-of-the-art SSC performance on CrowdOcc's scene-disjoint test set, reaching 15.80 IoU, 11.40 mIoU, and 46.23 Human IoU, demonstrating generalization to unseen indoor scenes.

</details>


### [44] [Parasitic Co-Denoising: Unlocking 3D Human Motion Generation in a Frozen Video Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03047)
*Yunjiao Zhou,Junlang Qian,Lihua Xie,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种从大型文本到视频扩散模型中提取隐式3D动作信号的方法，无需训练单独的动作模型，从而实现高效的3D动作生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的大规模文本到视频扩散模型能够生成逼真的人体动作，但这些模型并未经过专门训练以生成3D动作。本文提出了一种从这些已有的扩散模型中提取有效3D动作信息的新方法，从而实现了无需重新训练扩散模型即生成3D动作的效果。

Method: 该方法首先验证了大型扩散模型在去噪过程中有可恢复的运动信号，并在此基础上提出了“寄生除噪”（Parasitic Co-denoising）的概念。研究者开发了一种高效的流匹配解码器（Parasitic Motion Decoder, PMD）来从主模型的中间特征中提取运动信息，并通过σ-自适应多层融合方式共享主模型的噪声时间表。

Result: 所提出的PMD方法可以在不消耗大量参数的情况下，实现文本与动作的一体化生成，并且在文本-动作对齐方面明显优于专门训练的动作生成器。PMD生成的动作和视频可以在单次传递中输出，相比之下单一的动作基线方法无法达到这一效果。

Conclusion: 本研究表明，可以通过大规模文本到视频扩散模型的隐式知识高效生成3D动作，未来的研究可以进一步探索如何提高PMD方法的动作质量及应用范围。

Abstract: Despite never being supervised on explicit 3D motion, large-scale text-to-video diffusion models synthesize realistic human motion in their generated videos. We ask whether this implicit knowledge can be turned into explicit 3D motion generation, without training a separate motion model. Probing a frozen Wan2.1 reveals that a recoverable motion signal is present in its intermediate states across the entire denoising schedule, not confined to the clean output. Motivated by this, we introduce parasitic co-denoising, a paradigm in which motion is decoded from the host model along its denoising schedule rather than produced by an independent generator. We instantiate it as the Parasitic Motion Decoder (PMD), an efficient flow-matching decoder that shares the host's noise schedule and reads its intermediate features through a $σ$-adaptive multi-layer fusion, leaving the host unmodified. Drawing its coverage from the host rather than from motion data, PMD leads dedicated motion generators on text-motion alignment at a small fraction of their trainable parameters, while producing paired video and motion in a single pass that motion-only baselines cannot match.

</details>


### [45] [BeeWhere: Segmenting Bumble Bee Colonies to Quantify Behavioral Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03051)
*Roberta Hunt,August Easton-Calabria,James Crall*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BeeWhere 是一种 AI 辅助的注释和分析工作流程，它结合了 ArUco 检测和深度学习实例分割，用于从高分辨率群体图像和视频中量化bumble bee 的行为。这种方法比基于标记的跟踪更准确，特别是在蜜蜂被部分遮挡或成像条件复杂的情况下。此外，实例分割还可以捕捉到标记跟踪所无法识别的处理引起的蜜蜂空间组织变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前蜜蜂监测工作流程中的挑战是难以在密集、遮挡的巢穴环境中进行大规模测量，且标签基于跟踪（标记）方法在标记被遮挡时会失效，提供的信息有限。因此，研究旨在开发一种能够处理这些限制的 AI 辅助工作流程。

Method: 该方法结合了 ArUco 检测和实例分割模型，使用 YOLO 对标记的 bumble bee 和环境元素进行实例分割。

Result: 该方法在 bumble bee (Bombus impatiens) 微型群体上进行了 483 帧、8,443 只蜜蜂实例的注释，并建立了 YOLO 实例分割模型。结果显示，与基于标记的跟踪相比，该方法在部分遮挡或复杂成像条件下能够提高检测率，同时捕捉到处理引起的蜜蜂空间组织变化。

Conclusion: 实例分割能够在困难的群体条件下恢复行为相关的信号，比基于标记的跟踪更全面，为蜜蜂行为研究提供了一种有力的新工具。

Abstract: Social bees are important pollinators that support biodiversity and crop pollination globally and serve as important model systems for collective behavior, but scalable measurement of individual- and colony-level behavior remains difficult in dense, occluded nest environments. Existing monitoring workflows use fiducial tags (e.g., ArUco) to preserve individual identity, yet tag-based tracking can fail when markers are obscured and provide limited information about body extent, spatial context, and untagged individuals. We present BeeWhere, an AI-assisted annotation and analysis workflow that combines ArUco detections with deep-learnt instance segmentations to quantify bumble bee behavior from high-resolution colony images and videos. Using bumble bee (Bombus impatiens) microcolonies as a test case, we annotate 483 frames containing 8,443 bee instances. We additionally annotate pollen balls, nest structures, and chamber boundaries, and train YOLO instance segmentation models for downstream behavioral analysis. Instance segmentations enable quantification of important behavioral metrics based on body contours, including nearest-neighbor distance, proximity to nest structures, spatial occupancy within the nest, and detection counts over time. We apply the BeeWhere models to tag-based tracking in an exploratory validation study assessing the behavioral impacts of neonicotinoid pesticide exposure. BeeWhere increased detection rates compared to tag-based tracking, particularly when bees were partially obscured or under challenging imaging conditions, and also captured treatment-associated changes in bee spatial organization not captured using tag-based tracking alone. These results suggest that instance segmentation can complement fiducial-marker tracking by recovering behaviorally meaningful signals under challenging colony conditions.

</details>


### [46] [Where to Look Is Not How to Fix: Pre-Denoising Diagnostics and Modality-Dependent Control in Diffusion Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03068)
*Fangzheng Wu,Brian Summa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过控制锚点-压力协议研究了文本到图像扩散中的组合故障，引入了文本组合压力指数（CSI），并发现嵌入适配器减少残留，下游交叉注意力提高色度命中率（CHR），不同解码器对不同的干预具有不同的响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究图像生成过程中可组合的错误机制，通过引入CSI和特定干预措施，探索不同编码器和解码器模块的效果差异。

Method: 采用控制锚点-压力协议，结合文本编码器诊断和去噪干预，研究不同文本和图像模块的响应。

Result: CSI成功地区分了常见和罕见的组合，并揭示了不同模块对嵌入适配器和交叉注意力的影响。特定的干预措施可以减少残留，提升CHR，但不同结构（如深编码器和解码器）的响应不同。

Conclusion: 组合缺陷在去噪之前是可以诊断的，但暴露风险的坐标并不总是能改善生成的表现。

Abstract: Understanding compositional failures in text-to-image diffusion requires identifying both where stress is detectable and how intervention changes the output. We study these questions through a controlled anchor--stress protocol that jointly evaluates text-encoder diagnostics and denoiser interventions. We introduce a text-only Compositional Stress Index (CSI), which separates common from rare compositions across SD1.5, SDXL, and the SD3 text path and provides an upstream diagnostic coordinate. A matched six-prompt localization study links intervention location to distinct outcomes: residual-minimizing embedding adapters improve representation fit, while downstream cross-attention intervention increases color hit rate (CHR) by 0.0272. Across SD1.5 and SDXL denoiser blocks, the largest positive signed diagnostic-accessibility mean occurs at the deep encoder, whereas selective boost has its largest positive mean CHR response at decoder blocks. Selective subtraction and broad ablation reveal further modality- and architecture-dependent responses, including a substantial CHR decrease when SDXL decoder cross-attention is broadly ablated. We find a diagnosis-control dissociation under our controlled attribute-object composition setting: compositional defects are diagnosable before denoising, but the representation coordinate that exposes risk is not necessarily the coordinate or modality that improves generation.

</details>


### [47] [NegT2IBench: When Negation Changes the Picture. A Polarity Benchmark for Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03084)
*Omar Elfatairy,Maria A. Bravo,Jessica Bader,Zeynep Akata*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NegT2IBench 是一个新基准，包含4,800个具有不同复杂性和语义的提示，专注于评估生成图像对否定性约束的遵守情况，使用基于检测器的评分方式，能够明确指出哪些要求未被满足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的 T2I 基准主要关注生成图像是否包含所需内容，忽略了评估图像对否定性约束的遵守情况。因此，有必要开发一个新基准（NegT2IBench），专门评估在不同因素影响下的生成图像对否定性约束的遵守效果。

Method: NegT2IBench 包含4,800个提示，覆盖了两种属性类型和四种关系类别。提示按极性分类，分为需要满足的正面陈述和不应满足的负面陈述，每个范围从0到2。通过检测器进行评分，该评分方法具有可复现性、可审计性和能精确定位失败原因的特点。在600个有三个注释者的图像上进行测试，并将其结果进行比对。

Result: 在针对600个图像进行了三个注释者的评分后，NegT2IBench 的评分与视觉语言评判人的意见高度一致，但使用了更少的 GPU 内存。当在11种 T2I 模型上测试时，有9种模型在包含否定陈述的一次评分下得分较低。此外，研究表明，色度是主要的失败因素，而接近度的失败非常少，表明 41.5% 的失败陈述对否定性要求的遵守极为精确。

Conclusion: NegT2IBench 通过直接测量生成图像对否定性约束的遵守情况，为诊断否定性约束的失败提供了严格的测试环境，同时激励进一步发展相关方法以克服这些挑战。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models are judged by benchmarks that measure whether requested content appears, but these benchmarks largely overlook the complementary ability to satisfy negated constraints, for example, generating "a non-red cup." Measuring negation raises challenges not faced by affirmation-based benchmarks and requires careful prompt and evaluation design. We introduce NegT2IBench, a benchmark of 4,800 prompts covering two attribute types and four relation categories. Prompts are organized by polarity: the number of positive statements that must hold and negated statements that must not, each ranging from 0 to 2. Varying the two independently separates the effect of negation from the effect of prompt complexity. Our detector-based scoring is reproducible, auditable, and pinpoints which requirement failed. On 600 images with three-annotator labels, it agrees with humans as closely as vision-language judges up to 30x larger, while using only a fraction of their GPU memory. Across eleven T2I models and 211,200 images, nine score lower on a single negated statement than on a single positive one. Per-statement scoring reveals that the loss is largest for color and near zero for proximity, and that 41.5% of failed statements render exactly what the prompt forbids. Rendering what a prompt asks for and withholding what it forbids are distinct capabilities that an aggregate compositional score cannot distinguish. NegT2IBench measures the latter directly, providing a controlled testbed for diagnosing negation failures and developing methods to overcome them.

</details>


### [48] [Beyond Single Videos: Benchmarking and Active Evidence Seeking for E-Commerce Cross-Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03099)
*Jinghan Zhao,Yiman Hu,Liang Wu,Jian Xu,Bo Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了AdsCVR基准数据库和AdSeek框架，旨在解决电子商务视频跨视频推理的挑战。AdSeek通过动态选择视觉和音频工具，在多轮探索中进行主动证据采集，克服了强化学习中的稀疏奖励问题，从而提升了模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子商务视频的信息密集度高，消费者和商家都需要对其做出评判和策略制定。现有的多模态模型主要集中在单一视频的理解上，缺乏跨视频推理的能力。因此，该研究旨在填补这一空白，创建首个跨视频推理基准AdsCVR，以更好地理解电子商务视频中的信息。

Method: 研究者提出了集合理论和主动学习机制的AdSeek框架。它采用动态选择视觉和音频工具的方式，在多轮探索中进行主动证据采集，避免了传统模型的静态均匀抽样方法。此外，为了应对强化学习中的稀疏奖励问题，研究者还提出了一种离线轨迹修正机制，该机制能够识别出强化学习生成轨迹中的推理错误和缺失的多模态证据，从而提供监督式微调信号，减少强化学习过程中学习到的偏差，并支持了一个修正的强化学习管道。

Result: AdSeek在AdsCVR测试集上达到了74.30%的准确率，相较于其Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct基线模型提高了27.90个百分点。此外，AdSeek还展示了在开放领域CrossVid基准上的泛化能力，证明了其有效的主动证据收集机制。

Conclusion: 该研究为电子商务视频跨视频推理问题提供了一个新的思路和解决方案——AdSeek框架和所创建的跨视频推理基准AdsCVR。该研究拓宽了多模态学习在电子商务视频理解领域的应用范围，具有重要的实践和理论意义。

Abstract: E-commerce videos are information-dense and frequently compared by consumers evaluating products and merchants assessing marketing strategies. However, existing multimodal models mainly focus on single-video understanding and have limited ability to compare information across videos. We introduce AdsCVR, the first e-commerce cross-video reasoning benchmark, containing 2,483 videos and 6,110 question-answer pairs across six reasoning dimensions. Cross- video reasoning requires models to locate fine-grained evidence among many redundant frames and integrate visual details, speech, and on-screen text. We therefore propose AdSeek, an agentic framework that dynamically selects visual and audio tools during multi-turn exploration, replacing static uniform sampling with active evidence acquisition. To address the sparse credit assignment of reinforcement learning, we develop an offline trajectory rectification mechanism that identifies reasoning errors and missing multimodal evidence in RL-generated trajectories. The corrected trajectories provide supervised fine-tuning signals that reduce biases learned during RL. This mechanism supports a rectified bootstrapping pipeline in which initial RL exposes reasoning bottlenecks, supervised fine-tuning corrects them, and a final RL stage further improves the policy. AdSeek achieves 74.30 percent accuracy on the AdsCVR test split, outperforming its Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct backbone by 27.90 percentage points. It also generalizes to the open- domain CrossVid benchmark, demonstrating effective active evidence gathering.

</details>


### [49] [A Benchmark for Spatially Grounded Gesture Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03105)
*Anna Deichler,Rishabh Dabral,Fethiye Irmak Dogan,Anindita Ghosh,Jonas Beskow*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种空间定位手势生成基准，包括约2000个注释的手势片段，旨在区分时间对齐、空间定位和感知自然度。研究表明，几何定位可以优于人类手势，但没有增加感知自然度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏评估生成手势是否指向正确对象的通用框架，本文提出的基准旨在解决这一问题。

Method: 构建了一个包含来自自然对话的约2000个手势片段的基准，每个片段都有3D真实参考点。提出了一个基于MM-Conv-Flow的流动匹配基线，并与独立的检索系统和捕捉到的人类动作进行评估。

Result: 基线结果表明，几何定位可以在没有增加感知自然度的情况下超越人类手势。

Conclusion: 研究发现，手势的质量应该从几何定位和感知自然度等多个维度进行衡量，而不仅仅是考虑自然度。

Abstract: Communication in shared space interweaves verbal and non-verbal signals, and pointing gestures anchor language to the environment: "put the cup on that one" is uninterpretable without the gesture that fixes the referent. Yet no common framework exists for evaluating whether generated gestures indicate their intended referent; distributional metrics reward a gesture aimed at the wrong object as long as it looks natural. We introduce a benchmark for spatially grounded gesture generation, comprising ~2K pointing-annotated clips from naturalistic VR dialogue with ground-truth 3D referents, a task in which systems must decide when, how and where to point within conversational speech, and a protocol that separates temporal alignment, spatial grounding and perceived naturalness. We also provide a flow-matching baseline, MM-Conv-Flow. Evaluating it alongside an independent retrieval-based system and captured human motion, we find that geometric grounding can exceed that of human pointing without any gain in perceived naturalness, showing that referential gesture quality must be measured along separate dimensions.

</details>


### [50] [In-Distribution Forcing for Long Video Generation at Test Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03120)
*Jeongwoo Shin,Youngyoon Choi,Sangwoo Jo,Hyunmog Kim,Sungjoon Choi,Joonseok Lee,Jaewoong Choi,Jaemoo Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为ID-Forcing的新框架，通过自缓存机制防止缓存条目成为离分布的，从而解决了KV条目的分布问题。研究在标准视频生成基准上展示了其竞争力，并且在减轻漂移现象方面优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖于KV条件化来处理视频生成中的漂移问题，但这些方法假设缓存的KV条目保持在分布中。然而，在生成长视频时，这一假设常常失效，导致进一步的漂移现象。因此，需要一种新的框架来解决这一问题。

Method: 研究提出了ID-Forcing框架。该框架的关键机制是自缓存，即在缓存每个视频片段时，不参考之前的KV条目，这样可以保持滚动窗口内的内容在分布中。这种方法可以将短时间尺度的模型扩展到分钟级视频生成。

Result: 在标准视频生成基准上，该方法展示了其竞争力。实验表明，它在减轻漂移现象方面优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的ID-Forcing框架为解决长视频生成中的漂移问题提供了一种新的解决方案，并证明了其在保持视频生成质量的同时能够有效减轻漂移现象。

Abstract: Modern autoregressive (AR) video diffusion models excel at short-horizon video generation, yet generating long videos remains challenging due to drifting, where colors and textures shift, and motion dynamics decay. Existing works primarily rely on KV conditioning, which selects or modifies cached key-value (KV) entries to mitigate drifting. However, we observe that KV conditioning alone is insufficient as it assumes cached KV entries remain in-distribution. This assumption fails beyond the training horizon: nothing constrains the construction of KV entries during rollout, giving rise to the KV-provenance problem where cached entries themselves become out-of-distribution (OOD). To address this, we propose In-Distribution Forcing (ID-Forcing), a test-time framework that aligns both KV caching and KV conditioning with training configurations. Its key mechanism, self-caching, prevents OOD KV entries at their source. Each chunk is cached without attending to prior KV entry, keeping the rolling window exactly in-distribution. Consequently, ID-Forcing seamlessly extends short-horizon models to minute-scale video generation. Extensive evaluations show that our method remains competitive on standard video generation benchmark while substantially outperforming prior work in mitigating drifting, as validated by both our drift metrics and a user study.

</details>


### [51] [Foresight: planning future perception in streaming VLMs without retraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03123)
*Ashok Prasad Neupane,Dipan Bartaula,Ankit Belbase,Saugat Adhikari,Samip Ghimire,Saroj Poudel,Binod Bhattarai,Danda Pani Paudel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FORESIGHT 是一种双流架构，通过两个共享权重的 Siamese LLM 实现未来预测，动态配置未来的计算，且不中断实时推理，表现出低延迟和高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流式视觉语言模型在推理过程中无法动态调整其计算路径，以适应不断变化的场景动态。FORESIGHT 的引入是为了解决这一问题，通过实现前瞻计算来动态配置未来的计算，而无需训练，期望提高模型的实时性和灵活性。

Method: FORESIGHT 包含两个共享权重的 Siamese LLM，其中一个实时处理输入数据，另一个提前进行未来上下文的预测和规划，同时规划未来的计算步骤，并生成任务响应。计算计划通过一种高效地重新配置协议执行，使用模式指导解码和轻量级的差异更新。

Result: FORESIGHT 在 OmniPro Online 评估中获得了23.0均值联合 F1，比最强的训练基线高9.5%，在 StreamingBench 和 OVO-Bench 上分别提高了6.7% 和 15.4%，在视频流后半部分证据到达时的改进最大，高达18.7%。

Conclusion: FORESIGHT 的引入显著提高了流式视觉语言模型在预测和规划未来上下文上的前瞻能力，从而优化了计算配置和任务响应生成过程，实现了高效的动态适应。

Abstract: Existing streaming vision-language models (VLMs) continuously perceive and reason over visual streams, but their computational pathways remain fixed throughout inference. Consequently, they cannot adapt computation to evolving scene dynamics, where different future events demand different levels and forms of perception. We show that streaming VLMs inherently possess the ability to anticipate the immediate future, and leverage this capability to dynamically configure future computation in a training-free manner. Realizing such anticipatory computation, however, is very challenging: future anticipation must be sufficiently reliable to guide computation, planning must run concurrently with streaming inference, and online reconfiguration must incur negligible overhead. To address these challenges, we introduce FORESIGHT, a dual-stream architecture comprising two Siamese LLMs with shared weights, input encoders, and KV cache. The first LLM continuously processes incoming tokens, while the second runs ahead of the stream to anticipate future context, plan future computation, and generate task responses without interrupting streaming inference. Each plan decides when to reason next, what to check then, and how densely to sample, keeping transient evidence separate from persistent control. The resulting computation plan is executed online through an efficient reconfiguration protocol with schemaguided decoding and lightweight diff-based updates, enabling dynamic adaptation with low overhead. With a frozen Qwen3-VL-8B backbone, FORESIGHT achieves 23.0 mean joint F1 on OmniPro Online evaluation beating strongest trained baseline by 9.5%, while improving the backbone by 6.7 on StreamingBench and 15.4 on OVO-Bench, with the largest gain of 18.7 when evidence arrives later in the video stream. Our source code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [52] [Behavior Pack Optimization for Video MLLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03141)
*Zhaolu Kang,Shiyu Liu,Tailong Luo,Wei Zhang,Yingjie He,Lei Wei,Guansu Wang,Liang He,Siheng Wang,Guangyuan Dong,Jiaqi Su,Shuang Chen,Haoyu Ji,Qishi Zhan,Kaiyue Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为Behavior Pack Optimization (BPO)的方法，通过在多种虚拟视角下评估模型的表现来提高视频多模态语言模型在时间相关问题上的准确性和对关键证据的敏感度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视频多模态大语言模型在回答时间相关问题时表现不佳，其准确率主要依赖于外观和语言先验，而非视频中的时间证据。因此，通过优化模型的行为表现，使其在不同视角下能更稳定地工作，提高其对时间证据的敏感性和判断力成为研究动机。

Method: BPO通过使用一个行为包（一组跨假设视角的输出）来计算模型的奖励，每个包的奖励被设计成在无关干涉时保持稳定、关键证据缺失时富有敏感性和在无凭据时保持克制。

Result: BPO在TempCompass、MVBench和NExT-QA数据集上提高了Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct的宏观准确率，特别是在时间难题子集和无凭据测试任务上，分别提高了4.7和20.0百分点。

Conclusion: BPO方法不仅提高了视频多模态语言模型在时间相关任务上的性能，同时其原理还有望对其他类似模型和任务提供指导和帮助。

Abstract: Video multimodal large language models (MLLMs) keep climbing video question answering benchmarks, yet shuffling the frames, masking the segment that supports the answer, or occluding the target object barely changes their predictions. The accuracy rests on appearance and language priors, not on the temporal evidence the question asks for. We trace this to the unit of post-training: rewards are computed on a single response to the original clip, so the model is never asked to behave consistently across views. We propose Behavior Pack Optimization (BPO), which replaces the single response with a behavior pack of outputs across counterfactual views chosen by question type, scored jointly. The pack reward asks for stability when the intervention is irrelevant, sensitivity when key evidence is removed, and abstention when no evidence remains. To keep this objective stable at small pack sizes, BPO uses an anchor-relative advantage: the response on the original view serves as a per-prompt reference instead of a group mean over mixed views. On TempCompass, MVBench, and NExT-QA, BPO improves the macro accuracy of Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct by 4.7 pp, the temporal-hard subset by 7.8 pp, and abstention F1 by 20.0 pp over a budget-matched vanilla GRPO baseline from the same SFT checkpoint. The gains transfer to Video-MME, LongVideoBench, and to LLaVA-Video-7B; ablations confirm they follow the view sets, not the rollout count. We hope this pack-level perspective offers a useful starting point for the video MLLM and multimodal post-training community as the field moves toward evidence-grounded video reasoning.

</details>


### [53] [CalCErt: Bin-wise Certification of Confidence Calibration in Medical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03142)
*Leo Fillioux,Stergios Christodoulidis,Stergios Christodoulidis,Maria Vakalopoulou,Jose Dolz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为 CalCErt 的后处理策略，能够为任何预训练可微分类器提供-bin级置信度校准的认证。该方法结合了经验校准估计、统计集中性边界和置信函数的局部利普希茨估计，以确定在 l2 范数为 R 的球体内的最坏情况失校准的数据依赖性上界。在多种医学图像分类任务和对抗扰动下进行评估，证明了与基线策略相比具有更高的认证覆盖率，同时保持了竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的认证方法侧重于保持预测类别，但忽略了认证在对抗攻击下的校准行为，本文提出 CalCErt 旨在填补这一研究空白。

Method: 方法结合了经验校准估计、统计集中性边界和置信函数的局部利普希茨估计，以确定在最坏情况下的失校准的上界。

Result: 该方法在多种医学图像分类任务上进行评估，与基线策略相比，具有更高的认证覆盖率，同时保持了竞争力。

Conclusion: CalCErt 是一种简单且高效的后处理策略，能够在对抗攻击下提供置信度校准认证，并已在多种任务中得到验证。

Abstract: Deep neural networks remain vulnerable to adversarial perturbations, which can distort not only predictions but also confidence scores, undermining uncertainty calibration. While existing certification methods focus on preserving the predicted category, providing guarantees on how calibration behaves under adversarial attacks remains overlooked. In this work, we introduce CalCErt, a simple and efficient post-hoc strategy that certifies bin-wise confidence calibration for any pretrained differentiable classifier. Our approach combines empirical calibration estimates, statistical concentration bounds, and local Lipschitz estimates of the confidence function to derive data-dependent upper bounds on worst-case miscalibration within an $ell_2$-ball of radius R. We evaluate CalCErt across 11 medical image classification tasks and multiple adversarial perturbations, demonstrating substantially higher certified coverage than baseline strategies while maintaining competitive tightness. Our code is available at https://github.com/leofillioux/calcert.

</details>


### [54] [Does Physics Live in the Activations? Localizing Physical Quantities in Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03154)
*Jonas Kneifl,Jakub Skalski,Bartłomiej Twardowski,Kamil Deja*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究发现，尽管视频生成模型能够生成逼真的序列，但在物理推理方面存在明显缺陷。通过探查视频Diffusion Transformers模型内部表示，研究者发现模型在去噪过程中能够准确地解码关键的物理量，这些信息主要来自于模型动态去噪过程而非初始输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨视频生成模型是否准确地内化了物理原则，而不是仅仅再现熟悉的运动模式。

Method: 研究者通过探查视频Diffusion Transformers（DiTs）模型内部表示来进行研究，具体来说，是解码模拟器提供的地面真实物理量，检查这些信息在去除噪声过程中的可读性。

Result: 研究结果显示，关键物理量在去噪过程早期被准确解码，其准确性显著优于从模型自身噪声化显潜特征直接解码的结果，表明相关信息是在去噪过程中主动构建的。此外，研究还发现，在物体位置的激活值中蕴含了相关物理信息，并且可以在单个潜显帧上读取多帧定义的物理量。当直接拟合在全分辨率激活空间中时，探针方向还可以作为引导矢量来改变模型的输出。

Conclusion: 研究结论是，视频生成模型在去噪过程中构建并存储了关键的物理信息，而不是在输入中直接存在这些信息。

Abstract: Video generation models produce strikingly realistic sequences and are increasingly proposed as world models, yet recent benchmarks reveal pronounced deficits in their physical reasoning. This raises the question of whether these models internalize physical principles or merely reproduce familiar motion patterns. We address this by probing internal representations of video Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) for simulator-derived ground-truth physical quantities spanning kinematic motion and rigid-body dynamics under gravity and contact. We find that these quantities are linearly decodable with high accuracy early in the denoising process, substantially outperforming a baseline decoded directly from the model's own noised latents, indicating that the relevant physical information is actively constructed during denoising rather than already present in the input. Additionally, we show that activations at on-object tokens carry the relevant physical information and that quantities defined over multiple frames are readable from single latent frames. Hence, information is sharply localized within the token sequence and is computed globally but stored locally. The probes further show partial extrapolation, transferring to scene variations and object configurations outside their training regime, so what they read is not simply a correlate of the scenes they were fit on. When fitted directly in the full-resolution activation space, the probing directions can serve as steering vectors to change the model's output.

</details>


### [55] [Geometry-Aligned Semantic Matching for Cross-Modal Planar Image Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03167)
*Zhiwei Wang,Defeng He,Yuxing Li,Meilu Zhu,Edmund Y. Lam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了CDPM方法，通过几何一致的语义表示和多尺度DONI特征金字塔，精准跨模态配准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过语义表示提供跨模态一致性，但不能保证几何对应关系。同时，细粒度CNN特征虽能提供细节，但缺乏全景语义指导，CDPM旨在解决这些问题。

Method: CDPM首先通过几何一致的跨模态补丁对来适应DINOv3模型，促使特征相似性更准确地反映真实的跨模态空间对应关系。接着构建基于DINO的多尺度特征金字塔，维护稳定的跨模态对应关系，同时轻量级CNN支路提供辅助结构细节，实精准局部优化。

Result: 在三个跨模态数据集上进行了广泛的实验，CDPM展示了更优越的表现。相比密集匹配器RoMa，CDPM在VIS-IR上的AUC提高了7.36至10.42个百分点，且在所有评估指标上超越RoMa，同时FLOPs减少45.6%。

Conclusion: CDPM提升了跨模态图像配准的准确性且具有高效性，为相关研究提供了新的方法和技术。

Abstract: Cross-modal image matching establishes stable and accurate geometric correspondences across modalities for planar registration. Existing semantic representations provide cross-modal consistency, but semantic similarity does not necessarily imply geometric correspondence. Meanwhile, fine-grained CNN features provide accurate local details but lack global cross-modal semantic guidance for stable refinement. To address these issues, we propose CDPM, which first establishes geometrically consistent semantic representations and then preserves their dominant role in correspondence estimation during fine-grained localization. Specifically, we progressively adapt DINOv3 using geometrically consistent cross-modal patch pairs, enabling feature similarity to better reflect true cross-modal spatial correspondences. We then construct a DINO-Centric Feature Pyramid, where multi-scale DINO representations maintain stable cross-modal correspondences, while a lightweight CNN branch provides auxiliary structural details for precise local refinement. Extensive experiments on three cross-modal datasets demonstrate the superior performance of CDPM. On VIS-IR, compared with the dense matcher RoMa, CDPM improves AUC@3/5/10/20 by 7.36, 13.40, 13.75, and 10.42 percentage points, respectively, and reduces mACE from 5.83 to 2.78 pixels. It also outperforms RoMa v2 across all metrics while requiring 45.6% fewer FLOPs. The online demo and dataset are available, and the code will be released on our project page at https://warren-wzw.github.io/CDPM/.

</details>


### [56] [PocketSplat: Mobile Gaussian Reconstruction via World-Space Latent Allocatio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03192)
*Wenzhi Guo,Xianda Chen,Dongxuan Chen,Guangchi Fang,Bing Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PocketSplat 是一种预算导向的移动 Gaussian 资产构建框架，能够在遵守设备资源限制的同时，生成适合移动设备使用的 Gaussian 资产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常生成密集且与图像对齐的重建候选，这可能会超标移动设备资源。PocketSplat 旨在提供一种更加灵活的方法，能够在给定预算的情况下高效地生成高质量的 Gaussian 资产。

Method: PocketSplat 使用几何感知的密集潜在候选并预测其在世界空间中的位置。它通过在局部潜在单元中分配确切的整数容量来降低资源消耗，仅对保留的候选进行完整的 Gaussian 属性解码。此外，它通过条件空间融合聚合多视角证据，并通过空间责任解码根据局部稀疏化调整高斯支持。

Result: 在 DL3DV 和其他基准测试中，PocketSplat 在预算-质量权衡上优于现有的前馈 Gaussian 重建基线。与部署流媒体 MVSplat 变体相比，PocketSplat 可以在目标 iPhone 上更快地构造更紧凑、更高的质量 Gaussian 资产。然而，仅在原生 MVSplat 和 DepthSplat 上运行时，它们已超过设备内存预算。

Conclusion: PocketSplat 提供了一种更加灵活和经济高效的 Gaussian 资产生成方法，适用于移动设备上的应用。

Abstract: Mobile Gaussian reconstruction must satisfy two requirements: the reconstruction model must execute within a device resource envelope, and the resulting Gaussian asset must expose a representation size suited to downstream mobile use. Existing feed-forward Gaussian reconstructors commonly decode dense, image-aligned candidates whose final cardinality is implicitly determined by the input resolution and number of views. We present PocketSplat, a feed-forward framework for budgeted mobile Gaussian asset construction. Given a prescribed output budget, PocketSplat organizes dense geometry-aware latent candidates in predicted world space, allocates exact integer capacity across local latent cells, and decodes complete Gaussian attributes only for retained candidates. Cell-conditioned latent fusion aggregates repeated multi-view evidence before decoding, while spatial responsibility decoding adapts Gaussian support after local sparsification. Experiments on DL3DV and out-of-distribution benchmarks establish a strong quality--budget trade-off against feed-forward Gaussian reconstruction baselines. On Mip-NeRF 360, PocketSplat executes directly on a target iPhone and constructs compact, higher-quality Gaussian assets substantially faster than a deployable streamed MVSplat variant; native MVSplat and DepthSplat exceed the device memory budget.

</details>


### [57] [Bridging Research and Practice: A Systematic Evaluation of Generalist and Dermatology-Specific Models in Clinical Skin Lesion Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03193)
*Emanoel dos Santos,Kelvin Cunha,Rodrigo Mota,Fabio Papais,Thales Bezerra,Natalia Lopes,Erico Medeiros,Shirley Cruz,Jessica Araujo,Paulo Borba,Tsang Ing Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究针对临床皮肤科挑战，评估了多种架构在皮肤病学数据集上的表现，特别是在数据分布变化、模态转换和人口统计学差异下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床皮肤科诊断具有高不确定性，且皮肤病变的异质性加剧了这一挑战。为推动医学人工智能在皮肤病学中的实际应用，本研究旨在系统性地评估现代模型在异质数据集上的泛化能力。

Method: 本研究评估了一系列架构，包括通用和医学视觉语言模型，以及基础模型，对比了它们与特定皮肤科分类器（如基于嵌入的方法和卷积神经网络）的表现。

Result: 研究量化了当前最先进的模型与临床部署需求之间的差距，评估了模型在分布变化、模态变化和人口统计学差异下的性能。

Conclusion: 本研究为提升皮肤病学AI系统的可靠性和临床适用性提供了参考，旨在推动更加安全和公平的应用。

Abstract: The application of machine learning to dermatology has grown substantially in recent years, moving beyond proof-of-concept studies toward potential applications. However, clinical dermatology remains a challenging and still open problem. Diagnostic assessment is often ambiguous, and skin lesions exhibit high variability, compounded by differences in acquisition modality, device quality, and patient demographics. These factors hinder the development of robust models suitable for safe and equitable clinical use. To support translation into practice, it is essential to systematically evaluate how contemporary models generalize across heterogeneous data sources. In this work, we benchmark a diverse set of architectures on recent dermatology datasets, spanning dermoscopic images and smartphone-based clinical photographs. We assess the robustness of recent general-purpose and medical vision-language models, as well as foundation models, and compare them against task-specific dermatology classifiers, including embedding-based approaches and convolutional neural networks. Our study provides an evaluation of model performance under distribution shifts, modality changes, and demographic variability. By quantifying the gap between current state-of-the-art models and the requirements of clinical deployment, we aim to contribute to the development of reliable, accessible, and clinically applicable AI systems for dermatology.

</details>


### [58] [Contextual Flow Matching: Adaptive Step Selection in Flow Models for Efficient Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03202)
*Divya Jyoti Bajpai,Arun Verma,Manjesh Kumar Hanawal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: COCFLOW 提出了一种在线训练的方法，在不影响生成质量的前提下，通过上下文感知的自适应步长选择实现流匹配加速，适用于图像和视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在加速过程中引入了额外的训练开销或降低了生成质量。COCFLOW 旨在保持高质量生成的同时，提高流匹配的推理效率。

Method: COCFLOW 提出了一种在推理时适应性选择生成步骤的方法，通过上下文感知训练在线获得最优步长，并使用无监督奖励在推理效率与生成保真度之间进行折衷。

Result: COCFLOW 实现了超过 2.5 倍的加速，适用于图像和视频生成，同时保持了感知质量和语义质量。

Conclusion: COCFLOW 为流匹配推理提供了一种高效且高质量的方法，无需再训练基础生成模型，并通过理论分析证明了其时间精度。

Abstract: Flow Matching enables high-quality visual generation via continuous-time dynamics, but inference remains costly due to multiple sequential function evaluations. Existing acceleration methods reduce the number of function evaluations but often introduce additional training overhead, degrade quality, or fail to account for input-dependent variability. We propose COFLOW, an inference-time method that adaptively selects the step counts each generation based on the prompt features. Our context-aware COFLOW is trained online with an unsupervised reward that balances inference efficiency and generation fidelity. Our method is plug-and-play, requiring no retraining of the underlying generative model. It generalizes to image and video generation, achieving over 2.5x speedup while preserving perceptual and semantic quality. We further provide a theoretical analysis establishing an O(1/K) forward-Euler discretization error bound under standard regularity conditions.

</details>


### [59] [VisionMX: Unlocking Microscaling Post-Training Quantization for Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03218)
*Elad Dror Cohen,Ofir Gordon,Lior Dikstein,Idan Achituve,Hai Victor Habi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了视觉模型在训练后转换为微缩量化（MX）格式的效果，并提出了一种新的VisionMX方法来优化权重舍入并修正激活函数，该方法在多种视觉任务中表现出优于直接转换和基准的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前，微缩量化（MX）格式作为一种硬件支持的方法，被用于高效的训练和推理，但在视觉模型中的应用尚不够广泛。本文旨在系统地研究训练后MX量化的效果，并提出一种改进的方法。

Method: 研究首先分析了直接转换中的三个误差源，分别为块尺度表示、小卷积权重张量与非均匀元素网格之间较差的对齐以及非负激活函数的欠使用有符号码。然后提出VisionMX方法，优化有界权重舍入并对激活函数应用可折叠的仿射修正。

Result: VisionMX方法被评估了在图像分类、对象检测、语义分割和低光图像增强等多种视觉任务上的性能，结果显示其优于直接转换和评估的训练后量化基准。

Conclusion: 本文提出的VisionMX方法为视觉模型的微缩量化提供了一种优化方案，能够显著提升模型在MX格式下的性能。

Abstract: Microscaling (MX) formats are emerging as a hardware-supported approach to efficient training and inference. They combine low-precision elements with shared block scales, but their impact on vision models remains underexplored. We systematically investigate post-training MX quantization across vision models and tasks. An analysis of direct conversion identifies three sources of error: block-scale representation, the poor alignment of some small convolutional weight tensors with nonuniform element grids, and the underuse of signed codes by nonnegative activations. These findings motivate VisionMX, a post-training MX quantization method that optimizes bounded weight rounding and applies a foldable affine correction to activations. We evaluate VisionMX across image classification, object detection, semantic segmentation, and low-light image enhancement using several MX-style formats. It improves on direct conversion and the evaluated post-training quantization baselines, with the largest performance recoveries in architectures most sensitive to MX conversion

</details>


### [60] [VDOT++: Unified Few-Step Video Generation via Unbalanced Optimal Transport Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03221)
*Yutong Wang,Xingtong Ge,Enhuai Liu,Yunke Wang,Tianfan Xue,Yu Qiao,Yaohui Wang,Xinyuan Chen,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VDOT++通过引入不平衡对偶OTD和$oldsymbol{oldsymbol{	ext{ℓ}}}_{oldsymbol{1}}$地面成本，改进了生成器的不同任务族的训练，提高了稳定性与效率，并结合分布匹配和对抗优化提升了整体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的扩散模型在生成过程中耗时高昂，原因是需要多次评估大型网络骨干。VDOT++通过优化OTD（Optimal Transport Distillation）的方法，解决由于输出多样化导致的模型可靠性下降问题。

Method: VDOT++引入了不平衡对偶OTD，允许不可靠的学生态子不携带过多质量，同时通过$oldsymbol{oldsymbol{	ext{ℓ}}}_{oldsymbol{1}}$地面成本限定传播目标的影响范围，增强了对齐的稳定性。此外，VDOT++还结合了分布匹配与对抗优化，通过顺序反向传播进行多重尺度的优化。

Result: 实验结果表明，VDOT++在三个类型的任务（T2V、I2V和条件生成）上，生成出的四步骤模型能与多次步骤的基准模型和强少量步骤基准模型媲美。

Conclusion: VDOT++提供了一种新的统一蒸馏框架，适用于文本到视频、图像到视频和条件引导的生成任务，提高了解决这些问题的模型效率和性能。

Abstract: Video creation spans text-to-video (T2V), image-to-video (I2V), and condition-based generation, yet video diffusion models remain costly because they repeatedly evaluate large backbones during sampling. Distribution matching distillation (DMD) reduces this cost, but its reverse Kullback--Leibler (KL) objective can provide unstable or incomplete guidance when the student and teacher distributions have limited overlap. VDOT addressed this issue by adding optimal transport distillation (OTD), whose explicit coupling supplies geometric directions for condition-based generation. Balanced OTD, however, performs full-mass matching between the spatial tokens of each corresponding student--teacher frame pair. This assumption weakens for T2V and I2V, where one condition admits many valid outputs and spatial content need not align across different realizations. We present VDOT++, a unified distillation framework that applies the same training recipe separately to generators for the three task families. It makes OTD robust to output diversity through an asymmetric unbalanced formulation that allows unreliable student tokens to carry less mass while maintaining coverage of the teacher tokens. An $\ell_1$ ground cost further replaces mean-based aggregation with a more mode-preserving weighted median that limits the influence of distant transport targets. The two changes respectively determine whom to match and how the selected targets should be aggregated. We additionally combine distribution matching and adversarial refinement through sequential backward passes, and exploit the decoupled score networks for cross-scale distillation, where larger score networks improve a compact generator. Experiments on UVCBench, VBench, VBench-I2V, and the VACE benchmark show that the resulting four-step generators are competitive with many-step teachers and strong few-step baselines across all three task families.

</details>


### [61] [Uncertainty as a Proxy for Semantic Correctness in Diffusion-Based Medical Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03224)
*Yuxuan Ou,Konstantinos Kamnitsas,OxAAA Study,AICT Consortium,Regent Lee,Vicente Grau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了不确定性作为扩散模型在非对比CT至对比增强CT转换中进行解剖正确性评估的代理指标的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可视化真实度评估方法不能直接衡量解剖正确性，因此研究认为可以利用不确定性作为解剖正确性的替代指标。

Method: 研究采用了AortaDiff框架，结合图像生成和管腔分割任务，通过六种不同类型的不确定性（权重、架构扰动、生成固有不确定性、输入扰动）进行全面测试，评估其在像素、区域和图像级别的应用效果。

Result: 不确定性指标在其所测试的所有尺度上均为临床相关异常情况提供了有用的信息，并在外部多中心数据集下的分布转移中仍然具有有效性。特别是MCDropout方法，在所有尺度上表现优异，具有良好的泛化性能，且已在训练过程中使用Dropout模型的评估方法无需额外的训练成本。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，不确定性可以作为一种实用且计算成本较低的指标，用于质量筛选、可靠性和临床外数据检测的NCCT到CECT合成过程中。

Abstract: Diffusion models can synthesise contrast-enhanced CT (CECT) from non-contrast CT (NCCT), avoiding contrast administration and its environmental and patient-access costs. However, visually realistic images are not necessarily anatomically correct, and the pixel-intensity and feature-space similarity metrics used to assess generation quality do not directly measure anatomical correctness. In this work, we investigate whether uncertainty can serve as a proxy for semantic correctness in diffusion-based medical image synthesis.
  We study NCCT-to-CECT synthesis using AortaDiff, a multitask diffusion framework that jointly generates CECT images and lumen segmentations. The segmentation output provides an explicit representation of the generated vascular anatomy, enabling segmentation-derived errors to be used as a quantitative measure of generation correctness. Six methods spanning weight (Ensemble, HyperDiff, BayesDiff), architecture-perturbation (MCDropout), generative-stochasticity (RDS) and input-perturbation (TTA) uncertainty are compared at the pixel, region and image levels, and for detection of clinically relevant out-of-distribution (OOD) cases.
  Uncertainty proves informative at all three spatial scales, remains informative on an external multi-centre dataset under distribution shift, and supports OOD detection. MCDropout stands out among the six: it ranks among the leading methods at every scale, generalizes well on the external dataset, and can be enabled at inference on any model already trained with dropout, so reliable uncertainty comes at no extra training cost. Uncertainty reliably flags severe failures but discriminates poorly among already high-quality images. These findings support uncertainty as a practical and computationally economical signal for quality filtering, reliability assessment and OOD detection in NCCT-to CECT synthesis.

</details>


### [62] [EmbPASS: Towards Cross-Embodiment Open Panoramic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03248)
*Pujun Guo,Yuanfan Zheng,Fei Teng,Mengfei Duan,Guoqiang Zhao,Yuheng Zhang,Kai Luo,Kailun Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文引入了一项新的任务——跨体感开放全景分割，并建立了EmbPASS基准，包含多种平台的全景语义分割测试数据集，提出了EPONet网络以解决异构体感观察下的全景分割问题，实验表明其性能优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前全景图像在复杂环境中提供了全面的视角，但不同类型的体感平台观察视角和空间布局存在差异，这带来了跨体感观察的挑战，缺乏系统性研究。因此，本文旨在解决该问题，通过引入新的任务和方法来促进跨体感的全景感知研究。

Method: 本文采用了建立新的跨体感全景分割任务的方法，并开发了EmbPASS基准。提出了EPONet网络，结合了关系感知度量适配器和内容自适应语义转移模块，提升了跨体感观察条件下的空间建模和语义转移能力。

Result: 实验表明，EPONet在EmbPASS基准上的平台均衡性能为35.82% mIoU，并在现有的全景分割基准上有竞争力。

Conclusion: 本文通过建立 EmbPASS基准和提出EPONet网络，为跨体感全景分割提供了新的解决方案，有助于推进该领域的研究。

Abstract: Panoramic images provide a complete 360-degree field of view, enabling comprehensive scene understanding for embodied perception. However, heterogeneous embodied platforms exhibit substantial differences in observation viewpoints and spatial layouts, giving rise to cross-embodiment observation shifts that pose additional challenges to consistent and reliable panoramic perception, while systematic studies of this problem remain limited. To bridge this gap, we introduce a new task, termed Cross-Embodiment Open Panoramic Segmentation. Meanwhile, we establish EmbPASS, a multi-platform panoramic semantic segmentation benchmark spanning Vehicle, Drone, Wearable, and Quadruped platforms under a unified semantic taxonomy, providing a testbed for systematically studying cross-embodiment panoramic perception. We further propose EPONet, an open-vocabulary panoramic semantic segmentation network that integrates Relation-Aware Metric Adapter (RAMA) and Content-Adaptive Semantic Transfer (CAST) to enhance spatial modeling and semantic transfer under heterogeneous embodied observations. Extensive experiments show that EPONet achieves the best platform-balanced performance on EmbPASS with 35.82% mIoU, outperforming the strongest baseline by 1.10%, while remaining competitive on existing panoramic segmentation benchmarks. The source code and EmbPASS benchmark will be made publicly available at https://github.com/guopj1/EmbPASS.

</details>


### [63] [COSMI: COmpositional Synthesis of Multi-object Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03252)
*Daniel Eskandar,Ilya A. Petrov,Gerard Pons-Moll*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究通过组合单个物体捕捉的片段创建了一个包含多达五个物体的数据集COSMI，克服了记录多物体交互的高成本问题，提出了一种基于文本的多物体交互生成方法COSMI，并且在未见过的物体和交互组合上表现出了更好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的人物体交互数据集录制仅涉及单个物体，但日常活动通常涉及多个物体。研究者通过观察人物体交互的局部性，提出利用单个物体捕捉片段来构建多物体交互的组合数据集，以克服多物体录制的高成本问题。

Method: 研究者首先捕捉了不同人的单个物体交互片段，通过保持接触一致性并镜像处理单物体片段以平衡手部，然后利用语言模型和几何检查筛选出合理的组合。基于此数据集，他们构建了一个从文本生成交互的方法，通过共享物体槽位权重来生成可变数量的物体，并根据身体部位的移动来预测物体间的相对位置。

Result: COSMI数据集包含222k个序列和275小时的数据，拥有最多五个物体的组合，相比现有最大的多物体捕捉数据集大了近三十倍。最终，研究者基于COSMI数据集训练的一个文本驱动的多物体交互生成模型COSMI方法，在基准测试中展现了对未见过物体和交互组合的更好泛化能力，特别是在物体接触精度方面表现出显著的优势。

Conclusion: 该研究不仅提供了一个大容量、多物体组合的数据集COSMI，还提出了一种新的多物体交互生成方法COSMI，表明利用局部性信息和组合策略可以有效地创建丰富的人物体交互数据集，并能显著提高模型对于未见过的物体和交互组合的泛化能力。

Abstract: Generative models of human-object interaction are bounded by the data that exists: everyday activities involve several objects, but most captured datasets record one at a time, as multi-object capture is combinatorially expensive. Our observation is that interactions are local, so single-object captures already contain the parts of multi-object activities. We compose them: contact-consistent clips of single interactions, mirrored to balance the hands, transfer between bodies, and a language model and geometric checks admit only the pairings that are plausible, semantically and physically. Therefore, the dataset grows combinatorially with the clips rather than recording time. The COSMI dataset holds 222k sequences and 275 hours with up to five objects, nearly thirty times the largest multi-object capture, and can be extended by adding datasets or even hand-object recordings. On this data we train the COSMI method, a text-to-interaction diffusion transformer that follows how the data is built: weight-shared object slots generate a variable number of objects, predicted relative to the body parts that move them. On a benchmark with an unseen object and unseen interaction combinations, models trained on the dataset generalize to the unseen combinations. COSMI outperforms baselines in text alignment and contact accuracy, where its margin is largest on the unseen object. Code, models, and the dataset pipeline will be released on the project page: https://ptrvilya.github.io/cosmi.

</details>


### [64] [Consecutive Posterior Fusion for Diffusive Recovery of Unobservable Image Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03261)
*Elena Morotti,Davide Evangelista,Elena Loli Piccolomini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CPF-DDNM 提出了一种在不需重新训练或额外噪点清除评估的情况下，在推断时融合连续的量测感知估计，从而改善不可观测图像结构的扩散恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统扩散后验采样器依赖瞬时的量测感知估计，但未明确利用之前后验修正携带的信息。因此，作者旨在提出一种在推断时融合连续的量测感知估计的方法，以改善不可观察图像结构的恢复。

Method: CPF-DDNM 方法基于 DDNM 的范围/零空间分解，融合了连续的量测感知估计，同时保留量测决定的成分，仅作用于先验驱动的零空间估计。

Result: 实验表明，CPF-DDNM 在稀疏视图和模拟低剂量计算机断层扫描，以及医学图像超分辨率方面，均显示出对 DDNM 一致的改进，并且在与基于扩散的逆求解器的竞争中表现出竞争力。

Conclusion: CPF-DDNM 方法为解决严重病态成像逆问题提供了一种有竞争力的解决方案。

Abstract: Solving severely ill-posed imaging inverse problems requires recovering image structures that are unobservable or weakly constrained by the measurements. Diffusion models provide expressive learned priors for inferring such missing information, while posterior sampling incorporates measurement consistency along the reverse process. Standard diffusion posterior samplers, however, rely on instantaneous measurement-aware estimates, without explicitly exploiting information carried by previous posterior corrections.
  We introduce Consecutive Posterior Fusion Denoising Diffusion Null-Space Models (CPF-DDNM), an inference-time strategy that fuses consecutive measurement-aware estimates to improve the diffusive recovery of unobservable image structures, without requiring retraining or additional denoiser evaluations. We instantiate this principle within DDNM, whose range/null-space decomposition reveals that consecutive fusion preserves the measurement-determined component while acting exclusively on the prior-driven null-space estimate. We thus provide a geometric interpretation of CPF-DDNM and a local error analysis that characterizes the optimal time-dependent fusion coefficient, including the extrapolative regime.
  Experiments on sparse-view and simulated low-dose computed tomography, as well as medical image super-resolution, show consistent improvements over DDNM and competitive performance against diffusion-based inverse solvers.

</details>


### [65] [Moving Forward with Video Saliency: A New Dataset and Benchmark where Motion Matters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03276)
*Susmit Agrawal,Rebecca Wanner,Juliane Verwiebe,Matthias Tangemann,Matthias Bethge,Matthias Kümmerer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究发现，尽管更新了基准测试和模型，基于粗略检查，静态基线仍不能显著优于时间模型。然而，在新基准SalTempto上，时间模型表现出显著提升，表明捕捉到的时序信息多于原始LEDOV数据集。尽管如此，SalTempto仍展示了大量未被模型解释的时序信息，需要进一步改进视频注视预测模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在验证先前关于静态模型在视频场景中预测注视行为与流媒体视频中时序信息分布有关的假设，或者时间模型是否能更好地捕捉到这种时序信息。

Method: 研究对比了静态模型和时间模型在LEDOV和SalTempto基准测试上的表现。

Result: 在LEDOV上，静态模型恢复了大量的注视行为信息。但在提出了全新视频偏见数据集ShapedBias后，在SalTempto基准测试上，静态模型的表现大打折扣，几乎只恢复中心偏差下的13%的注视行为信息。相比之下，最先进的时间模型即使在经过微调后，仍能显著超越静态模型，表明它能捕捉到静态模型无法理解的时序信息。然而，即使如此时间模型仍然未能解释半数以上的注视行为，显示出改进空间。

Conclusion: 研究得出结论，在具有更高动态性的SalTempto数据集上，时间模型表现出明显的性能提升，但在当前的基准测试中仍然存在改进空间。

Abstract: Video saliency prediction is inherently harder to model than static image saliency due to the additional temporal dimension. Video saliency benchmarks rest on the premise that predicting gaze on video requires utilizing temporal activity distributed across frames. Prior work has challenged this, showing that static baselines recover a significant fraction of the explainable gaze information on LEDOV, a popular video saliency dataset, and that video saliency models fail in the same places as this static baseline. We verify that this diagnosis still stands: under a more capable gold standard than the original analysis, and an updated panel of recent architectures, the strongest temporal architecture in the panel still does not substantially improve over a fine-tuned static baseline. However, it remains unclear whether the marginal gain reflects limitations of current temporal architectures or a lack of temporal patterns in the benchmark itself. We introduce SalTempto, a video saliency benchmark with greater dynamism: 224 clips of highly dynamic content, sourced from the HACS-Segments dataset so that each clip contains an event together with its lead-up and aftermath, with gaze recordings from up to 16 subjects and a training split for adapting pretrained models. On SalTempto, the static baseline recovers only about 13\% of the headroom above the centerbias, against more than half on LEDOV. A fine-tuned temporal architecture shows a substantial gain in performance over the static baseline, indicating that it does capture meaningfully more temporal information, which LEDOV fails to measure. Yet, even this SoTA model still leaves nearly half of SalTempto's headroom unexplained, indicating room for improvement in video saliency modelling. Examination of SalTempto also lets us describe human tendencies that models miss. SalTempto link: https://huggingface.co/datasets/bethgelab/video_saliency.

</details>


### [66] [T3lescope: Arbitrary-Resolution High-Fidelity Generative Surface Reconstruction from Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03308)
*Atsuhiro Noguchi,Tianhan Xu,Yiming Liang,Yuta Kikuchi,Masahiro Ishiyama,Shintaro Takagi,Hitoshi Murai,Eiichi Matsumoto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: T3lescope通过单一固定分辨率生成器在推理时进行粗细层次展开，无需场景优化，适用于各类场景和视图数量，提升了对细节结构和高光透明表面的重建效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于学习的方法在稀疏观测或高光面/透明面时表现不佳，而现有生成方法在高频细节上有限制。T3lescope旨在解决这些问题，提供一种适用于从单物体到大规模室外场景重建的解决方案。

Method: T3lescope在推断时采用粗细层次展开的单一固定分辨率生成器。初始粗层次建立场景布局，后续精细层次在逐步精细的空间单元内精确几何并去噪以恢复表面细节。模型跨规模和单元位置共享权重。

Result: T3lescope在室内、室外和城市规模的场景中均优于前馈和生成基础模型，匹配或超越场景特定优化性能，同时重建精细结构及高光透明表面。

Conclusion: T3lescope通过采用单一固定分辨率生成器及粗细层次展开方法，解决了现有方法在处理稀疏观测、高光面和透明面时的不足。该方法展示出了在不同场景、视图数量和图像分辨率下的泛化能力。

Abstract: We reconstruct high-fidelity 3D scene meshes from posed multi-view images without per-scene optimization, across scales ranging from single objects to large outdoor scenes. Per-scene optimization methods lack the learned 3D prior needed when observations are sparse or surfaces are glossy or transparent. Existing generative methods leverage such priors to complete geometry in sparsely observed regions, but typically operate at a fixed resolution over a limited spatial extent, trading spatial coverage against detail. Reconstructing a large scene therefore often requires partitioning it into independently processed overlapping local regions, making it difficult to maintain global geometric consistency. To address these issues, we propose T3lescope, which applies a single fixed-resolution generator across scene scales in an inference-time coarse-to-fine cascade. A coarse level establishes the scene layout, and finer levels perturb and denoise geometry inherited from the coarser level within progressively finer spatial cells to recover surface detail. The model is trained on individual cells at multiple scales and shares its weights across all levels, so no hierarchy is fixed during training, and the number of levels, cell scales, and cell locations are determined at inference time. On indoor, outdoor, and city-scale scenes, T3lescope outperforms feed-forward and generative baselines, matches or surpasses per-scene optimization, and recovers fine structures as well as glossy and transparent surfaces. These results show that our method generalizes across diverse scenes, view counts, and image resolutions. Project page: https://pfnet-research.github.io/t3lescope/

</details>


### [67] [A Fully Automatic Pipeline for 3D Dendrite Instance Segmentation in SBF-SEM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03332)
*Zewen Zhuo,Ilya Belevich,Eija Jokitalo,Alejandra Sierra,Jussi Tohka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种完全自动的3D树突实例分割流水线，包括YOLOv6引导的下采样切片SAM提示、两维掩码迭代细化、随机森林3D实例连接和高分辨率实例增强，该流水线无需手动提示即可实现高语义准确度和良好的树突分割结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在利用3D重建技术量化大脑结构可塑性时，由于大量数据手动标注不可能实现，本文提出了一种自动化方法以提高效率和精度。

Method: 该方法结合了YOLOv6引导的下采样切片SAM提示、迭代二维掩码细化、随机森林3D实例连接和高分辨率实例增强，最终实现高精度的3D树突实例分割。

Result: 在实验中，该方法在控制组织中实现了高语义准确度（Dice 0.93和0.91），而在更具挑战性的癫痫组织中主要限制在于密集区域的实例识别。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的方法能够高分辨率地恢复树突突触，为进一步的突触层面分析提供基础，代码已开源。

Abstract: Accurate three-dimensional (3D) reconstruction of individual dendrites in serial block-face scanning electron microscopy (SBF-SEM) is essential for quantifying structural plasticity in the brain, yet manual annotation at scale is infeasible. We present a fully automatic pipeline for 3D dendrite instance segmentation that unifies YOLOv6-guided Segment Anything Model (SAM) prompting on downsampled slices, iterative two-dimensional mask refinement, random forest 3D instance linking, and instance-aware high-resolution refinement using nnU-Net at native resolution into a single system requiring no manual prompting at inference. Applied to hippocampal CA1 SBF-SEM datasets from a control rat and a pilocarpine- induced epileptic rat, our pipeline reconstructs coherent, well- separated dendrites with high semantic accuracy (Dice 0.93 and 0.91) and strong instance-level performance on control tissue, while analysis of the more challenging epileptic tissue identifies instance recognition in dense regions as the principal remaining limitation. The high-resolution refinement stage recovers thin dendritic protrusions, providing a basis for downstream spine- level analysis. Code is available at https://github.com/ ZE-WEN/dendrite-3d-instance-seg.

</details>


### [68] [LAS-CLIP: A Lightweight Adapter Steering Approach for CLIP's Visual Encoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03370)
*Anh-Khoa Dinh-Duc,Duc-Tai Dinh,Tam V. Nguyen,Minh-Triet Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LAS-CLIP 提出了一种轻量级的适配器引导方法，保持 CLIP 的所有参数冻结，通过 MaskAdapter 生成注意力偏差并将它们注入冻结的自注意力层，使其在目标区域聚焦。这种方法在不需要掩码时能无缝地恢复为普通 CLIP，同时保持其基础的零样本能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 CLIP 调整方法依赖于视觉提示、输入掩码或编码器微调，这会损害预训练的表示。为了克服这些问题，提出了 LAS-CLIP 来实现区域级别的任务，同时保留零样本能力。

Method: 提出了一种名为 LAS-CLIP 的方法，该方法通过一个紧凑的 MaskAdapter 生成特定于头部和层的注意力偏差，这些偏差被注入到冻结的自注意力层中，以引导关注目标区域，同时保持整个基础架构的不变。

Result: 在 Imagenet-S 的零样本分类和 RefCOCO 的参考表达理解任务中，与 Alpha-CLIP 相比，尽管 Alpha-CLIP 整个编码器在数百万样本上进行了微调，LAS-CLIP 的性能却是竞争性的或更优的。此外，定性的分析结果表明在不正确的掩码下和下游生成任务中，LAS-CLIP 具有更强的表示一致性。

Conclusion: LAS-CLIP 方法表明，即使在使用少量可训练参数（约 116K 到 145K 个）和有限的训练样本（100K）的情况下，也能在不牺牲性能的情况下实现区域级别的任务和保持零样本能力。

Abstract: CLIP's visual encoder produces only global image representations, limiting its use in region-level tasks. Existing adaptations rely on visual prompting, input masking, or encoder fine-tuning, each compromising pre-trained representations. We propose LAS-CLIP, a Lightweight Adapter Steering approach that keeps every CLIP parameter frozen. A compact MaskAdapter generates per-head, per-layer attention biases from an input mask and injects them into the frozen self-attention layers, steering attention toward the target region. Crucially, because the backbone remains strictly untouched, LAS-CLIP seamlessly reverts to vanilla CLIP when no mask is provided, preserving its foundational zero-shot capabilities. With approximately 116K to 145K trainable parameters and 100K training samples on two T4 GPUs, LAS-CLIP achieves competitive or superior results compared to Alpha-CLIP on ImageNet-S zero-shot classification and RefCOCO referring expression comprehension, despite the latter fine-tuning its entire encoder on millions of samples. Qualitative analysis further confirms stronger representational fidelity under incorrect masks and in downstream generation. Our project page is link to https://github.com/AnhKhoa585/lasclip

</details>


### [69] [EVEWorld: Physical Evolution Supervision for Embodied World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03374)
*Kaiqi Wang,Songxin Zhang,Zejian Xie,Xiao Xiong,Zhuoyang Song,Ziwei Wu,Jun Yu Lu,Yitan Teng,Ziying Song,Jiaxing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EVEWorld 提出了一种新的物理演变监督框架，通过 Instance-Guided Restoration (IGR) 和 Temporal Instance Alignment (TIA) 促进了实例一致性和跨帧一致性，显著减少了 Model Laziness Rate (MLR)，在多个基准测试中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界模型在物理推理方面存在不足，本文旨在通过引入物理演变监督框架 EVEWorld 来解决这些问题，提高机器人在模拟交互中的能力。

Method: EVEWorld 方法包括两个组成部分：Instance-Guided Restoration (IGR) 和 Temporal Instance Alignment (TIA)。IGR 通过恢复监督促进实例一致性，TIA 则通过跨帧对齐目标实例来保持跨帧一致性。此外，还引入了 Model Laziness Rate (MLR) 作为衡量生成轨迹中实例不一致性的指标。

Result: 在 DreamGenBench，EWMBench 和 PBench 上的广泛实验表明，EVEWorld 在 Model Laziness Rate (MLR) 上实现了 87.5% 的显著减少。在 WorldArena 2.0 的 Track 1 领先榜中，模型在 JEPA 相似性方面排名第 6 位，总体排名第 17 位，验证了演变监督策略的有效性。

Conclusion: EVEWorld 通过对物理演变的监督，显著增强了机器人在模拟交互中的表现，为机器人学习提供了更强大的基础。

Abstract: Embodied world models enable scalable simulation of embodied interactions for robot learning. However, existing models are prone to Model Laziness, as they focus on visual fidelity at the expense of physical reasoning and lack process-level supervision over the temporal dynamics of manipulated objects. In this work, we propose EVEWorld, a physical evolution-supervision framework for physically consistent target evolution. EVEWorld consists of two components: Instance-Guided Restoration (IGR) and Temporal Instance Alignment (TIA). First, IGR promotes instance consistency through restoration supervision. Second, TIA promotes cross-frame consistency by aligning target instances across adjacent frames. We further introduce the Model Laziness Rate (MLR), a metric that measures persistent violations of instance consistency in generated trajectories. Extensive experiments on DreamGenBench, EWMBench, and PBench demonstrate the effectiveness of EVEWorld, notably achieving an 87.5% reduction in MLR compared with GigaWorld-0. On the WorldArena 2.0 Track 1 leaderboard, our model ranks 6th in JEPA Similarity and 17th overall, which further validates the performance of our evolution supervision strategy.

</details>


### [70] [Interpretable Deepfake Detection in Videos via Explicit Forensic Features and Temporal Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03380)
*Chahira Benhama,Mohand Saïd Allili,Assia Hamadene*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种可解释的深度伪造检测框架，通过建模视频序列中的空间和时间连贯面部特征来检测深度伪造内容，该框架利用物理基础的法医线索，实现了透明分析和多数据集的良好泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于深度伪造视频在帧级上可能具有视觉一致性，但存在细微的时间不一致性，现有的端到端深度模型依赖于隐式表示，可能导致不透明的分析结果，因此需要一种新的框架来提高检测效果并增强透明性。

Method: 该方法通过将视频转化为身份一致的面部轨迹，将它们分割成固定长度的时间窗口，并使用包含四种互补域（光度、纹理、几何和压缩）的68结构描述符来表示每一帧，进而通过LSTM网络捕捉时间依赖性和微妙的不连续性。

Result: 该方法在四个基准数据集FaceForensics++、Celeb-DF v2、DFDC定制子集和DeeperForensics上分别取得了98.0%、91.0%、97.6%和96.2%的F1分数，展示了良好的跨数据集泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该研究为视频深度伪造检测提供了一种稳健且可解释的解决方案，通过结合物理基础的法医线索和多种特征表示，显著提高了检测的效果和透明度。

Abstract: Deepfake detection in videos remains challenging, as manipulated content may appear visually consistent at the frame level while exhibiting subtle temporal inconsistencies. This paper introduces an interpretable deepfake detection framework that models spatially and temporally coherent facial features in video sequences. Unlike end-to-end deep models relying on implicit representations, the proposed approach explicitly encodes physically grounded forensic cues, enabling transparent analysis and improved multi-dataset generalization. The pipeline transforms videos into identity-consistent facial trajectories, segments them into fixed-length temporal windows, and represents each frame using 68 structured descriptors spanning four complementary domains: photometric, textural, geometric, and compression-based features. These descriptors provide a compact multi-domain representation of manipulation artifacts and are processed by a Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) network to capture temporal dependencies and subtle irregularities. Evaluation on four benchmark datasets, FaceForensics++, Celeb-DF v2, a curated subset of the DeepFake Detection Challenge (DFDC), and DeeperForensics, yields strong and consistent F1-scores of 98.0%, 91.0%, 97.6%, and 96.2%, respectively. The approach also demonstrated a good cross-dataset generalization, providing a robust and interpretable solution for video deepfake detection.

</details>


### [71] [From Patching to Pruning Visual Computation in Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03389)
*Rahul Chowdhury,Timothy A Rupprecht,Xuan Shen,Shaoyi Huang,Pu Zhao,Yanzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: P2P是一种训练无损的框架，通过前向和反向层扫描识别无需特定视觉计算的解码器区域，用固定中立代理激活向量替换，从而减少了55%的FLOPs，同时保留了序列长度、标记顺序、位置信息和残差路径，证明了视觉处理在VLMs中不是均匀分布的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉语言模型（VLMs）在推理时消耗大量成本，因为每个视觉标记都会被每个解码器层的注意和MLP投影处理。这种处理在许多深度时是不必要的。P2P框架通过机制可解释性，提出了一种在不修改预训练模型权重的情况下减少计算量的方法。

Method: P2P通过前向和反向层扫描进行验证指导下的动态剪枝，识别出可以使用固定中立代理激活向量替代的解码器区域。此过程保留了序列长度、标记顺序、位置信息和残差路径。

Result: P2P在四个VLMs上的实验表明，即使在3%的准确度公差下，也可以保持94%的密集准确率，同时降低55%的FLOPs。此外，P2P能够有效识别不同层级在视觉处理中的作用，使得早期和晚期层可以减少特定的视觉计算。

Conclusion: P2P框架不仅提高了VLMs的推理效率，而且还提供了一种洞察视觉信息在VLMs中处理机制的工具。

Abstract: Vision language models (VLMs) incur substantial inference cost because every visual token is processed by the attention and MLP projections of every decoder layer, even when token-specific visual computation is unnecessary at many depths. We introduce Patch-to-Prune (P2P), inspired by Mechanistic Interpretability, a training-free framework that converts activation patching from a diagnostic tool into an inference-time computation bypass. P2P performs validation-guided forward and backward layer sweeps to identify decoder regions whose visual-token projection outputs can be replaced by fixed neutral proxy activation vectors within a user-specified accuracy tolerance. Unlike conventional token-pruning methods, P2P preserves the sequence length, token order, positional information, attention mask, and residual pathways, thereby pruning computation without removing tokens or modifying the pretrained model weights. We evaluate P2P on four VLMs from the Qwen2.5-VL and LLaVA families across seven multi-modal benchmarks using mutually disjoint calibration, validation, and test partitions. P2P at a 3% tolerance retains around 94% of dense accuracy while reducing FLOPs by 55%. Beyond these efficiency gains, our layer-wise analysis suggests that visual processing in VLMs is non-uniformly distributed across decoder depth: early and late layers often require little token-specific visual computation, whereas intermediate layers appear to perform most task-relevant visual integration, enabling later reasoning to rely largely on visual information already embedded in shared residual and textual representations. This makes P2P both an efficient inference framework and a causal lens into visual information processing in VLMs.

</details>


### [72] [Native Action-Prior Learning from Videos for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03391)
*Zhaochong An,Fei Zhang,Menglin Jia,Duncan Frost,Zijian Zhou,Yikai Wang,Xudong Wang,Aditya Patel,Belinda Zeng,Tao Xiang,Serge Belongie,Amir Bar,Sen He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NAVA-WAM 通过直接从仅观测视频中预训练动作策略，无需间接的表示到控制的转换或单独的潜在动作模型，从而实现动作先验学习，该方法在广泛的实验中表现出优于先前方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进现有的基于视觉的动力学集成方法，减少对动作标注的依赖，提升在未见过的任务上的推广能力。

Method: NAVA-WAM 采用两阶段培训策略：第一阶段从仅观测的视频中预训练动作理解转移网络（Action-DiT），通过视频流匹配监督学习视觉转换以优化动作和学习先验；第二阶段使用动作标记的演示进行后训练，通过动作流匹配和不对称注意力机制增强视觉分支的独立性和效率。

Result: NAVA-WAM 在分布内和分布外设置中均表现出优越性，特别是在动作标签效率和真实机器人上的泛化能力方面。
