Conclusion: NAVA-WAM 将动作先验学习引入动力学集成架构中，实现从仅观测视频直接预训练动作策略，提供了一条超越标注动作数据的可扩展路径。

Abstract: World action models integrate future visual dynamics with robot action prediction, but their scalability remains limited by the need for action-annotated robot trajectories. Observation-only videos contain rich evidence about interaction dynamics, but existing approaches typically use them either to pretrain visual representations that must later be adapted for control, or to infer latent actions that are subsequently grounded to robot commands. We present NAVA-WAM, which introduces native action-prior learning by directly pretraining the action policy from observation-only videos, avoiding indirect representation-to-control transfer or a separate latent-action model. Our training consists of two stages. First, we pretrain on observation-only videos, where future-video flow-matching supervision over visual transitions is propagated through transition-structured joint attention to optimize the Action-DiT and learn action-relevant priors. Second, we use action-labeled demonstrations to post-train the Action-DiT for robot control through joint video--action flow matching, while asymmetric attention decouples the visual branch from iterative action denoising and enables efficient action-only inference. Extensive experiments show that NAVA-WAM consistently outperforms prior approaches under both in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings, while demonstrating strong action-label efficiency and effective real-robot generalization. These results establish native action-prior learning as an effective approach to directly pretrain action policies from observation-only videos, providing a scalable path beyond action-labeled robot data.

</details>


### [73] [Beyond Entropy: Self-Diagnostic Multi-Role Token Optimization for Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03400)
*Yudong Han,Yong Wang,Zaiquan Yang,Liang Lin,Chongyang Tao,Xiangxiang Chu,Liyuan Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了DyCPO框架，它通过动态的反事实信号优化实现了可靠的token选择和自适应反事实干预，从而在多模态强化学习中提高了token级别的信用分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法如高熵token启发式方法和基于反事实的视觉token定位在视觉推理中存在探索过长和过多伪视觉噪声的问题，DyCPO旨在解决这些问题。

Method: DyCPO框架通过建造一个多角色依赖度度量，平衡视觉探索与答案相关性挖掘，同时抑制仅探索的填充token和伪视觉噪声。它动态地从模型的成功和失败部署中获取反事实信号，进行自我诊断分析。

Result: 在复杂视频推理和一般视频理解基准测试中，DyCPO展示了持续的性能改进，确立了它作为多模态强化学习中可靠的token级别信用分配范式的地位。

Conclusion: DyCPO通过动态反事实干预优化实现了可靠的token选择，解决了现有方法中存在的问题，展示了在多模态强化学习中的应用效果。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has substantially advanced multimodal reasoning, yet it remains fundamentally limited by ambiguous token-level credit assignment. While high-entropy token heuristics encourage possibility exploration, naively extending them to video reasoning tends to induce lengthy reasoning, as the model becomes overly reliant on high-entropy visual activations. Alternative approaches that rely on counterfactual-based visual token localization for credit assignment also tend to over-prioritize visual exploration at the expense of decisive reasoning cues for answer derivation, thereby exacerbating the interference from spurious visual nuances. Moreover, these methods employ static counterfactual strategies that fail to co-evolve with the policy during training. In this paper, we introduce DyCPO, a co-evolutionary framework that jointly optimizes reliable token selection and adaptive counterfactual intervention. It constructs a multi-role dependence metric to balance visual exploration and answer-relevance mining in token-wise contrastive learning, while suppressing exploration-only filler tokens and spurious visual noise. Rather than relying on static counterfactual priors, DyCPO dynamically derives counterfactual signals from the model's own successful and failed rollouts, enabling self-diagnostic analysis and co-evolution of the optimization objective with the policy. Extensive experiments on complex video reasoning and general video understanding benchmarks demonstrate consistent performance improvements, establishing DyCPO as a robust token-level credit assignment paradigm for multimodal reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [74] [ForestQuery: Boundary-Aware and Spatially Anchored Query Learning for Unified Forest Point Cloud Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03403)
*Zhihao Zhan,Le Tao,Yifei Tian,Xin Liu,Jie Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ForestQuery 是一种边界感知且基于空间的查询学习框架，用于统一森林点云分割任务。该方法通过两种设计策略解决了实例查询构建和语义查询增强的问题，提高了森林点云的实例和语义分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于查询的森林分割方法在统一语义和实例预测方面表现出潜力，但它们尚未充分利用森林特有的空间结构并且未能充分考虑边界不确定性。

Method: ForestQuery 采用了边界感知查询学习和基于空间的语义查询增强技术。具体来说，它引入了边界不确定性的建模以引导实例查询的可靠构建，并通过自适应损失重权进行查询优化。同时，它还利用可学习的3D锚点来编码森林垂直层次结构先验知识，以增强语义查询的空间参考。

Result: 在多个公开的森林点云基准测试和一个自我收集的真实世界数据集上进行的广泛实验证明，ForestQuery 在多种森林场景中的一棵树分割和语义分割方面都取得了稳定的进步。

Conclusion: ForestQuery 提供了一种创新的方法来提高森林点云分割的性能，通过增强实例和语义查询，有效解决了森林点云分割中的挑战。

Abstract: Forest point cloud segmentation is fundamental for fine-grained 3D forest scene understanding, yet remains challenging due to irregular tree structures, severe occlusions, density variations, and ambiguous instance boundaries. Recent query-based forest segmentation methods have shown promise for unified semantic and instance prediction, but they still insufficiently exploit forest-specific spatial structure and account for boundary uncertainty. In this paper, we propose ForestQuery, a boundary-aware and spatially anchored query learning framework for unified forest point cloud segmentation. ForestQuery enhances instance and semantic query learning through two complementary designs. Specifically, boundary uncertainty is explicitly modeled to guide reliable instance query construction and modulate query optimization through adaptive loss reweighting. Meanwhile, spatially anchored semantic query enhancement (SA-SQE) introduces learnable 3D anchors encoding forest vertical stratification priors to enrich semantic queries with explicit spatial references. We evaluate ForestQuery on multiple public forest point cloud benchmarks and a self-collected annotated real-world dataset. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent improvements in both individual-tree segmentation and semantic segmentation across diverse forest scenes. Code and data are publicly available at https://zhan994.github.io/ForestQuery

</details>


### [75] [OuroReward: Sequential Reward Scheduling for Reinforcement Learning in Text-to-3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03423)
*Bingyang Cui,Yujie Zhang,Yiling Xu,Yunfeng Guan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了OuroReward和AdaSelect策略，优化了文本到3D生成中的多维度优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习方法在进行文本到3D生成时会同时优化多个质量维度，但这种方法会导致维度间的不平衡优化和持续干扰问题，本文提出了一种新的策略来解决这个问题。

Method: OuroReward提出了一个干扰感知的序列奖励调度策略，通过估计多维度之间的相互依赖关系，构造了一个循序渐进的优化路径。此外，AdaSelect是一种自适应的提示选择策略，通过识别与模型当前能力相匹配的提示，提高训练的稳定性。

Result: 实验结果表明，该框架可以从多个维度提高生成质量。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种新的RL方法，以优化多维度的文本到3D生成问题，取得了良好的效果。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for Text-to-3D (T23D) generation requires optimization across multiple quality dimensions such as semantic alignment and texture clarity. Existing methods typically optimize these dimensions simultaneously through multiple reward aggregation, without explicitly modeling inter-dimension dependencies. This can cause imbalanced optimization and persistent interference among conflicting dimensions. To address this limitation, we propose OuroReward, an interference-aware sequential reward scheduling strategy for T23D RL. OuroReward first estimates pairwise dependencies among dimensions and constructs a cyclic optimization path that minimizes cumulative interference. By incorporating the tail-to-head dependency, the cycle captures global compatibility across the entire schedule. Then, OuroReward converts the cycle into a one-pass sequence, and starts optimization from the dimension with the lowest aggregate interference. Rather than assigning a fixed optimization budget to each dimension-wise reward, training adaptively determines when to advance to the next reward according to the remaining optimization headroom of the current one. We further introduce AdaSelect, an adaptive prompt selection strategy that identifies reliable and informative prompts aligned with the model's current capability. By focusing policy updates on these prompts, AdaSelect effectively improves training stability. Extensive experiments across different T23D models and RL algorithms demonstrate that our framework consistently improves generation quality across multiple dimensions.

</details>


### [76] [Depth Hypothesis Guided Iterative Refinement for Event-Image Monocular Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03439)
*Daikun Liu,Teng Wang,Changyin Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HypoDepth是一种基于事件图像的一阶段单目深度迭代细化框架，通过引入离散深度假设体(DHV)，将深度回归问题转化为深度搜索任务，从而有效处理深度估计中的逆问题和非线性性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法大多通过优化上下文特征来提升单目深度估计的性能，但仍然面临直接全局深度回归的逆问题和非线性性质的挑战。HypoDepth通过引入离散深度假设体(DHV)，将该问题转化为深度搜索任务，旨在克服这些挑战。

Method: HypoDepth首先创建DHV（深度假设体），并将其特征与上下文特征间的3D成本体积进行构建，随后进行多尺度相关性搜索以引导稳定的残差优化，从而实现全局到局部的多分辨率精细化。

Result: HypoDepth在DSEC和MVSEC数据集中取得了最先进的结果，具有出色的零样本泛化能力。此外，在资源受限的设备上，该模型实现了较高的准确性和效率之间的平衡，能够实现实时性能。

Conclusion: HypoDepth代表了单目深度估计领域的一种突破，展示了离散深度假设体在解决逆问题和非线性性质方面的潜力。

Abstract: Event cameras hold excellent dynamic properties, showing great potential for monocular depth estimation (MDE). However, existing methods mainly improve performance by optimizing contextual features, but still struggle with the ill-posed and nonlinear nature of direct full-depth regression. In this paper, we propose HypoDepth, the first event-image monocular depth iterative refinement framework. By introducing a discrete Depth Hypothesis Volume (DHV), we transform the depth regression problem into a constrained depth search task. Specifically, we construct a 3D cost volume between the DHV features and contextual features and perform a multi-scale correlation search to guide stable residual optimization. This lightweight cost volume enables efficient global-to-local refinement across multi-resolution. Our method outperforms existing approaches on DSEC and MVSEC with state-of-the-art results and strong zero-shot generalization. Meanwhile, our tiny model achieves an excellent balance between accuracy and efficiency, enabling real-time performance on resource-limited devices.

</details>


### [77] [ChromaGS: Text-Driven Semantic Editing of 4D Gaussian Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03441)
*Antonio Canela,Jordi Sànchez-Riera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ChromaGS提供了一种实时、语言引导的3D高斯头像颜色编辑方法，通过自然语言修改语义区域的颜色，并在渲染时应用编辑，无需重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于生成的编辑方法可能会引入意外修改，ChromaGS希望通过提供确定性的、精确局部化的语义控制来克服这些问题，为用户提供直观的一体化交互体验。

Method: ChromaGS通过为每个高斯原始数据添加学习到的软分配给语义区域，并将颜色分解为区域级别的基础颜色和高斯级别的余项，实现了色彩的连贯转移。该方法还包括一个多阶段语言管道，将文本指令转换为目标颜色。

Result: 实验证明ChromaGS能够在不同主题下保持忠实的外观并提供直观的交互。

Conclusion: ChromaGS提供了一种高效、准确的实时3D动画角色颜色编辑方法，增强了用户对语义区域颜色的控制。

Abstract: We present ChromaGS, a method for real-time, language-guided color editing of animatable 3D Gaussian head avatars. Given a trained animatable avatar, users can instantly modify the color of semantic regions through natural language, with edits applied at render time and no retraining required. Our key insight is to augment each Gaussian primitive with learned soft assignments to semantic regions and decompose colors into region-level base colors and Gaussian-level residuals. This decomposition enables coherent color transfer: modifying a region's base color propagates naturally through all associated Gaussians while preserving fine appearance details encoded in residuals. A two-stage language pipeline translates text instructions into target colors, supporting both absolute specifications and relative adjustments. Unlike generative editing methods that may introduce unintended modifications, our approach provides deterministic, precisely localized semantic control. Experiments demonstrate faithful appearance preservation and intuitive interaction across diverse subjects. Project page and code are available at: https://a-canela.github.io/chromags/

</details>


### [78] [Corrupted but Correct: Why Vision-Language Models Lie to Themselves Internally](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03445)
*Arun Josephraj Arokiaraj,Zekun Wu,Adriano Koshiyama*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过操纵视觉语言模型的输入，可以使其在教师强制训练阶段几乎达到零误差，但自由生成时仍保留原始正确的描述。研究表明，这一现象主要是由语言模型的解码器的先验决定的，而不是视觉编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究视觉语言模型的对抗鲁棒性，特别是在自回归模型中的表现，并探究其中的区别原因。

Method: 使用Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct模型和受控的两阶段PGD攻击，对200个独立的COCO图像进行分析。通过图像级别的像素统计、重塑条件下的预测值以及语言模型解码器各层的追踪来识别问题的根本原因。

Result: 实验证明，图像级别的像素统计几乎没有预测图像被操纵的能力。进一步分析发现，在生成过程中，目标词在生成过程中固定位于词汇表中的第3,488位，而无论最终结果如何，视觉编码器都会对每个图像的表示进行相似程度的操纵，但语言模型解码器会根据图像的抵抗性差异影响这种操纵。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，视觉语言模型的对抗鲁棒性主要依赖于语言解码器的先验，而不是视觉编码器，这直接指导了对部署中视觉语言模型的忠实性评估和防御措施重点的方向定位。

Abstract: A targeted adversarial perturbation can drive a vision-language model's (VLM's) teacher-forced training loss for a fixed target caption to near zero, yet the same model, allowed to generate freely, produces the original, correct description with no trace of the target. We call this dissociation the train/inference gap, and give it a precise mechanistic account on Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct using a controlled two-stage PGD attack on 200 held-out COCO images. First, we show that image-level pixel statistics, including a correctly re-implemented, texture-based attackability measure from the CNN robustness literature, have essentially no predictive power over which images are corrupted (best predictor r=-0.050, p=0.484; ridge regression R^2=0.069). Second, using the logit lens, we localise the gap to a single autoregressive step: the rank of the target token, conditioned on the correct first token already being generated, is fixed at exactly 3,488 out of 152,064 vocabulary entries for every image and every condition, with zero variance. Third, tracking target-token rank across all 28 LLM decoder layers reveals that the visual encoder corrupts every image's representation by a comparable margin regardless of eventual outcome, but the language model decoder then differentially arbitrates: amplifying the corrupted signal for susceptible images and actively suppressing it, past its clean-image baseline, for resistant ones (p<0.001, rank-biserial r=0.579). A linear probe on the merger hidden state separates these two outcomes with AUC=0.858, though we flag a circularity concern in this estimate. Together these results argue that adversarial robustness in autoregressive VLMs is substantially a property of the language decoder's prior, not the visual encoder, with direct implications for where faithfulness evaluations and defenses for deployed VLM systems should be targeted.

</details>


### [79] [Preserving Anatomical Continuity: Three-Stage Pipeline for Colon Segmentation in 3D Abdominal CT Scans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03467)
*Deshan Kalupahana,Sonit Singh,Praveen Ravindran,Arcot Sowmya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种三阶段拓扑保持分割管道，旨在解决深度学习方法在CT图像中进行结肠分割时预测不连续的问题。通过中心轴桥接和重建阶段，实现了更好的结构一致性，同时保持分割准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的结肠分割对于结直肠疾病的分析至关重要。然而，基于深度学习的方法往往会产生断开的预测，因为结肠的解剖结构非常复杂。

Method: 提出了一个三阶段的拓扑保持分割管道。第一阶段使用基于深度学习的方法进行初步分割，第二阶段通过中心线桥接重新连接不连通的区域，第三阶段通过重建阶段进一步细化连续性。

Result: 在采用重叠、距离和拓扑度量的TotalSegmentator和RAOS数据集上进行的评估表明，该方法在保持分割准确性的同时，提高了结构一致性。

Conclusion: 该方法增强了拓扑完整性，使结肠分割更加可靠，适用于临床和研究应用。

Abstract: Accurate colon segmentation from CT images is essential for colorectal disease analysis, yet deep learning based methods often produce disconnected predictions due to complex anatomy. This study introduces a three-stage, topology-preserving segmentation pipeline to address this issue. The first stage performs initial deep learning-based segmentation, followed by centreline bridging to reconnect disjoint regions and a reconstruction stage to refine continuity. Evaluations on TotalSegmentator and RAOS datasets using overlap, distance and topology-based metrics demonstrate improved structural consistency while maintaining segmentation accuracy. The proposed method enhances topological integrity, enabling more reliable colon segmentation for clinical and research applications.

</details>


### [80] [A Vision-Language Model (VLM)-based Pipeline for End-to-End Procedural Modeling of Field-Grown Maize from Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03468)
*Mozhgan Hadadi,Talukder Z. Jubery,Adarsh Krishnamurthy,Baskar Ganapathysubramanian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种自动化管道，可以从3D点云数据中重建无须人工调整或特定物种训练数据的程序生成的玉米模型。通过多模态视力-语言模型标注叶片中线，几何算法进行反投影，交叉视图共识合并成3D叶片，并通过可微分NURBS拟合细化表面。整个植株的Chamfer距离中位数为5.4毫米，重建效果优于半自动管道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现大规模田野种植作物的可编辑3D模型支持高通量表型分析及计算育种试验，传统的手动调整和特定物种训练方法耗时，因此需要一个无需人工调参和特定物种训练数据的自动化管道。

Method: 研究采用多模态视力-语言模型标注叶片中线，通过几何算法反投影和交叉视图共识重建3D叶片，使用NURBS模型生成器填充器官参数，并通过可微分NURBS拟合细化叶片表面。

Result: 该管道在100株来自MaizeField3D数据集的玉米植株上达到了中位数5.4毫米的Chamfer距离，比基于手动标注的半自动管道的重建效果更佳。该方法在没有使用那些标签作为输入的情况下，恢复了1,017个（99.4%）高交集度的参考叶片。

Conclusion: 该自动化管道表明，通过合适的后续几何处理可以将VLM注释转换为有用的器官级测量，使生成可编辑的3D植物资产成为大规模表型实验可行的方法。

Abstract: Editable 3D models of field-grown crops support high-throughput phenotyping and in silico breeding trials, but building them from scanned point clouds requires organ-level segmentation and fitting. Procedural generators can turn an organ-level parameter set into an analysis-suitable 3D model, but obtaining that set requires hours of manual tuning per plant or segmentation models trained on species-specific labels. We present an automated pipeline that reconstructs procedural maize models from raw 3D point clouds without manual tuning or species-specific training data. A multimodal vision-language model (VLM) annotates leaf midlines in rendered orthographic views. Deterministic geometric algorithms back-project the annotations onto the point cloud, merge them into 3D leaves by cross-view consensus, and grow the midlines to full blades on an orientation-weighted surface graph. Measured organ parameters populate a plant descriptor for a Non-Uniform Rational B-Spline (NURBS)-based procedural model generator. Each leaf surface is then refined against its scan points by differentiable NURBS fitting. The pipeline reached a median whole-plant Chamfer distance of 5.4 mm on 100 genotypically diverse field-grown maize plants from the MaizeField3D dataset. The reconstructions were closer to the scans than those of an earlier semi-automated pipeline based on manual annotations. The pipeline recovered 1,017 of 1,023 (99.4%) curated reference leaves at an intersection-over-union of at least 0.5 without using those labels as input. These results show that VLM annotations become usable organ-level measurements when downstream geometric stages can correct them. This makes automated generation of editable 3D plant assets feasible at the scale of modern phenotyping experiments.

</details>


### [81] [World Embedding Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03632)
*Yiqi Liu,Ruifeng Yuan,Yang Wang,Long Li,Fengyu Cai,Hou Pong Chan,Jialin Yu,Hao Zhang,Chenghua Lin,Chenghao Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了World Embedding基准，包括8,000个来自80个家庭的受控模拟案例，涵盖流体力学、固体力学、动力学和光学与电磁学，旨在评估视频中物理信息的编码。实验表明，预训练的跨模态嵌入模型在检索和分类任务上的表现不佳，而轻量级探针能够从冻结的视频嵌入中恢复有用的物理信息。继续对比训练可以改善检索和分类，但会降低物理属性回归的表现，揭示了物理对齐与定量信息恢复之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来，物理保真度在世界模型和视频生成中引起了越来越多的关注，但视频表示如何编码物理信息尚未充分理解。本文旨在通过创建World Embedding基准来提高对物理信息编码的理解。

Method: 该方法通过创建包含8,000个控制模拟案例的基准，涵盖各种物理领域。这些案例用于三个互补任务：文本-视频检索、物理属性回归以及视频描述配对分类。

Result: 实验结果显示，预训练的跨模态嵌入模型在跨模态物理对齐方面的表现欠佳，但在恢复定量物理信息方面表现良好。继续对比训练能够提高某些任务的性能，但也降低了物理属性回归的表现。

Conclusion: 研究结论表明，需要同时评估物理对齐和属性恢复性能，且物理表示对于提高视频生成的物理保真度具有潜在的实用性。

Abstract: Physical fidelity has received increasing attention in world models and video generation, yet how video representations encode physical information remains less understood. We introduce the World Embedding Benchmark, comprising 8,000 controlled simulation cases from 80 families spanning fluid mechanics, solid mechanics, dynamics, and optics & electromagnetism. Each case pairs a rendered video with simulation-derived physical annotations, supporting three complementary tasks: text-video retrieval, physical-property regression, and multiple-choice video-description pair classification. We use these tasks to distinguish cross-modal physical alignment from the recoverability of quantitative physical information. Evaluated pre-trained omnimodal embedding models show weak retrieval and near-chance within-family pair classification, while lightweight probes recover useful physical information from frozen video embeddings. Continual contrastive training with physics-specific video-text pairs improves retrieval and pair classification but degrades physical-property regression, revealing a trade-off between alignment and quantitative information recoverability. Finally, we use the embeddings to retrieve reference videos for retrieval-augmented generation with MiniMax-H3. Retrieved references improve the physical fidelity of generated videos, with stronger retrieval models yielding larger gains in our experiments. Together, these findings highlight the need to evaluate physical alignment and property recoverability jointly, and demonstrate the utility of physical representations for improving video generation.

</details>


### [82] [UniDynamics: Event-RGB Fusion for Unified Future 4D Dynamic Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03473)
*Daikun Liu,Xin Zhan,Teng Wang,Xiaoping Wang,Changyin Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniDynamics 是一种基于扩散的框架，能够从单一事件-RGB 对生成未来 4D 动态场景（包括 RGB、深度和光学流量），无需依赖长时间历史或控制先验，通过利用事件流作为替代运动先验并通过多模态建模强化几何和运动约束，从而实现物理上合理且时空一致的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的方法依赖于长时间的历史记录或运动控制的先验知识来生成未来场景。而该研究旨在通过利用事件流数据和结合多模态建模来提升动态场景生成的性能，特别是关于高动态运动模糊场景。

Method: 该研究设计了一个事件潜增强(ELE)模块来对齐并增强事件潜变量，以便于扩散模型的条件特征注入，同时引入了一个感知动力学空间(PDS)嵌入到多尺度 U-Net 中，以解耦深度和流动并反馈约束给外观特征，从而增强几何和运动的一致性。

Result: 在 VKitti2 和 DSEC 数据集上的实验展示了UniDynamics 的卓越性能，生成了高质量、时空一致且 4D 一致的未来预测，特别是在高动态运动模糊场景下。

Conclusion: UniDynamics 通过多模态建模和事件流的利用，成功地提高了动态场景生成的质量，在高动态环境中尤其具有优势。

Abstract: We propose UniDynamics, a diffusion-based framework for future 4D dynamic scenes (RGB, depth, and optical flow) generation from a single event-RGB pair, without requiring long histories or control priors as in existing methods, while explicitly modeling future motion fields. The core idea is to leverage event streams to offer an alternative motion prior for single-RGB extrapolation, and to enforce geometric and motion constraints throughout generation via multimodal modeling. Specifically, we design an Event Latent Enhancement (ELE) module to align and enhance event latents into diffusion-injectable conditioning features, providing robust initial motion priors and reliable texture/structure cues. We further introduce a Perceptual Dynamics Space (PDS) embedded in the multi-scale U-Net, which decouples and adaptively interacts depth and flow while continuously feeding back constraints to appearance features, improving geometric-motion consistency for physically plausible and spatiotemporally coherent prediction. Experiments on VKitti2 and DSEC demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, producing high-quality, temporally coherent, and 4D-consistent future predictions, especially under challenging high-speed motion blur.

</details>


### [83] [Fed-ADApt: Federated Anytime Depth Adaptation for Resource-Aware Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03474)
*Abhijeet Parida,Zhifan Jiang,Pooneh Roshanitabrizi,Austin Tapp,Maria J. Ledesma-Carbayo,Syed Muhammad Anwar,Ziyue Xu,Marius George Linguraru,Holger R. Roth*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Fed-ADApt是一种深度自适应的联邦学习框架，能够在保持高效性能的同时，解决低资源医疗机构的计算预算问题，特别适用于医学图像分割任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习框架假设机构之间具有相同的计算预算，这限制了低资源机构的参与度。Fed-ADApt旨在解决这一问题，通过联合解决低计算预算的训练和推理，使低资源医疗机构能够参与联邦学习，同时保持了与全资源模型相当的性能。

Method: Fed-ADApt通过集成多深度监督与层次化深度聚合，允许每个站点根据当地的计算预算进行训练，同时对部署时支持动态深度选择的全球模型都做出了贡献。

Result: 在多站点2D视网膜视网膜盘分割和3D脑肿瘤分割任务中，联邦协作显著增强了在域移位下的鲁棒性。Fed-ADApt在3D任务中达到了与全资源FedAvg相当的性能，在2D任务中的Dice降低了4.7%，同时减少了3D推理成本的19.5%和2D的34.5%的推理成本，以及100%的在最受限站点的训练成本。

Conclusion: Fed-ADApt提供了一种实际且公平的解决方案，使跨异构临床和边缘增强影像环境中的联邦医学图像分割成为可能，特别适合低资源医疗机构的参与。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative training of medical image segmentation models without sharing raw patient data, yet existing approaches assume a homogeneous compute budget across institutions, limiting participation of low-resource sites. We propose Fed-ADApt, a depth-adaptive federated framework for UNet-based segmentation that jointly addresses low-compute training and inference. Fed-ADApt integrates multi-depth supervision with hierarchical depth-wise aggregation, allowing each site to train according to its local compute budget while contributing to a global model that supports dynamic depth selection at deployment. We evaluated Fed-ADApt on multi-site 2D retinal fundus disc segmentation and 3D brain tumor segmentation. Across both tasks, federated collaboration substantially improves robustness under domain shift. Fed-ADApt matched the full-resource FedAvg performance in 3D and achieved competitive 2D performance with a 4.7% average Dice reduction, while reducing average inference cost by 19.5% in 3D and 34.5% in 2D and substantially reducing training cost by 98% at the most constrained sites. Importantly, Fed-ADApt enables low-resource institutions that cannot train full-capacity models to participate in federations while maintaining competitive global performance under a favorable accuracy to efficiency trade-off. By considering training and inference compute budgets, Fed-ADApt provides a practical and equitable solution for federated medical image segmentation across heterogeneous clinical and edge-enabled imaging environments.

</details>


### [84] [Weave Forcing: Compositional Memory Routing for Interactive Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03510)
*Ziyi Wang,Junchi Yao,Heqian Qiu,Wenbo Shi,Chengjiu Wang,Jinyang He,Binkai Hong,Hongliang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Weave Forcing 提出了一种无需额外训练的框架，用于交互生成长视频中的组成记忆复用。通过 LLM 实现语义槽路由来拆分用户提示并明确选择历史参考。使用掩码记忆编织和覆盖自适应 RoPE 来引导当前镜头的生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的自回归视频生成主要是时间和连续场景的扩展，而互动故事讲述需要跨镜头的不同元素组合。Weave Forcing 框架通过解决这些挑战，使视频生成更符合互动故事的需求。

Method: Weave Forcing 使用 LLM 进行语义槽路由，结合掩码记忆编织和覆盖自适应 RoPE，来实现不需要重新训练的记忆重用。

Result: 实验证明，Weave Forcing 能够在生成长视频时提高跨镜头的人物和背景一致性，同时保持视觉质量和文本匹配的竞争力。

Conclusion: Weave Forcing 是一个创新的框架，能够显著提高交互式视频生成的质量，特别是在处理需要使用不同镜头元素的情况下。

Abstract: Recent advances in autoregressive video generation have improved temporal consistency over extended durations, yet interactive storytelling requires more than continuous scene extension: a new shot may combine characters and backgrounds from different historical shots. Whole prompt retrieval can overlook the distinct reference needs of individual components, while directly combining all historical memories may introduce unrelated visual content. To address these problems, we present Weave Forcing, a training-free framework for compositional memory reuse in interactive long video generation. First, we use an LLM for semantic slot routing to decompose user prompts into character and background descriptions and explicitly select suitable historical references for each component. To isolate the required content, masked memory weaving uses contrasting attention maps conditioned on semantic slots to construct refined semantic masks, selectively exposing relevant tokens from compressed historical KV memories to guide the generation of the current shot. We further introduce coverage adaptive RoPE to adjust temporal offsets and memory retention according to no, partial, or full reference coverage, addressing visual artifacts observed when incomplete historical references are positioned close to the current generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Weave Forcing improves cross-shot subject and background consistency while maintaining competitive visual quality and text alignment.

</details>


### [85] [ProgressNet: Sketching and Prompting with a Frozen Text-to-Image Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03512)
*Arkaprabha Basu,Chaitat Utintu,Yi-Zhe Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProgressNet 是一个无需训练的框架，允许冻结的文本到图像模型跟随画图过程，用户可以在画图过程中添加或删除线条，调整提示，并且模型能够实时更新图像，保持良好的保真度和渐进一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图像生成器在画图过程中无法处理逐帧绘制的需求，而且通常无法在保持之前部分生成内容的基础上继续完成图像，也无法像人类那样在中途编辑。ProgressNet解决了这些问题，通过引入三种推理时间机制，使得冻结的文本到图像模型能够实时适应画图过程的变化。

Method: ProgressNet 通过三种推理时机制改进了冻结的文本到图像模型，包括 Previous-Concept Memory (概念记忆)，Layer-Selective K/V Injection (选择性键/值注入层) 和 Banded Adaptive Control (带状自适应控制)。这些机制允许模型在画图过程中保留前一个步骤的状态，根据当前草图的完成度选择性地保留或清除图层特征，并调整对未完成草图的信任程度。

Result: ProgressNet 在不同画图完成度下的生成图像的质量要好于其他比较方法。具体来说，FLUX+ControlNet 基线方法在完成度介于 10% 和 100% 之间的图像质量差倍数大于2；而 ProgressNet 的图像质量基本保持了一个较低的 FID 值。用户倾向于选择 ProgressNet 的生成结果，尤其是在涉及线条擦除的情况下。

Conclusion: ProgressNet 提供了一种新的方法，可以直接利用现有的冻结的文本到图像模型来支持画图过程，而无需改变其原本的参数。这种方法有效地解决了一些画图过程中所面临的问题，提高了生成图像的质量和用户的满意度。

Abstract: Humans draw progressively: a few strokes, a look at the result, a stroke erased, a prompt revised. Image generators do not work this way. They typically take a finished sketch and produce the image in a single pass, so every edit starts the picture again, and the models that do keep state across turns are driven by text, cannot take a stroke, and are too slow to draw with. We present ProgressNet, a training-free framework that lets a frozen text-to-image model follow a drawing session as it unfolds: strokes are added and erased, the prompt is revised, and the image keeps up at about a second per turn. It needs no new parameters because the frozen model already has what a progressive generator needs, a pathway through which the previous turn can be remembered, layers that can carry appearance forward without freezing structure, and an internal signal of how far to trust an unfinished sketch; three inference-time mechanisms (Previous-Concept Memory, Layer-Selective K/V Injection and Banded Adaptive Control) use each in turn. As a sketch fills in, every existing method degrades, the FID of the FLUX+ControlNet baseline doubling between 10% and 100% completion on FS-COCO, while ProgressNet's barely moves; it maintains strong fidelity and progressive coherence across three sketch domains and is preferred by users over five competitors, most widely on erasure.

</details>


### [86] [Feedforward Novel View Synthesis for Heterogeneous Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03522)
*Meng Wei,Cheng Zhang,Boying Li,Yihang Chen,Jianmin Zheng,Hamid Rezatofighi,Jianfei Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种可以处理异构中央相机的前端新颖视图合成方法，通过结合相机位置相对编码和局部射线图，并使用投影感知的2D RoPE来统一不同相机的相对位置推理，从而在混合相机评估中优于相机条件基线，并展示了对全景视图的零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的新颖视图合成方法大多假设目标视图和上下文视图共用固定的相机家族，这在实际多传感器系统中通常是不成立的。作者旨在解决这一问题，使得方法能够处理不同类型的相机，例如透视、鱼眼和全景相机。

Method: 该方法结合了相机位置相对编码（Token-Center Relative Camera Positional Encodings）和局部射线图（local raymaps），并提出了投影感知的2D RoPE（projection-aware 2D RoPE），在相机网格坐标基础上引入由射线诱导的角坐标，确保不同相机间的相对位置推理一致。

Result: 该方法在ScanNet++的异构相机系统中表现良好，优于相机条件基准线，并能够零样本泛化到全景视图。

Conclusion: 这是一种用于异构中央相机的新颖视图合成方法，通过处理不同类型的相机而展示了更好的性能和泛化能力。

Abstract: Feed-forward novel view synthesis has recently shown promising results from sparse posed images, but most existing methods assume that context and target views share a fixed camera family. This homogeneous-camera assumption breaks in practical multi-sensor systems, where perspective, fisheye, and panoramic cameras may coexist and where the target projection may be unseen during training. We study feed-forward NVS across heterogeneous central cameras and identify a key ambiguity introduced by tokenization: a visual token aggregates a projection-dependent bundle of pixel rays, while existing camera encodings mainly expose absolute rays or token-center relations. To address this, we combine token-center relative Camera Positional Encodings and proposed local raymaps, a token-level representation that explicitly describes the intra-patch ray distribution summarized by each token. We further propose projection-aware 2D RoPE, which replaces raw image-grid coordinates with ray-induced angular coordinates so that relative positional reasoning is aligned across camera projections. Together, these components treat diverse cameras as calibrated samplings of a shared ray space rather than separate visual domains. On ScanNet++ with heterogeneous-camera system, our method improves over camera-conditioned baselines under mixed-camera evaluation and demonstrates zero-shot generalization to panoramic views.

</details>


### [87] [DuoMatching: Joint-Marginal Distribution Matching for Few-Step Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03543)
*Jiahao Zhan,Yan Wang,Yongrui Ma,Qunliang Xing,Ruchang Yao,Runtao Liu,Shijie Zhao,Tianfan Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DuoMatching 提出了一种新的分布匹配框架，通过联合边缘形式近似真实视频分布。这种方法增强了帧级监督，提升了视觉质量和语义对齐，同时保留了动作动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的联合分布匹配方法虽然减少了自回归滚动过程中的偏移，但在视觉质量和语义对齐方面仍有局限。DuoMatching通过引入额外的边缘匹配目标改进了这一点。

Method: DuoMatching 使用联合边缘形式来近似真实视频分布，引入了 LatentBridge 来解决视频学生和图像教师之间的潜在表示不匹配问题。此外，Latent Variation Sampling 使帧级监督分布在不同的时间片段中，减少了冗余。

Result: 实验表明，DuoMatching 在视觉质量、构图和语义对齐方面有所改进，同时保持了运动动态。人类评价结果显示，与所有基线相比，整体偏好率超过 80%。

Conclusion: DuoMatching 提供了一种有效的方法来提高视频生成的质量和一致性，未来可以进一步探索如何更有效地利用帧级监督。

Abstract: Streaming video generation has benefited from distribution matching distillation (DMD), which matches the joint distribution of video frames to a video teacher's approximation of the real video distribution. Although this joint matching mitigates drift during autoregressive rollouts, limitations remain in visual quality and semantic alignment. To address these limitations, we propose DuoMatching, a distribution matching framework that approximates the real video distribution through a unified joint-marginal formulation. On top of existing joint matching formulations, the additional marginal matching objective provides dedicated frame-level supervision from an image generator, transferring complementary visual and semantic priors from it. To apply this frame-level supervision in video generation, we introduce LatentBridge to resolve the latent representation mismatch between the video student and the image teacher. Latent Variation Sampling further distributes such frame-level supervision across distinct temporal segments, reducing redundancy. Experiments demonstrate that DuoMatching improves visual quality, composition, and semantic alignment while largely preserving motion dynamics. Human evaluations show overall preference rates above 80% against all evaluated baselines. The project page is available at https://johnzhan2023.github.io/DuoMatching/.

</details>


### [88] [Rethinking What to Cache in Few-Step Diffusion Transformers: Solver-Aware Target Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03577)
*Shuo Yang,Lihao Fang,Yi Zhang,Haixiang Wang,Xincheng Ye,Shufan Chen,Jipeng Guo,Youqing Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 介绍了一种名为AutoTarget的方法，用于选择扩散变换器（DiT）缓存过程中的最佳缓存张量，以优化生成高质量图像和视频的速度和质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的加速方法主要依赖预先决定缓存张量的选择，而AutoTarget方法旨在通过动态选择最有可能减少误差的缓存张量来提高加速效果。

Method: AutoTarget方法首先通过无缓存重演来测量重用每个候选张量所产生的误差，并选择误差最小的候选张量作为缓存目标。此外，分析了误差对最终样本的影响，并针对Euler采样，确定了会产生相同轨迹的缓存目标。

Result: 实验结果显示，最佳缓存目标随模型、图像分辨率和求解器变化而变化。与无缓存运行相比，生成质量仍然保持相近，Calibration排名与预设缓存运行一致。在不同设置下证明了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: AutoTarget方法在保持生成质量的同时显著减少了DiT评估次数和缓存存储量，有助于改善扩散变换器的效率和性能。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) can generate high-quality images and videos, but generating each sample requires multiple costly DiT forward passes. Two common ways to accelerate DiT sampling are step distillation, which reduces the number of sampling steps, and caching, which skips some DiT evaluations by reusing a tensor computed at an earlier step. Most caching methods decide in advance which tensor to reuse. After distillation, adjacent sampling steps are farther apart. Reusing a tensor across this larger gap introduces more error, so choosing what to cache becomes especially important. We therefore introduce AutoTarget, a method that chooses the cached tensor for a given model, solver, and reuse schedule. AutoTarget uses a small set of runs without cache reuse to measure the error caused by reusing each candidate tensor, then selects the candidate with the lowest error. We also analyze how an error at one reuse step affects the final sample. For Euler sampling, we identify cache targets that produce the same trajectory and show why a stored solver update may not. Experiments on distilled image and video DiTs show that the best cache target changes with the model, image resolution, and solver. AutoTarget reduces DiT evaluations and retained cache storage. Generation quality remains close to the corresponding uncached run. On the tested PixArt-LCM and FLUX.1-schnell settings, its calibration ranking matches the ranking from held-out cached runs. To help others reproduce the method, we provide its core implementation on GitHub at https://github.com/wali1024-offical/AutoTarget.

</details>


### [89] [ManifoldSplat: Language-Guided Semantic Shape Editing of 3D Gaussian Head Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03599)
*Antonio Canela,Jordi Sànchez-Riera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ManifoldSplat 是一种语言引导的新型框架，用于动画 3D 笛卡尔点云头像的语义形状编辑，可以在 90 秒内通过单目视频重建并编辑头像，具有高效的渲染速度（约 800 FPS），并提供局部提示对准、几何一致性以及身份保持的新基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前方法在通过自然语言修改几何形状时存在缓慢的优化过程或需要牺牲身份和布线的情况，ManifoldSplat 解决了这些挑战，提供了一个端到端的框架，允许精细定位的局部控制，同时严格保持身份和动画。

Method: ManifoldSplat 使用结构化的 FLAME 流形进行编辑，而不是直接优化无结构的高斯点云。引入了 DeltaRegion，这是一种分隔的条件变分自编码器 (CVAE)，通过其预测局部形状偏移，继而有一个恢复阶段恢复视图一致的细节。

Result: ManifoldSplat 在 90 秒内通过消费者级 GPU 重建并编辑头像，渲染速度高达约 800 FPS。广泛的评估表明，ManifoldSplat 在局部提示对准、几何一致性和身份保持方面达到了最新技术水平。

Conclusion: ManifoldSplat 为语义形状编辑提供了新的可能性，通过严格的局部控制和身份保持，为未来的研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: High-fidelity 3D head avatars have reached near-photorealistic quality. While recent methods enable text-driven manipulation, they struggle to provide fine-grained localized control, often entangling features or lacking geometric consistency. Modifying geometry through natural language currently requires slow per-prompt optimization or compromises identity and rigging. We present ManifoldSplat, the first end-toend framework for language-guided semantic shape editing of animatable 3D Gaussian Splatting avatars reconstructed from monocular videos. By performing edits within the structured FLAME manifold rather than directly optimizing an unstructured Gaussian cloud, we strictly preserve identity and animation. We introduce DeltaRegion, a per-region disentangled Conditional Variational Autoencoder (CVAE) delivering feedforward shape deltas, alongside a refining stage to recover view-consistent details. ManifoldSplat reconstructs and edits an avatar in ~90 seconds on a consumer GPU, rendering at ~800 FPS. Extensive evaluations demonstrate our approach sets a new state-of-the-art in localized prompt alignment, geometric coherence, and identity preservation. Project page and code: https://a-canela.github.io/manifoldsplat/

</details>


### [90] [DEPICT: Scoring Text-to-Image Alignment by Answer Agreement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03617)
*Vasco Ramos,Sandra Godinho Silva,Joao Magalhaes,Ricardo Rei,Pedro Henrique Martins*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DEPICT 是一种无需训练的图像-文本对齐评估方法，通过使用图像基和文本基答案之间的预期一致性来改进错误标注的处理，同时结合整体评分恢复细节，最终优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的评估方法存在多种局限性，DEPICT 的提出旨在解决这些问题。

Method: DEPICT 使用的是图像基和文本基答案之间的预期一致性来进行评估，通过调整问题的重要性来提高对否定性描述的准确率，并通过结合整体评分来恢复细节信息。

Result: DEPICT 在五个基准和三种模型家族中的 eleven backbones 上的表现 excellent，不仅超过了所有无需训练的评估方法，还在两个关于人工相关性的基准上超过了已训练的评估器。

Conclusion: DEPICT 成功地解决了当前评估方法的不足，尤其是在处理错误标记和恢复细节信息方面表现出色。

Abstract: Image-text alignment is a core problem in computer vision with applications in caption evaluation, hallucination detection, data curation, and the benchmarking of text-to-image (T2I) generators. As T2I models improve, benchmarking has become demanding, requiring metrics capable of finding a series of issues like missing objects, swapped attributes, miscounts, and ignored negations. Recent work addresses this by fine-tuning evaluators on preference data or by prompting a vision-language model, either holistically with the caption or with decomposed verification questions. However, existing approaches fall short: fine-tuned metrics remain bound to one backbone and training distribution; holistic metrics miss fine-grained details; and decomposed metrics rely on a fixed-YES assumption that penalizes faithful images whenever that assumption fails. In contrast, we propose DEPICT, a training-free metric that replaces fixed reference answers with expected agreement between image-based and caption-only answers, weighting questions by how decisively the caption determines them. By replacing fixed references, our agreement rule increases negation accuracy from 19% to 88%. To recover the context lost during decomposition, DEPICT merges this agreement score with a holistic score. We evaluate DEPICT on five benchmarks and eleven backbones from three model families and find that it surpasses all training-free metrics and exceeds fine-tuned evaluators on two out of three human-correlation benchmarks.

</details>


### [91] [LoGo: Local-Global Rewards for Consistent Long-Horizon Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03636)
*Ziqi Ma,Shreya Sharma,Mohamed El Banani,Katja Schwarz,Chongjie Ye,Chao-Yuan Wu,Li Fei-Fei,Ben Mildenhall,Georgia Gkioxari,Justin Johnson,Gowthami Somepalli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LoGo通过结合全局和空间局部奖励改进了相机控制视频模型中的3D不一致性，有效减少了局部对象位移、伪影和全局场景变化，提升了视频质量和镜头跟随性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练技术无法有效纠正长时间生成过程中的3D不一致性问题，LoGo旨在针对相机控制视频模型引入一种新的奖励机制，以提高3D一致性、保持镜头跟随以及维持视频质量。

Method: LoGo使用结合全局与局部奖励的方法，其中局部奖励提供精细的信用分配，有助于提升3D一致性；而全局奖励则保证了镜头跟随性与视频质量。

Result: LoGo在三个基模型上对DL3DV和TrajectoryBench的评估中表现出优势，有效减少了局部对象位移、伪影和全局场景变化，证明了后训练视频模型中信用分配的重要性。

Conclusion: LoGo为相机控制视频生成提供了实用的方法，提高了视频的质量和3D一致性，同时也为未来的研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Camera-controlled video models are rapidly advancing toward long generation horizons and complex camera control. A key failure mode is 3D inconsistency: as the camera moves, objects lose permanence and scene structures shift. Existing post-training techniques, which assign a single scalar reward to the entire generation, are poorly suited to correcting these inconsistencies over long horizons. We introduce LoGo, which blends global and spatially localized rewards for camera-controlled video models. The local reward provides fine-grained credit assignment, which substantially improves 3D consistency, while the global reward preserves camera following and video quality. Across three base models, LoGo shows a clear advantage on DL3DV and TrajectoryBench, a new benchmark for long-horizon, complex-camera-control generation that current evaluations lack. LoGo effectively reduces local object shifts, artifacts, and global scene changes, illustrating the importance of credit assignment in post-training video models. Project website: https://ziqi-ma.github.io/logo-website/

</details>


### [92] [On-Board Anomaly Detection for Efficient Marine Environmental Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03649)
*Thomas Goudemant,Clotilde Szywala,Benjamin Francesconi,Michelle Aubrun,Yves Bobichon,Marjorie Bellizzi,Adrien Girard*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多/高光谱传感器的地球观测卫星海洋事件检测管道，使用自监督神经网络压缩卫星图像并利用机器学习模型检测异常，展示了优化后的系统在实际任务中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种能够有效检测海洋环境异常的轻量级管道，提高海洋生态系统健康监测的响应速度和通信效率。

Method: 使用自监督神经网络压缩卫星图像，通过机器学习模型检测异常，并优化以适应小型卫星计算资源。

Result: 与传统算法相比，本研究的管道在海洋环境异常检测上表现良好，能够及时传输关键信息，优化卫星通信带宽。

Conclusion: 该研究为地球观测卫星提供了一种实用的海洋事件检测方法，已被多个卫星任务采用，证明了其实用性。

Abstract: Marine ecosystems are impacted by various threats such as oil spills, algal blooms, and sediment floods, which disrupt habitats, wildlife, and human activities. Advances in satellite imagery and Artificial Intelligence (AI) have enhanced our capabilities for early detection and mitigation of such hazards. In this paper, we propose a marine event detection pipeline for Earth observation satellites equipped with multi- or hyperspectral sensors. Our approach includes a self-supervised neural network encoder that compresses satellite images into a reduced latent space, enabling efficient onboard processing. A machine learning anomaly detection model identifies deviations from normal sea patterns to detect environmental anomalies. We compare its performance against traditional algorithms such as Isolation Forest, One-Class Support Vector Machine and Local Outlier Factors. Our lightweight, resource-efficient pipeline is optimized for deployment on satellites with limited computational resources, ranging from embedded CPUs to AI hardware accelerators. By prioritizing the transmission of critical information, our solution enhances system responsiveness and optimizes satellite communication bandwidth. Demonstrated through current integration across multiple missions, including European Space Agency's (ESA) Phisat-2 mission and Microsoft/Thales Alenia Space IMAGIN-e mission, our pipeline aims to improve marine environmental monitoring by providing timely alerts and efficient data reduction.

</details>


### [93] [ProAR: Learning Prospective Reasoning with Autoregressive Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03664)
*Linghui Shen,Tinghui Zhu,Sheng Zhang,Muhao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProAR 提出了一种新的框架，通过结合目标导向预测和未来表示自对齐机制，使自回归视频生成变为一个目标导向的推理过程，从而改善了视觉推理任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自回归视频生成模型在因果生成方面表现出色，但由于依赖于下一帧预测，只能以短视的、反应式的方式工作。这种情况对于倾向于通过有效中介状态实现目标结果的任务尤为重要。

Method: ProAR 引入了两种关键机制：目标帧预测和未来表示自对齐。目标帧预测通过不对称注意力掩码将目标预测纳入自回归循环中，以确保生成的中间状态不会影响目标。未来表示自对齐则鼓励当前隐藏状态预测即将发生的时空动态。

Result: 实验表明，ProAR 的组成部分可以在各种视觉推理基准测试中提高性能，并且这种框架在训练效率方面表现出色，只需标准自回归基线的 25% 的训练步骤即可超过它们。

Conclusion: ProAR 提出了一种前景导向的方法，改善了自回归视频生成的方法，可以应用于各种视觉推理任务，并且在训练效率上表现出色。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) video models excel at causal generation, but their reliance on next-chunk prediction confines them to a short-sighted, reactive paradigm. This limitation is particularly consequential for reasoning-oriented generation, where achieving a target outcome through valid intermediate states matters more than local visual plausibility. To address this challenge, we propose Learning Prospective Reasoning with Autoregressive Video Models (ProAR), a novel framework that transforms autoregressive video generation into a goal-oriented reasoning process. ProAR introduces two key components: (1) To anchor generation to the long-range outcome, we integrate goal-frame prediction into the autoregressive loop via an asymmetric attention mask, enabling the predicted goal frame to guide the generation of intermediate states without being disrupted by them. (2) To guide short-range transitions, we introduce future representation self-alignment to encourage current hidden states to anticipate upcoming temporal dynamics. By leveraging teacher-forcing in AR training, we extract clean future representations in a single forward pass and align current representations with them using a lightweight, training-only predictor. Together, these two mechanisms seamlessly combine explicit, sparse target supervision with implicit, dense step-wise guidance, promoting coherent, goal-directed reasoning progress with modest computational cost. Experiments show that ProAR's complementary components consistently improve performance across diverse visual reasoning benchmarks. The framework proves highly training-efficient, surpassing fully trained standard AR baselines using only 25% of the training steps. This paradigm also demonstrates promising applicability to embodied reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [94] [SigLIP2 for aerial fire risk classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03689)
*Yunus Serhat Bıçakçı*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了将预训练的SigLIP2图像编码器转移到七个类别火灾风险分类的任务，同时介绍了可重复的数据分区方法和数据记录机制，并通过冻结编码器探针与完整模型适应两种初始运行进行对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文的动机是对火灾风险分类任务进行深入研究，通过使用预训练的SigLIP2图像编码器，旨在改进现有方法，提高分类准确性。

Method: 研究使用了一个公开的火风险训练镜像并进行了一个可重复的分割，同时记录了数据来源、预处理和模型选择过程。实验包括两轮初始运行，分别测试冻结编码器探针和完整模型适应的效果。

Result: 完全适应模型在验证分区上达到了63.05%的准确率和58.94%的宏F1值，而探针模型则为55.95%和50.19%。所有实验使用一个训练种子并在同一验证分区上选择检查点。

Conclusion: 本文的研究结果支持进一步评估SigLIP2在火灾风险分类任务中的应用前景，但尚未在独立测试集或未见过的地区进行性能验证。

Abstract: We examine the transfer of a pretrained SigLIP2 image encoder to seven class fire risk classification from aerial imagery. We introduce a reproducible partition of the public FireRisk training mirror and an implementation that records data provenance, preprocessing and model selection. Two initial runs compare a frozen encoder probe with full model adaptation. On the validation partition, full adaptation reaches 63.05% accuracy and 58.94% macro F1, compared with 55.95% and 50.19% for the probe. Both runs use one training seed and select their checkpoint on the same validation partition. These development results support further evaluation of SigLIP2 but do not establish performance on an independent test set or unseen regions. The accompanying code provides a common framework for repeated experiments and comparisons with additional visual encoders.

</details>


### [95] [FlowHMR: Physically Plausible Motion Capture from Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03691)
*Zhanke Wang,Chengfeng Zhao,Qing Shuai,Jingzhong Lin,Heng Li,Zeyu Ling,Yuxin Wen,Jing Li,Di Kang,Chunchao Guo,Linchao Bao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowHMR 提出了一种新的框架，通过预训练和后训练的方法，从单目视频中恢复出合理的3D 人体动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的人体动作恢复方法直接从视频中进行回归，并且由于深度的不确定性，容易导致解模糊的结果趋向于平均解。此外，恢复的动作可能并不符合物理法则，无法成功进行物理跟踪。

Method: FlowHMR 通过将视频动作捕捉问题定义为视频条件下的动作生成任务，首先预训练一个流动匹配模型。在预训练完成后，采用了 Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) 进行后训练，加入了保真度奖励和跟踪奖励来引导模型输出更符合视频特征且更易于物理追踪的动作。

Result: 实验结果表明，FlowHMR 在总体动作保真度上超过了现有的顶级方法，并且在物理跟踪的成功率上也显著提高到了 82.47%，而最强基线是 62.82%。

Conclusion: FlowHMR 提出了一种有效的从单目视频中恢复3D人体动作的方法，通过预后训练的方式显著提高了动作的保真度和物理跟踪的成功率。

Abstract: We present FlowHMR, a framework for recovering physically plausible global 3D human motion from monocular video. Previous learning-based methods typically regress human motion directly from video and train the network with geometric supervision. However, recovering human motion from monocular video is inherently ambiguous in depth, and direct regression tends to collapse toward an averaged solution. Moreover, the recovered motions are not guaranteed to be physically plausible, so physics-based tracking of them often fails. To address these challenges, we formulate video motion capture as a video-conditioned motion generation problem and first pretrain a flow matching model for this task. Given an input video, the pretrained model generates diverse motion candidates, but not all of them are faithful to the video or physically trackable. We therefore post-train the model using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with two rewards. A fidelity reward encourages consistency with the input video. A tracking reward favors motions that a physics-based controller can track successfully. Together, these rewards shift the model's output preference, so the post-trained model stays faithful to the input video while producing more physically plausible motion. We further introduce Wild-4K, a large and diverse dataset of about 4K internet videos, for evaluating human motion recovery in the wild. Qualitative and quantitative experiments on Wild-4K show that our method outperforms state-of-the-art methods in overall motion fidelity and achieves a physical tracking success rate of 82.47%, compared with 62.82% for the strongest baseline, GVHMR.

</details>


### [96] [Decoding the Functional Roles of Register and High-Norm Patch Tokens in Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03698)
*Neel Varma,Andrew Rufail,Dipika Khullar,Vasu Sharma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过训练稀疏自编码器并在DINOv2的注册标记和异常标记激活上进行分析，发现注册标记更倾向于高层次语义概念，而异常标记则更多关联于低级结构、背景和纹理。进一步的因果消融表明，破坏由注册标记衍生的功能会显著降低表示余弦相似度，而破坏由异常标记衍生的功能影响较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索自监督ViTs内部标记的功能作用，特别是理解注册标记与异常标记的区别及其对模型性能的影响。

Method: 使用自动可解释性管道、UMAP聚类和CLIP空间交叉验证训练稀疏自编码器，对注册标记和异常标记激活进行分析，通过因果消融实验研究破坏不同标记特征对模型性能的影响。

Result: 研究表明，注册标记与高层次语义概念紧密相关，而异常标记则更多关联于低级结构、背景和纹理。破坏由注册标记衍生的功能显著降低了模型表示的相似度，而破坏异常标记功能的影响较小。

Conclusion: 我们的研究结果支持了自监督ViTs中标记专业化的作用，揭示了标记在模型内部表示中的不同功能，对于理解模型行为和优化模型具有重要价值。

Abstract: Self-supervised Vision Transformers (ViTs), such as DINOv2, learn rich visual representations, but the functions of their internal tokens remain poorly understood. Recent architectures introduce dedicated register tokens to reduce high-norm out- lier patch tokens that emerge in background re- gions, yet the semantic and functional roles of both token types have not been fully established. In this paper, we analyze these roles by training sparse autoencoders (SAEs) on register-token and outlier-token activations in DINOv2. Using an automated interpretability pipeline, UMAP clus- tering, and CLIP-space cross-checks, we find that register-token features are more strongly associ- ated with high-level semantic concepts. Outlier- token features, by contrast, are more often associ- ated with lower-level structural, background, and texture-dominant patterns. Causal ablations fur- ther reveal a substantial functional asymmetry: disrupting top-activating register-derived features produces a 48.17% drop in representation cosine similarity, whereas disrupting outlier-derived fea- tures produces only a 0.31% drop. Together, our results provide evidence for token specialization in self-supervised ViTs.

</details>


### [97] [4DCodeBench: Benchmarking Agents on Inverse Graphics of Dynamic Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03715)
*Ruihong Shen,Žiga Kovačič,Peter Kulits,Xingrui Wang,Zizhang Li,Joshua B. Tenenbaum,Alan Yuille,Jieneng Chen,Jiajun Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 4DCodeBench是一个基准，用于评估代理从视频中重建动态场景的能力，并提供了一个测试平台来跟踪代理通过代码解释世界动态的进步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的静态重建能力尚未推广到复杂动态的有效重建，因此需要一个基准来测试和支持这一领域的进展。

Method: 创建了一个包含现实世界视频和合成场景的数据集，这些场景覆盖了多样的物理现象，用于评估各种前沿模型。

Result: 发现了一些前沿模型在动态重建上的不足，强调了仅依赖于静态重建信息的局限性。

Conclusion: 4DCodeBench为设计能够通过代码解释世界动态的代理提供了一个强大的测试平台和数据集，这对于进一步研究和开发具有重要意义。

Abstract: We introduce 4DCodeBench, a benchmark for 4D inverse graphics through code generation, in which agents reconstruct dynamic scenes from video as executable graphics programs. To accomplish this, agents must translate visual observations into compact representations of scene structure and dynamics, by implementing abstractions such as physical simulations to reproduce complex behavior. To evaluate this capability, we curate a set of real-world videos and construct synthetic scenes spanning diverse physical phenomena, including deformation, fluid flow, and fracture. We perform extensive benchmarking of frontier models, finding that strong static reconstruction capabilities do not yet translate into reliable reconstruction of complex dynamics. 4DCodeBench provides a testbed for tracking progress toward agents that can interpret the dynamics of the world through code. Our benchmark is available at https://github.com/4DCodeBench/4DCodeBench

</details>


### [98] [MoSE3: Learning World-Space SE(3) at Every Pixel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03716)
*Jiahuan Cheng,Zhiyi Li,Tian Xia,Ruojin Cai,Yilun Du,Qianqian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoSE3 通过预测每像素的 SE(3) 移动并使用 Art-Kubric 数据集进行训练，实现了在像素、部件和对象级别上鲁棒且准确的密集 SE(3) 估计，以及超过 3D 点跟踪的最先进的平均 3D 点跟踪准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的点跟踪方法仅捕捉像素移动的位置，不包括旋转和刚体内的像素如何一起移动。MoSE3 目的是通过预测每像素的 SE(3) 移动，提供更丰富的场景运动视图。

Method: MoSE3 通过两种中间学习的 3D 点轨迹和刚性嵌入来预测每像素的 SE(3) 移动，并通过在每个软刚性簇内差异性调整变换来进行恢复。为了弥补数据缺口，还引入了 Art-Kubric 合成数据集。

Result: MoSE3 在刚体和具有丰富物理相互作用的刚化物体基准测试中达到了最先进的 SE(3) 估计效果，并在三个数据集上实现了超过 3D 点跟踪的最高平均 3D 点跟踪准确性。

Conclusion: MoSE3 通过改进点跟踪能力，为动态场景中的密集 SE(3) 估计提供了强有力的方法，并通过合成数据集训练展示了对真实世界视频的良好泛化能力。

Abstract: Dense 3D point tracking has been a prominent paradigm for modeling motion in dynamic scenes, but a point track is just a 3-DoF translation curve per pixel: it captures where pixels go, not the rotation of the underlying part, nor which pixels move together as one body. We propose MoSE3, the first feed-forward model that predicts dense SE(3) motion from monocular RGB video, producing full 6-DoF rigid transforms at every pixel in world space. Per-pixel SE(3) motion offers a richer view of how a scene moves: rotation, translation, and grouping all at once. Directly predicting SE(3) is challenging: rotations lie on a curved manifold that is ill-suited to Euclidean regression, and annotations for SE(3) are particularly difficult to acquire. To address these challenges, MoSE3 predicts per-pixel SE(3) through two jointly learned intermediates, 3D point tracks and rigidity embeddings, and recovers SE(3) by differentiably fitting transforms within each soft rigid cluster, enabling end-to-end prediction and supervision. To close the data gap, we introduce Art-Kubric, a large-scale synthetic dataset with dense SE(3) and rigidity labels for articulated objects with rich physical interactions. MoSE3 achieves state-of-the-art SE(3) estimation at pixel, part, and object levels on both rigid and articulated benchmarks, and state-of-the-art average 3D point tracking accuracy across three datasets, while showing strong generalization to real-world videos despite being trained solely on synthetic motion data.

</details>


### [99] [Less Decoder is More Encoder: Geometric Representation Learning from Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03717)
*Keerthi Kaashyap,Dennis Anthony,Akshay Krishnan,Nhi Ngoc Nguyen,Jeremy Collins,James Hays,Shreyas Kousik,Animesh Garg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探讨了新颖视图合成(NVS)在几何表示学习中的作用。SNAP作为一种自监督编码器-解码器变换器，通过姿态条件化的局部解码器和潜空间重建目标来解决现有方法的问题，展示了在多种任务上的竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于编码器的新颖视图合成方法的表现不佳，主要原因在于解码器和像素目标设计不当。为了改善这一问题，作者提出了一种新的自监督模型SNAP，以提升多视角几何表示的迁移能力。

Method: SNAP采用了一种姿态条件化的局部解码器和潜空间重建目标，克服了传统方法中解码器表现不足和像素目标限制的问题，从而提高多视角几何表示的效益。

Result: SNAP在五项任务（视觉定位、姿势估计、点对应、深度估计、机器人操控）上表现出色，其生成的局部特征在不同的摄像机偏移条件下依然保持一致性，证明了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: SNAP能够在较低的计算和数据预算下，取得与高度监督模型相近的效果，对于多视角几何表示学习具有重要价值。

Abstract: This paper examines the role of Novel View Synthesis (NVS) in geometric representation learning. In principle, NVS should reason about 3D scene structure, thereby enabling transferable multi-view geometric representations. Yet, existing encoder-based NVS methods yield poor representations. This is not because of a lack of supervisory signal, but rather due to inconspicuous architectural choices: \textit{spatially expressive decoders} that dilute representational capabilities of the scene encoder, and \textit{low-level pixel-space targets} that hinder feature learning. We present SNAP, a self-supervised encoder-decoder transformer that addresses both through a pose-conditioned local decoder and a latent-space reconstruction objective. SNAP is task agnostic, and we show that it is competitive with special-purpose geometry-supervised methods. SNAP also performs competitively against self-supervised representations across five tasks: visual localization, pose estimation, point correspondence, depth estimation, and robot manipulation. Remarkably, SNAP's patch features exhibit emergent viewpoint invariance that approaches heavily supervised models despite lower compute and data budgets. Under camera shifts where standard 2D representations collapse, SNAP degrades more gracefully, revealing that restricting decoder expressivity actively prevents the suppression of transferable geometric structure. https://snap-nvs.github.io

</details>


### [100] [GlanceWAM: Sparse Test-Time Imagination for World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23927)
*Linhan Wang,Zijian An,Mingyuan Zhang,Chen Dai,Yi Xu,Can Cui,Jiayan Wang,Zichong Yang,Yinlin Chen,Lifeng Zhou,Chang-Tien Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GlanceWAM通过异步从关键路径中分离视觉想象，实现实时推理和更高的成功率，其控制延迟仅为同步世界-动作模型的二十分之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界-动作模型在保持实时推理和视觉想象之间的平衡上遇到了难题，GlanceWAM通过异步生成预先想象未来帧，解决了这一问题。

Method: GlanceWAM设计了一个共享的Video DiT骨干，其中异步提议者在较慢的时钟上预测未来帧，而动作头部在控制器速率下解码动作片段。

Result: 实验结果显示，GlanceWAM在24任务的RoboCasa厨房基准测试中达到72.2%的成功率，比同步的Cosmos Policy高出5.1%，在LIBERO数据集上达到99.0%的成功率。相对于同步的WAMs，GlanceWAM的每个动作片段控制延迟减少了24倍。

Conclusion: GlanceWAM通过其独特的框架解决了同步WAMs在速度和成功率之间的权衡问题，表现出优越的性能，尤其是在单手和双臂真实机器人操作中具有更高的成功率。

Abstract: Video generative models provide rich physical priors for robot learning, yet existing world-action models (WAMs) face a fundamental trade-off: synchronous video generation at control rate is latency-prohibitive, while abandoning test-time visual imagination sacrifices task success. We show that visual imagination achieves both real-time inference and superior success rates when generated asynchronously off the critical path and consumed directly in latent space. We introduce GlanceWAM, which decouples imagination from control on a single shared video DiT backbone: an asynchronous proposer glances ahead on a slow clock to imagine a single lookahead frame seconds into the future in the background, while an action head decodes action chunks at control rate (48 ms) purely in latent space without blocking. Enabled by a non-interfering attention mask that isolates video representations and staleness-robust horizon training that accommodates asynchronous lookahead aging, GlanceWAM breaks the speed-success dilemma. Trained purely on demonstrations, it attains 72.2% on the 24-task RoboCasa kitchen benchmark (vs. 67.1% for synchronous Cosmos Policy) and 99.0% on LIBERO while cutting per-chunk control latency $24\times$ relative to synchronous world-action models (48 ms on one A100). In single-arm and bimanual real-robot manipulation, it achieves higher average success than $π_{0.5}$ without any robot-data pretraining. Code is available at https://github.com/linhanwang/GlanceWAM.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [HakemBench: A Turkish Benchmark of Typed Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02293)
*Sait Furkan Teke*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HakemBench 是一个包含 2,346 项任务和 4,275 个选项的土耳其语基准测试，涵盖七个领域。模型需给出每个选项的概率，基准测试通过多种评估指标评估模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立 HakemBench 的目的是为了提供一个用于评估和比较模型在不同领域决策质量的平台，特别是在处理复杂决策任务方面。

Method: HakemBench 使用黄金标准标签与一AI 模型家族和多个大型语言模型的投票结果进行比较。基准测试包括决策质量、校准度和选择性自动化等多方面评估。

Result: 基准测试结果表明，即使在经过优化的模型上，表现也有所不同，尤其是在垃圾邮件拦截、中立性检查和客户服务等领域。报告还提供了每个模型的详细得分和排名。

Conclusion: HakemBench 作为一个开放的基准测试平台，能够帮助研究人员和开发者更好地理解和改进模型的决策过程，并为未来的研究和实验提供宝贵的参考数据。

Abstract: HakemBench is a Turkish benchmark of typed decisions, in which the model under test reads a text, a question and a fixed set of options and returns a probability for every option. Version 1.0 is released fully open under CC BY 4.0, with 2,346 items and 4,275 choice, yes/no and score questions in seven tracks (fact-check triage, education, guardrails, legal routing, moderation, spam and phishing, and customer support). One harness scores decision quality (macro F1), calibration (from the normalised Brier score) and selective automation (from the normalised area under the generalised risk-coverage curve), combines them by a geometric mean and reports intervals from 2,000 bootstrap draws; probes for option order, paraphrase, English translation and substituted names are reported alongside. Most gold labels come from blind passes of one AI model family compared with the votes of a panel of large language models from other model families; they are not human-verified. On a board of 16 rows the leader scores a composite of 0.888 and the lab's own model is 7th at 0.660. Its numbers are not blind. Earlier runs' test results shaped its training data, so its guardrail, moderation and customer support numbers are flagged; with every model scored on the other four tracks only, its composite is 0.678, 6th of 16.

</details>


### [102] [Finding the Move Is Not Winning the Game: XiangqiBench for Closed-Loop Evaluation of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02425)
*Yekun Chai,Qiwei Peng,Haoyi Xiong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 介绍了XiangqiBench基准测试，它是针对 Chinese chess中的策略执行进行评估的动态评价环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的评价方法更多关注语言模型预测正确第一步的能力，而忽略了在对手干预下持续执行策略直至获胜的能力。

Method: XiangqiBench从119个有明确结果（和棋或必胜）的棋局残局开始，让语言模型与引擎守方进行对战，通过可互动的命令行界面记录了12个前沿语言模型在两种观测协议下的8,568条多回合轨迹。

Result: 识别出了三个表明能力的信号，但在闭环执行中实际表现低于预期。具体来说，转换差距、一致性差距与模拟差距显示了很多看似有能力的行为实际上并未转化为真正的胜利。

Conclusion: 文章建议，应该调整评估标准，不仅要评估模型找到正确第一步的能力，还需要评估模型在动态环境中执行策略到最终胜利的能力，并报告执行可靠性，而不仅是覆盖范围。

Abstract: Static evaluations credit a language model for naming the right move, but an agent must carry a plan through to a verified outcome while an opponent responds. We introduce XiangqiBench, an executable benchmark that measures this difference in Chinese chess: starting from 119 tactical endgames with forced mates supported by engine or checks-only search, an LLM agent must deliver checkmate against an engine defender. An interactive REPL interface separates real moves, state queries, and forward simulation, and we record 8,568 multi-turn trajectories from 12 frontier LLMs under two observation protocols. Three signals that look like competence each overstate closed-loop success. (i) The Conversion Gap: models play the stored reference first move in 26.1\% of Sighted trials, yet only 13.9\% of these trials end in a win. (ii) The Consistency Gap: the leading model reaches 38.7\% pass@3 but only 5.9\% pass^3, winning all three trials on 7 of the 46 positions it ever wins. (iii) The Simulation Gap: 32.3\% of accepted simulation calls stop on an illegal move, and in 49.3\% of comparable cases the real defender replies differently from the line the agent simulated; self-authored rollouts check legality but cannot anticipate the opponent. Finding the move is not winning the game: agent evaluations should score closed-loop outcomes and report reliability alongside coverage.

</details>


### [103] [Counterexample Generation via Per-Theorem Symbolic Verifiers: When Imitation Hurts and Reinforcement Repairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02444)
*Omar Farouk Zouak,Houssam Eddine Boukhalfa,Soumaya Lakehal,Shiv Katiyar,Samia Nefti-Meziani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过将反例生成定义为针对定理Python验证器的约束式见证发射，展示了大型语言模型在证明正确性方面的能力有限，而通过强化学习训练则有所改善。这促进了4B模型在数学推理任务上的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究探讨了为何大型语言模型尽管能证明一个定理，但在找到与其相矛盾的反例方面却表现不佳，提出了一个解决方案以提高模型的反驳能力。

Method: 研究将反例生成问题定义为一个约束优化问题，目标是在一个执行验证器的环境中生成反例。通过监督微调和基于奖励的强化学习两种方法来训练模型。

Result: 研究发现通过监督微调（SFT）训练反例生成器会导致模型在识别证明正确性方面出现失败；然而，使用基于结果的稀疏奖励进行强化学习（RLVR）则可以显著提高模型表现，尤其是在离散奖励上的表现优于连续奖励。

Conclusion: 研究展示了生成式推理在数学领域中的应用潜力，4B模型在数学推理任务上超过了大多数同规模的开放权重语言模型，甚至在某些任务上也接近了商业API的表现。

Abstract: Large language models often solve a theorem forward yet fail to disprove a closely related false one: a falsification gap that supervised fine-tuning does not close and can actively worsen. We frame counterexample generation as constrained witness emission against a deterministic per-theorem Python verifier, and release SymCE, a corpus of 4,707 false undergraduate-algebra and real-analysis conjectures, each paired with executable verifiers. The verifier also serves as the reward function, making SymCE a training environment. Training Qwen3-4B with SFT followed by GRPO under this oracle reveals an imitation trap: counterexample-only SFT collapses true-theorem recognition from 0.27 to 0.00, while RLVR with a sparse outcome-only reward repairs this and exceeds the base, to 0.66. The collapse replicates across four seeds and on Gemma-3-4B. Sparse and dense rewards yield statistically indistinguishable in-domain success yet diverge by 33 points on a held-out calibration probe, a dissociation we trace to the partial-credit term. Our 4B model outperforms every evaluated 7B open-weights math specialist, remains competitive with six frontier commercial APIs, and transfers under unchanged prompting to GSM8K, MATH-500 and MMLU-college-math. A human audit of 177 verifier decisions finds 97.7% accuracy. Code, data, verifier modules and annotations: https://github.com/ce-rlvr/SymCE.

</details>


### [104] [FinDialogLens: Event Extraction over Multi-Party Dialogue for Missed-Trade Identification in Financial Chatrooms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02455)
*Chin-Lun Fu,Hong Ni,Behrouz Madahian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了FinDialogLens，一种结合LLM的事件抽取管道，用于从多参与者的财务对话中提取报价单及其执行结果，通过任务分割和成本意识路由实现高效准确的RFQ恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多参与财务聊天室复杂性使得手动恢复错过交易变得不可行，特别是每笔报价单（RFQ）及其最终价格和交易结果跨越多条消息出现，且与来自其他参与者的同时报价单交织。

Method: FinDialogLens采用混合LLM管道，包含紧凑的细调分类器作为推理时的辅助框架：检测RFQ触发消息及价格/交易结果元数据，RFQ级模块将事件分割为单个事件的RFQ窗口，并由交易引擎填充参数角色。

Result: 使用GPT-4o，FinDialogLens分别在最终价格和交易结果上的准确率达到92.1%和94.3%，优于全聊天室推理方法；即使是少量参数（3B）的开源细调LLM也表现出相媲美的性能，只需少量领域数据。通过成本意识路由，将较低成本规则引擎与高性能的LLM驱动交易引擎相结合，削减LLM调用85%，同时恢复一半的准确率差距。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了如何利用可扩展的LLM方法，在严格的成本预算下实现实时金融交易恢复，证明了领域知识的应用和成本优化策略是提升可扩展性的关键。

Abstract: Multi-party financial chatrooms are vital for sales-and-trading professionals, but their complexity makes manual recovery of missed trades infeasible: each Request for Quote (RFQ) is an event whose final price and trade outcome appear many messages after the RFQ-trigger message (the inquiry message), interleaved with concurrent RFQs from other participants. We cast this as event extraction (EE) over multi-party dialogue and present FinDialogLens, a hybrid LLM pipeline in which compact fine-tuned classifiers act as inference-time scaffolds: they detect RFQ-triggers and price/trade outcome metadata, an RFQ-Level Module segments per-event RFQ windows, and a Trade Engine fills argument roles. With GPT-4o, FinDialogLens reaches 92.1% and 94.3% accuracy on final price and trade outcome, respectively, outperforming full-chatroom CoT prompting methods; fine-tuned open-source LLMs with as few as 3B parameters achieve comparable performance with modest in-domain data. To make the LLM-based solution practical at scale, a difficulty-aware router balances cost and accuracy by allocating RFQs between a low-cost rule-based engine and the higher-performing LLM-powered Trade Engine, cutting LLM calls by 85% on final price while recovering half of the accuracy gap to FinDialogLens (GPT-4o), saving over $300/day at our 70,000-RFQ/day scale.

</details>


### [105] [CUEing User Simulators: Calibrated User Embeddings for Multi-Turn Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02460)
*Anjali Kantharuban,Jonas Mueller*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Calibrated User Embeddings (CUE) 提供了一种新的用户仿真实现框架，通过编码和解码观察到的会话数据来模拟真实用户的行为。与现有仿真技术相比，CUE 在 $τ^2$-Bench 上表现更好，减少了模拟误差，更真实地再现了真实用户与智能体的互动模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的基准测试主要依赖于用户模拟器来评估AI代理在多轮交互中的表现。然而，这些现有的模拟技术仅在表面上再现了人类行为和风格，而在何时以及如何仿真实际用户群体中的代理失败方面存在不足。因此，需要一种新的方法来校准现有的用户仿真模型。

Method: CUE首先收集和编码实际用户与智能体交互的真实会话，然后通过解码来生成连续的表示，以驱动语言模型（LLMs）以用户模拟的形式行动。CUE利用这种方式评估基于用户条件的过去会话回放，并通过采样新的角色来汇总指标一致性。

Result: 在$τ^2$-Bench基准测试中，CUE模型比其他基于角色的仿真方法表现更优。CUE能够显著减少模拟员属性错误，更准确地重现真实用户与智能体的互动模式，聚集成功率，并且特定任务与用户对齐的成功案例中表现出更高的准确性。即使CUE是在客户服务交互数据上训练的，它也能有效推广到文档创建，数学辅导和非正式对话等不同场景。

Conclusion: CUE为多轮交互设定新的基准，并通过不需对模型进行重新训练就能在不同领域中保持有效的优势，展示了强大的泛化能力。

Abstract: Recent benchmarks rely on user simulators to evaluate AI agents in multi-turn interaction. While existing simulation techniques demonstrate surface fidelity to human style and behavior, ecologically valid interactive benchmarking also requires alignment in when and how agents fail across simulated and real user populations. We find that existing simulators lack outcome calibration: agreement with observed success rates and failure patterns when real users interact with the same agent. We introduce Calibrated User Embeddings (CUE), a framework that both encodes observed sessions and samples continuous representations, then decodes them into persona commands to steer LLMs to act as user simulators without training. Through this, we evaluate user-conditioned replay of past sessions and aggregate metric agreement when sampling novel personas for the same tasks. On $τ^2$-Bench, CUEd simulators commit fewer simulator-attributed errors and more faithfully reproduce real-user agent failure modes, aggregate success rates, and outcomes for specific task-user pairs than other persona-based simulation methods. These gains coexist with competitive user fidelity as measured using metrics established in prior work. After being fit to mostly customer support interactions, the same CUEd simulators generalize to document creation, math tutoring, and casual conversation, and remain effective across different simulator LLMs without CUE retraining.

</details>


### [106] [APDMem: Agent-Controlled Progressive Disclosure for Query-Adaptive Long-Term Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02472)
*Chin-Lun Fu,Anagha Kulkarni,Hong Ni,Behrouz Madahian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: APDMem通过层次化的记忆架构和渐进式披露机制，实现从长对话历史中高效恢复稀疏证据，尤其适用于复杂查询。实验结果显示其在长上下文记忆推理上表现优异，仅访问了8%的对话。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM助手在处理复杂查询时需要从长的对话历史中恢复详细的证据，这增加了查询时间和资源消耗。APDMem寻求通过一种新颖的层次化记忆架构来优化这一过程。

Method: APDMem通过将对话历史分为四个层次：主题总结、个性化关键事实、回合级证据笔记和原始消息，采用渐进式披露机制从这些层次中检索证据。控制器根据查询的复杂程度决定检索哪些层次，从而在成本和精确度之间找到平衡。

Result: 实验结果显示，APDMem在LongMemEval上表现出色，能够完成复杂的长上下文推理任务同时仅访问了总对话的8%。

Conclusion: APDMem证明了在保持高度精确度的同时，可以显著降低对长对话历史的需求，是一种有效的解决个性化LLM助手在复杂查询处理上挑战的方法。

Abstract: Personalized LLM assistants must recover sparse evidence from long conversation histories across queries of varying complexity. We introduce APDMem (Agent-controlled Progressive Disclosure Memory), a hierarchical long-term memory architecture that applies progressive disclosure to memory retrieval. Rather than relying on a flat memory store or fixed retrieval granularity, APDMem represents conversation history as four progressively detailed layers: thematic summaries, personalized key facts, turn-level evidence notes, and raw messages. At inference time, a controller applies progressive disclosure to the memory hierarchy: it first reads high-level summaries and drills into finer evidence only when needed. This creates an adaptive cost-fidelity trade-off: simple queries can terminate early, while complex temporal, multi-hop, or exact-evidence queries trigger deeper inspection. A note synthesizer converts retrieved evidence into a query-focused structure that consolidates facts, orders events, and flags contradictions before final answer generation. Experiments on LongMemEval show that APDMem achieves strong performance for long-context memory reasoning while accessing only 8% of the total conversations.

</details>


### [107] [From Retrieval to Typed Decisions: Calibrated System One Models from Biomedical Sentence Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02486)
*Pritam Deka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究使用预训练的生物医学句子编码器构建决策模型，并通过检索训练和微调来改进模型性能。PFR模型在某些情况下受益于检索训练，而C模型在所有目标下均优于PFR。公开的奖励调整方法在某些指标上表现较差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索预训练的生物医学句子编码器作为构建决策模型起点的有效性，并通过检索训练和微调来优化模型性能。

Method: 将Sentence-Transformers编码器转换为双编码器、跨头和先验融合残差决策模型。通过一个名为BIODECIDE的生物医学决策套件对这些模型进行训练，并针对MEDLINE-S1数据集中的243k训练决策进行评估。

Result: 检索训练提高了零样本匹配效果；在某些情况下，PFR模型受益于检索训练，C模型则在所有目标下均表现更优；公开的RLCD方法在某些指标上表现较差。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，双编码器与先验融合残差决策模型有各自的优缺点，未来研究可以优化这些模型或探索新的架构。

Abstract: Typed decision models answer schema-constrained questions about a text in one forward pass and return probabilities meant to be thresholded. We ask whether biomedical sentence encoders trained for retrieval are good starting points for such models. We present SBERT2S1, which converts Sentence-Transformers encoders into bi-encoder, cross-head (C) and prior-fused residual (PFR) decision models, together with BIODECIDE, a biomedical typed-decision suite, and MEDLINE-S1, 243k training decisions derived from NLM indexing. Across six parent-retriever pairs, retrieval training improves zero-shot matching of content-bearing options. After fine-tuning, its effect depends on the head: across five pairs and three training-set sizes, retrieval training significantly helps PFR, which keeps the retrieval prior, in 10 of 15 comparisons, but helps C in one and hurts it in five. A matched grid of two heads and five training objectives shows that C outperforms PFR under every objective, and that the released RLCD recipe of open System One models trails cross-entropy by 2.5-3.0 points. The deficit stems mainly from its reward normalisation, which inflates the noisy score-function term 3.6-15-fold; an unbiased leave-one-out estimator recovers most of the gap. After temperature scaling, no objective is clearly better calibrated than cross-entropy. We release the code, the MEDLINE-S1 labels and a model.

</details>


### [108] [A generative-informed neuro-symbolic framework for syntactic ambiguity resolution: Evidence from Arabic DPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02529)
*Mohammed Damom,Muneef Y. Alshawsh,Ashraf A. Naji,Mustafa Ali Alhamzi,Fawwaz An-Nashef,Jameel Ahmed Elayah,Mohammed Q. Shormani,Noman AL-Sayadi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种生成驱动的神经符号框架来解决现代标准阿拉伯语（MSA）定语从句的结构性歧义问题，模型在未见过的测试集上达到了较高的准确率和F1分数，但不同类别的表现存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿拉伯语在形态学上极为丰富，造就了大量的句法歧义，使得句法结构分析成为一个持久性的挑战。

Method: 研究提出了一种将生成的句法规则与AraBERT结合的框架，利用逻辑驱动的方法将歧义表示为候选集决策任务，使用候选条件化输入表示语言学动机的替代方案，并进行显式构建和评估。

Result: 模型在未见过的测试集上取得了96.88%的准确率、95.92%的宏F1、96.83%的加权F1以及93.94%的二元F1。具体类别层面的分析显示了不同的表现，N1类别中高/VP连接的召回率为99.71%，而N2类别中低/NP/嵌入连接的召回率仅为89.26%。

Conclusion: 研究得出结论，形式化的句法表示可以在基于Transformer的语言处理中实现为语言结构和语境神经建模间的显式接口，并提供了一个受控且可解释的句法歧义解决方法，不仅限于阿拉伯语。

Abstract: Syntactic ambiguity poses a persistent challenge for Arabic NLP, particularly in morphologically rich nominal constructions where multiple structu6ral interpretations may be compatible with the same surface sequence. This study proposes a generatively informed neuro-symbolic framework for resolving structural ambiguity in Modern Standard Arabic (MSA) DPs. The framework integrates generative syntactic notions with AraBERT by representing ambiguity as a candidate-based decision task in which linguistically motivated alternatives are explicitly constructed and evaluated through candidate-conditioned input representations. Findings indicate that the model achieved 96.88% accuracy, 95.92% macro-F1, 96.83% weighted F1, and 93.94% binary F1 on the unseen evaluation set. Class-level analysis revealed asymmetric performance, with recall of 99.71% for High/VP Attachment (N1) and 89.26% for Low/NP/Embedded Attachment (N2), indicating greater difficulty in recovering the embedded interpretation. The study concludes that formal syntactic representations can be operationalized within Transformer-based NLP as an explicit interface between linguistic structure and contextual neural modeling, providing a controlled and interpretable approach to Arabic syntactic ambiguity resolution and beyond.

</details>


### [109] [Evaluating Multi-Dimensional Generalization of Large Language Models in Temporal Extraction Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02549)
*Fahmid Shahriar Iqbal,Ritam Dutt,Soumitra Das,Arnav Verma,Sagnik Ray Choudhury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过多模型配置在四个维度上评估了时间与事件表达抽取任务中的泛化能力，发现基础任务表现强烈的一般预测能力较好，但在大规模分布迁移中减弱。诱导性提示方法在不同领域迁移、对抗性扰动、组合性和长度增加方面最为一致，而关于规模、架构和演绎/归纳提示策略的效果则在各维度上差异明显。文章强调，单个维度上的泛化能力不足以预测LLM在时间提取任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评价方法主要关注领域内性能，提供有限的对外部领域泛化能力的洞察。本文通过系统评估多种模型配置的迁移能力，旨在揭示LLM在时间与事件表达抽取任务中的泛化规律，尤其是面对领域变化和外部环境扰动时的表现。
