On held-out subjects, the model reaches an overall MAE of 25.96 / 22.80 across two evaluation batches (valence 21.94 / 19.6, arousal 29.98 / 26.0), well below EEGNet and ASAC-Net baselines reported for the same subject-independent split (raw scale score 60.6 and 55.0 respectively). We further report a systematic negative-result search across model architectures, feature representations, and prediction targets that found no signal able to improve on the single alpha-synchrony marker.

</details>


### [216] [BitNest: Bit-Nested Speculative Decoding for Memory-Efficient LLM Inference Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02800)
*Chence Yang,Ningxi Cheng,Arash Akbari,Qitao Tan,Qingchan Zhu,Ci Zhang,Changdi Yang,Yanzhi Wang,Wei Niu,Jinhui Wang,Jin Lu,Geng Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BitNest是一种嵌入低精度草稿的比特嵌套推测解码框架，能够同时提高资源利用效率和解码速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推测解码方法在移动设备等资源受限的设备上存在内存使用过高的问题，而BitNest通过将低精度草稿直接嵌入高精度目标表示中，解决了资源利用率和解码速度之间的权衡。

Method: BitNest框架首先构建一个强的低精度基础，然后通过残差细化来恢复更高精度的目标。此外，它还扩展了渐进精度设计到KV缓存以支持长上下文推断。

Result: 在多种7B-8B边缘友好型语言模型和不同工作负载上，BitNest的推测接受率达到95.2%，接近保留高精度模型的质量，并提供了1.48-1.61倍的端到端速度增益。

Conclusion: BitNest框架成功实现了在保持高精度模型质量的同时，显著提高推测解码的速度和效率。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive generation by using a lightweight draft to propose multiple tokens for parallel verification. However, existing methods often require an additional draft model or weight representation, introducing non-negligible memory overhead on resource-constrained devices. Self-speculative approaches reduce this overhead, yet still face trade-offs between draft quality, target quality, and storage efficiency. We propose BitNest, a bit-nested speculative decoding framework that embeds a low-precision draft directly into the higher-precision target representation. Instead of deriving a draft from a predefined target, BitNest first constructs a strong low-precision base and then recovers the higher-precision target through residual refinement, enabling both models to share a single physical weight representation. BitNest further extends this progressive-precision design to the KV cache for long-context inference. Across multiple 7B--8B edge-friendly LLMs and diverse workloads, BitNest achieves an average speculative acceptance rate of 95.2% while closely preserving higher-precision model quality, and delivers 1.48--1.61x end-to-end speedup over FP16 autoregressive decoding. On the LLaMA models supported by all representative self-speculative baselines, BitNest also achieves consistently competitive or higher decoding speedup.

</details>


### [217] [VIGOR: Zero-Shot Visual Generalization via Latent-Space Consistency in Model-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02801)
*Mingyu Park,Samyeul Noh,Hyun Myung,Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VIGOR 是一种增强模型在未知视觉干扰下的泛化能力的方法，通过整合三种互补的组件来提高模型的鲁棒性和样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的 MBRL 在面对未见过的视觉干扰时表现较差，且相较于其他方法在样本效率上没有显著优势，本文提出的 VIGOR 框架旨在解决这一问题。

Method: VIGOR 通过集成不对称弱强增强、动态一致性、编码器稳定性三种组件，确保模型在进行潜空间回归和动作预测时具有鲁棒性。具体来说，它使用弱增强和弱强增强视图的混合增强，强制预测在增强不变的情况下保持一致，同时防止编码器在交叉增强监督下漂移。

Result: 在 DeepMind 控制套件 (DMC) 和 Robosuite 上，VIGOR 高于最先进的模型自由和模型基础基线，显著改善了测试集的表现。

Conclusion: VIGOR 证明了在未见过的视觉干扰下，潜空间一致性的鲁棒性不受增强策略的影响，是驱动模型泛化的核心因素。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) achieves strong sample efficiency by planning within learned latent dynamics, yet its performance degrades substantially under unseen visual distractions such as background variations, lighting changes, or camera shifts. Unlike model-free RL, where encoder perturbations affect only single-step predictions, MBRL suffers from a two-level vulnerability: visual distractions first push encoder outputs out of distribution, and these errors then compound through recursive latent rollouts over the planning horizon. We propose visual generalization via latent-space consistency in model-based RL (VIGOR), a framework that enables zero-shot generalization to unseen visual distractions while retaining the sample efficiency of its MBRL backbone. VIGOR integrates three interdependent components: (i) asymmetric weak-to-strong augmentation, which pairs weak-only and weak-to-strong latent views within a single batch; (ii) dynamics-level consistency, which enforces augmentation-invariant transition predictions through direct latent regression; and (iii) encoder-level stabilization, which prevents encoder drift under the cross-augmentation supervision imposed by dynamics-level consistency. Evaluations on the DeepMind Control Suite (DMC) and Robosuite show that VIGOR outperforms state-of-the-art model-free and model-based baselines, surpassing the second-best baseline by 3.4% on DMC and 43.6% on Robosuite. Ablations further show that VIGOR's robustness is augmentation-agnostic: replacing the default augmentation with alternatives from distinct perturbation families preserves strong generalization, confirming that latent-space consistency, not the augmentation choice, drives robustness.

</details>


### [218] [ROUTEAUDIT: Interaction-Aware Identification for Budgeted Multi-Verifier Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02808)
*Miaobo Hu,Shuhao Hu,Xiaobo Guo,Xin Wang,Bokun Wang,Tianshu Fu,Daren Zha,Jun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究将验证人员路由问题重新定义为合同条件下的身份验证问题，并提出了新的衡量方法和评估协议来改善多验证系统中的验证人员分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前，多验证系统的评估主要依赖于端点质量-成本差距，但这忽略了随政策变化而变化的各种因素，如验证器目录、可用性、会计、信息过滤和评分器。因此，作者试图改进验证策略的评估方法。

Method: 作者首先定义了验证人员路由的合同条件，包括请求支持、验证器目录、实现的可用性、资源计费、在线过滤和事后评分记录。然后，作者引入了三个可测量对象，即合同格、政策独立响应带和请求级边界。最后，研究通过协议承诺付费观察和账本事件，进行比较后返回每个比较的属性证书。

Result: 在两个保留的尾部缓存数据集上，匹配的静态SF+SA等同于级联，分配给验证人员集边缘的显要增益为0.1797和0.1250。在1319个保留的任务请求上，学习和RLVR研究报告的质量分别为0.9522和0.9553，匹配的静态值为0.9484。RLVR静态配对差为+0.0068，请求配对区间为[0.0015,0.0122]，训练种子按请求分层区间为[0.0006,0.0131]。控制着归属恢复得到平均绝对误差0.0011和端点重建误差0.0004。因子、桥梁顺序和随机提供方研究评估了证书接口；RLVR在相同的识别合同下提供了学习策略的压力测试。

Conclusion: 研究证明了新的方法和评估协议的有效性，改善了证明人分配的策略评估过程。

Abstract: Adaptive multi-verifier systems are commonly compared through endpoint quality-cost gaps, even when the verifier catalog, availability, accounting, information filtration, or scorer changes with the policy. We formulate verifier routing as a contract-conditioned identification problem. The contract records request support, verifier catalog, realized availability, resource accounting, online filtration, and post-trace scoring; a matched route contrast changes only the policy coordinate. ROUTEAUDIT adds three measurable objects to this contract. A contract lattice averages coordinate increments over every admissible bridge order and reports the resulting attribution together with its path sensitivity. A policy-independent response tape identifies paired sequential contrasts when adaptive policies reveal different observations. For incomplete matching, request-level bounds use whichever potential outcome remains observed and give a sharp finite-population interval. The protocol commits paid observations and ledger events before the oracle join and returns an attribution certificate for each comparison. On two held-out raw-tail caches, matched static SF+SA equals the cascade, assigning the apparent gains of 0.1797 and 0.1250 over full static to the verifier-set edge. On 1,319 held-out task requests, the learned and RLVR studies report quality 0.9522 and 0.9553 versus 0.9484 for matched static; the RLVR-static paired difference is +0.0068 with a request-paired interval $[0.0015,0.0122]$ and a training-seed-by-request hierarchical interval $[0.0006,0.0131]$. Controlled attribution recovery yields route mean absolute error 0.0011 and endpoint reconstruction error 0.0004. Factorial, bridge-order, and stochastic-provider studies evaluate the certificate interface; RLVR supplies a learned-policy stress test under the same identification contract.

</details>


### [219] [iS-KV: Online Low-Rank KV Cache Compression via Block-Incremental SVD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02815)
*Yiren Zhao,Guanghui Song,Tianrui Qin,Kejiang Ye,Cheng-zhong Xu,Xitong Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: iS-KV 是一种在线低秩 KV 缓存压缩方法，适用于长时间推理。它通过保持近期窗口准确，并增量地将较旧的状态折叠到有界秩表示中来工作。在 DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B 和 Qwen3-8B 上，iS-KV 实现了与原始模型相近的准确率，同时显著减少了缓存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过不可逆删除历史状态来控制 KV 缓存的增长，但这种删除可能会影响后续推理的准确性。为此，研究提出了 iS-KV 方法以支持在线低秩 KV 缓存压缩。

Method: iS-KV 通过实时更新低秩基底并同步历史坐标来维护表示一致性，从而避免历史状态随时间漂移的问题。

Result: 在 DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B 上，iS-KV 达到了 82.6% 的准确率，压缩比为 4.06 倍，与原始模型相当。在 Qwen3-8B 上，iS-KV 达到了 89.2% 的准确率，压缩比为 5.64 倍。

Conclusion: iS-KV 能有效地将 KV 缓存压缩比提高数倍，同时保持与原始模型相近的准确率，尤其在匹配内存预算的条件下性能超过基于令牌删除的基线方法。

Abstract: Long chain-of-thought reasoning substantially increases KV-cache memory during autoregressive decoding, as every generated token introduces new key and value states and causes the cache to grow linearly with decoding length. Existing KV-cache compression methods typically control this growth through token eviction, but irreversible deletion can remove historical states that later reasoning may need to revisit. SVD-based low-rank compression provides an alternative by retaining all positions with a more compact representation. However, extending it from a fixed prompt cache to online decoding is non-trivial. Through our investigation, we find that if the basis is updated for new tokens while old tokens keep their coordinates in the old basis, the stored history drifts substantially. Based on this observation, we propose iS-KV, an online low-rank KV-cache compression method for long-horizon reasoning. iS-KV keeps a recent window exact while incrementally folding older states into bounded-rank representations. As the low-rank basis evolves, it synchronizes historical coordinates with the updated basis to maintain representation consistency. On DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B, iS-KV achieves 82.6% accuracy at 4.06-fold persistent-KV compression, close to the original model's 83.6%. On Qwen3-8B, it achieves 89.2% accuracy at 5.64-fold compression. Under matched memory budgets, iS-KV consistently outperforms token-eviction baselines.

</details>


### [220] [MetaRubric: Learning to Reward for Rubric-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02824)
*Yuxuan Fan,Jaehong Yoon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MetaRubric通过交替进行基于证据的策略优化与响应引导的评判标准适应，解决判题中的虚构评分问题，从而提高诸如PubMedQA等医疗相关任务的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决判题中的虚构评分问题（即即使缺少关键信息仍给予高评分），以避免这种不准确评分影响任务的优化结果。

Method: 引入MetaRubric机制，该机制结合证据感知的策略优化与响应引导的评判标准自适应调整。

Result: MetaRubric在多个医疗相关基准上相比静态评判的判题方式（GRPO）提高了6.00至20.40个百分点的准确性，其中在某些基准上取得了进一步提高。

Conclusion: MetaRubric方法通过自适应调整评判标准和策略优化，有效克服了虚构评分问题，显著提升了任务性能。

Abstract: Rubric-based reinforcement learning extends reward-driven optimization to open-ended tasks by assigning partial credit to individual response requirements. However, rubric judges can assign a high criterion score even when the information or action it requires is absent from the response, a failure mode we term Vacuous Credit. Such awards persist after the required information is removed and can reverse the sign of a response's GRPO advantage. To address this problem, we introduce MetaRubric, which alternates evidence-aware policy optimization with response-guided rubric adaptation. We construct counterfactual counterparts by changing one task-relevant fact in each prompt. During policy optimization, credit is assigned only when the response contains sufficient evidence to satisfy the required rubric criterion. After each policy-optimization stage, current policy responses guide revisions to original and counterfactual criteria while preserving the meaning of the original prompt's initial rubric as interpreted under each prompt's facts. We also adapt criterion weights at stage boundaries to better address observed policy errors. Across multiple backbones, MetaRubric improves PubMedQA accuracy by 6.00--20.40 percentage points over static-judge GRPO, with further gains on HealthBench-Hard and two multimodal medical benchmarks.

</details>


### [221] [Scaling Trajectories for Complex Tasks through Recursive Self-Rewrite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02826)
*Zongxia Li,Yucheng Shi,Zhongzhi Li,Junyao Yang,Ruhan Wang,Chengsong Huang,Fuxiao Liu,Haitao Mi,Jordan Boyd-Graber,LeoweiLiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为递归自我重写(RSR)的框架，利用单一基模型Qwen-3.8-27B，通过不同屏蔽下的成功轨迹发现与重构，以增强模型性能。实验结果显示，使用RSR方法后，无论是任务完成率还是长期任务表现，模型都取得了显著提升，支持在多任务下的广泛应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法依赖特定屏蔽下的成功实例来辅助模型改进，但在实际部署中可能会遇到屏蔽不可用的情况。RSR框架旨在通过集中处理和重组来自不同屏蔽的成功轨迹，使模型能够更好地泛化到未见过的屏蔽下。

Method: RSR框架包含规划者、批评者和执行者三个组件。规划者将过程分解为可执行的规范，批评者检查并指导迭代修订以消除验证者泄露和解决方案泄露，执行者则根据合格的规范在新的沙盒中执行。通过联合三个屏蔽，RSR探索出更多的成功轨迹，用于监督微调。

Result: 在大约3000个自定义终端任务中，三种屏蔽组合解决了759个任务，比单一最强屏蔽解决了的任务多了34.3%。监督微调后，模型的性能显著提高：在Terminal-Bench 2上pass@3从57.0%提升到74.2%，在Terminal-Bench 4上从1.5%提升到9.1%，在自定义的Terminal-Bench Hard上从39.0%提升到63.0%，在Software Terminal-Bench上从3.0%提升到6.0%。长期任务的表现也从0.21提升到0.29。

Conclusion: 研究证明，通过RSR框架利用多样化屏蔽下的经验，能够构建出可重用的模型能力，提高模型在不同屏蔽下的性能，展示了这种方法在多任务场景下的潜力。

Abstract: Successful trajectories on difficult tasks provide valuable supervision for model improvement, but specialized harnesses introduce interventions that may be unavailable during deployment. We propose Recursive Self-Rewrite (RSR), a framework that uses one base model, Qwen-3.8-27B, to discover successful solutions under diverse harnesses and reconstruct them as training trajectories under a general harness. A planner extracts procedures into runbooks, a critic screens for verifier and solution leakage and guides recursive revision, and an executor follows qualified runbooks in fresh sandboxes. Across approximately 3K self-curated terminal tasks, three harnesses jointly solve 759 tasks, 34.3% more than the strongest individual harness in the recorded pool. RSR expands 2,001 successful source trajectories into 11,094 rewritten trajectories for supervised finetuning. Training on these trajectories outperforms both the base model and direct trajectory SFT. Compared with the base model, pass@3 increases from 57.0% to 74.2% on Terminal-Bench 2, from 1.5% to 9.1% on Terminal-Bench 4, from 39.0% to 63.0% on our self-curated Terminal-Bench Hard, and from 3.0% to 6.0% on our Software Terminal-Bench. Process reward on Long-Horizon Terminal-Bench rises from 0.21 to 0.29. These results show how diverse harness-assisted experiences can be reconstructed into reusable capabilities for a model operating under a general harness.

</details>


### [222] [MLCommons Jailbreak Benchmark v1.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02827)
*Carsten Maple,Cagatay Yucel,Isaac Holeman,Chris Knotz,Peter Mattson,James Goel,Jonathan Petit,Sean McGregor,James Ezick,Abhishek Kumar,Alicia Parrish,Murali Emani,Kashyap Iyer,Faiza Khan Khattak,Washington Mbonu,Daniel Machlab,Eileen Long,Shaona Ghosh,Jibin Varghese,Roman Lutz,Andrew Gruen,Bennett Hillenbrand,Prabal Gupta,Mohammed Serrhini,Dhivya Nagasubramanian,Aakash Gupta,Jun,Lu,Kurt Bollacker,Chang Liu,Jonathan Petit,Cong Chen,Jean-Philippe Monteuuis,Brent Miller,Apurv Verma,Roman Eng,Armstrong Foundjem,Mohammed Serrhini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本基准测试1.0版提供了一种端到端的方法来评估大型语言模型对文本式监狱突破攻击的鲁棒性，通过综合多种评估标准和人工注释的评价，发现模型在面对监狱突破攻击时的安全性能表现有所下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI系统设计为拒绝危险请求，但可能存在被‘囚犯逃脱’攻击绕过的情况。因此，需要建立一个评估标准来检测这些系统的安全性和鲁棒性。

Method: 该基准测试结合了系统和攻击选择的驱动标准，基线和对抗性评估，人工注释，自动评估者校准，评分，评分和风险调整披露。它使用AIAgilitate评估标准v1.4进行响应评估，并通过衡量基线条件和敌对条件下的安全性能变化来测量鲁棒性。

Result: 所有评估系统的不安全响应率从基线条件下的11.08%增加到监狱突破条件下的18.65%，平均风险差距为7.57%。可访问系统显示出更大的均值差距，而不同攻击类别和危害下的攻击效果差异显著。

Conclusion: 该基准测试为后续跨系统、攻击、危害和评估方法的对比研究奠定了可重复的方法论基础。

Abstract: Modern AI systems are designed to refuse hazardous requests. A jailbreak is a prompt crafted to bypass those safeguards and elicit outputs that the system would normally refuse to provide. The MLCommons Jailbreak Benchmark v1.0 provides an end-to-end methodology for evaluating the robustness of large language models to single-turn, text-based jailbreak attacks. It combines criteria-driven system and attack selection, paired baseline and adversarial evaluation, human annotation, automated evaluator calibration, scoring, grading, and risk-calibrated disclosure within a single benchmarking pipeline. The benchmark evaluates eight open-weight systems using 264 seed prompts spanning eleven hazard categories and representative attacks drawn from the MLCommons Jailbreak Taxonomy. Responses are assessed using the AILuminate Assessment Standard v1.4, and robustness is measured through the Resilience Gap: the change in safety performance between baseline and adversarial conditions. Across all evaluated systems and attacks, the unsafe-response rate increased from 11.08% under baseline conditions to 18.65% under jailbreak conditions, producing an average Resilience Gap of 7.57%. Accessible systems showed a larger mean gap, while attack effectiveness varied substantially across attack categories and hazards. The benchmark also examines evaluator reliability and sources of measurement error. Beyond reporting results, Jailbreak Benchmark v1.0 establishes a reproducible methodological foundation for comparative jailbreak evaluation and for future expansion across systems, attacks, hazards, and evaluation methods.

</details>


### [223] [FSPO: Policy-Consistent Risk and Pareto-Feasible Control for Budgeted LLM RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02828)
*Miaobo Hu,Shuhao Hu,Xiaobo Guo,Xin Wang,Bokun Wang,Daren Zha,Jun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FSPO通过引入风险到终点模型和长期效用模型，解决多训练调节后的残余问题；DCTC校准针对临时控制器选择的动作轨迹的风险；PRCC确保资源分配的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的训练后调整方法存在一些未解决的问题，包括未来风险模型无需估计部署控制器的风险，评分可能因选择性动作而失衡，以及资源成本分配可能不可行。

Method: FSPO通过结合学习一致的风险到终点模型、长期效用模型、决策条件轨迹校准和帕累托资源继续证书，解决了上述问题。

Result: FSPO在各种评估指标上超过了现有的适应性基线PB2，特别是在部分领域和前所未见数据上的准确性有显著提升。此外，结合所有三种组件，FSPO能够显著降低决策后的ECE并消除虚假可分配的案例。

Conclusion: FSPO提出了一种新的反馈状态控制器，通过缓解上述问题，改进了训练后调整的性能，特别是在面对高行为部署差异时，提高了决策的有效性和准确性。

Abstract: Adaptive LLM reinforcement-learning post-training changes multiple training actuators online, including rollout temperature, group size, clipping, KL regularization, verifier allocation, and update budget. Three coupled issues remain unresolved. A future-risk model trained from behavior trajectories need not estimate the risk induced by the controller that will be deployed; a score calibrated on logged state-action pairs can become miscalibrated after selective action choice; and independent per-resource minimum costs do not in general certify a feasible multi-resource continuation. We introduce FSPO, a feedback-state controller for budgeted LLM RL post-training that addresses these issues jointly. FSPO learns a policy-consistent risk-to-go model whose Bellman target follows the same frozen controller used for future decisions, together with a long-horizon utility model. Decision-conditioned trajectory calibration (DCTC) calibrates risk on cross-fitted trajectories generated by actions selected by provisional controllers. A Pareto resource continuation certificate (PRCC) admits an action only when a non-dominated cumulative reservation remains feasible over the residual horizon. Under a matched GRPO resource envelope, FSPO reaches 66.11% held-out and 59.43% OOD accuracy, compared with 64.47% and 57.03% for PB2, the strongest evaluated adaptive baseline. Three paired training seeds give gains of +2.42 and +3.19 percentage points over the contextual bandit on held-out and OOD evaluation. Under high behavior-deployment mismatch, policy-consistent risk lowers selected-decision ECE from 0.108 to 0.053; DCTC lowers it from 0.039 to 0.022 at matched acceptance; PRCC removes false-feasible admissions on an 18-action catalog ($0.197\rightarrow0.000$); and enabling all three components reduces trajectory failure from 0.181 to 0.083 in a factorial ablation.

</details>


### [224] [AMBER: Multi-View Adaptive Budget Allocation for Listwise Vision-Language Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02831)
*Wenteng Chen,Jiachen Zhu,Rong Shan,Tianyi Xu,Yuxiang Chen,Congmin Zheng,Teng Wang,Junjie Wu,Weiwen Liu,Changwang Zhang,Weinan Zhang,Jun Wang,Jianghao Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AMBER 提出了一种新的在线预算多视图重排序框架，通过将 VLM 输出视为局部锦标赛并使用连续的 Elo 更新维护轻量级全局排名状态，动态分配计算资源，从而在较低预算设置下仍能实现最强的整体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 VLM 重排序方法依赖于固定的时间表，导致在不关键的候选对和简单查询上浪费昂贵的 VLM 调用。为此，AMBER 提出了一个动态优化全局资源分配的在线预算多视图重排序框架，以改进性能并简化多视图策略。

Method: AMBER 利用连续的 Elo 更新将 VLM 生成的碎片化全局重排序表示视为多个局部锦标赛。在计算分配方面，AMBER 采用两层策略：根据得分不明确性动态构建候选视图，并通过最大化预期信息增益来调度查询。

Result: AMBER 在 CIRR、CIRCO 和 PhotoBench 上的表现优于其他多呼叫 VLM 重排序方法，特别是在资源预算较低的设置下仍然有效。

Conclusion: 研究证明了 AMBER 的有效性，强调在多呼叫 VLM 重排框架中的动态资源分配策略的重要性，提供了一种改进的方法来优化高效多视图处理。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are powerful listwise rerankers for multimodal retrieval, but high inference costs restrict them to evaluating small local candidate views. Existing multi-call strategies rely on fixed schedules, wasting expensive VLM calls on uninformative candidate pairs and easy queries. To address this, we propose Adaptive Multi-view Budgeted Elo Reranking (AMBER), an online, budgeted multi-view reranking framework that dynamically optimizes global resource allocation. AMBER treats fragmented listwise VLM outputs as local tournaments, using continuous Elo updates to maintain a lightweight global ranking state. Building on this, it allocates computation at two levels: dynamically constructing candidate views with high score ambiguity, and scheduling queries to maximize expected information gain. We show that each Elo update corresponds to a stochastic gradient ascent step on the Bradley-Terry log-likelihood, and provide a submodular information-theoretic motivation for the query-level allocation strategy. Experiments on CIRR, CIRCO, and PhotoBench demonstrate that AMBER achieves the strongest overall performance among the compared multi-call VLM reranking methods under comparable VLM-call budgets, while remaining effective in lower-budget settings. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/wnlfc/AMBER.

</details>


### [225] [DNAlign: Dynamic Null-Space Safe Alignment for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02844)
*Jisheng Dang,Yushuo Zhao,Dewei Liu,Junfeng Fang,Bimei Wang,Tiantian Rao,Hong Peng,Bin Hu,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DNAlign 提出了一种结合控制理论优化和零空间投影的轻量级安全对齐框架，可以减少有害输出并保持语言模型的流畅性、连贯性和事实准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的安全对齐方法要么提高计算成本，要么无意中破坏模型的核心知识，导致在执行简单任务时的流畅性和准确性下降。为了解决这一问题，本文提出 DNAlign 框架。

Method: DNAlign 框架通过将大型语言模型视为动态系统引入可控扰动，引导生成行为偏向安全行为。其关键组件包括限制扰动的投影模块以及根据人类偏好数据训练的价值函数，用于自适应优化控制信号以与人类安全偏好对齐。

Result: 实验结果表明，DNAlign 在多个大规模语言模型的基础之上，能够有效降低有害输出，同时保持流畅性、连贯性和事实准确性，并且相比之前的安全对齐基线方法，不牺牲生成的多样性。

Conclusion: DNAlign 框架为确保大型语言模型的安全部署提供了一个有效且可部署的解决方案。

Abstract: Ensuring the safe and reliable deployment of large language models (LLMs) remains a fundamental challenge. Existing safety alignment approaches either incur high computational cost or unintentionally disrupt the model's core knowledge, leading to degraded fluency and factual accuracy on benign tasks. This reveals a persistent trade-off between safety and utility. We propose DNAlign, a lightweight alignment framework that integrates control-theoretic optimization with null-space projection. By treating the LLM as a dynamic system, the proposed framework introduces controllable perturbations to steer generation toward safe behavior. A key component is the projection module, which restricts these perturbations to the harmful-related subspace derived from neutral hidden states, thereby preserving general knowledge and response quality. A value function trained on human preference data adaptively optimizes the control signals to align with human safety preferences. Extensive evaluations across multiple LLM backbones demonstrate that our framework consistently reduces harmful outputs while maintaining fluency, coherence, and factual utility. It achieves superior overall performance compared to prior alignment baselines without sacrificing generation diversity. These results indicate that the proposed framework provides an effective and practically deployable solution for safe LLM alignment. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/DNAlign.

</details>


### [226] [Bounded Reachability & Jailbreak Detection via Contraction-Constrained State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02853)
*Omanshu Thapliyal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于状态空间模型的安全头部，通过收缩条件能够实现线性时间不变分类器的确界传播认证，并在有毒评论检测和监狱破解检测中展示了其有效性和实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的安全头部研究主要集中在其实用检测性能上，但对其形式上的鲁棒性研究较少。论文旨在探究在何种条件下基于状态空间模型的安全头部能够被认证为生成一致预测，以弥补这一空白。

Method: 该研究首先定义了收缩条件 ¡∞ ̅</i></sub>(A)<1。通过这一条件，论文提供了线性时间不变分类器的确界传播认证方法。此外，论文通过设置带有剪刀罚则的状态空间模型来保证收缩条件的满足，并通过实验验证了这一方法的有效性。

Result: 研究结果表明，当收缩条件满足时，输出区间具有有界的稳态宽度并且可以对分类结果进行认证；而当收缩条件不满足时，则无法在任何实际的扰动半径内进行认证。在有毒评论数据集上，认证比例从41%提高到了59%。进一步应用收缩正则化S4头部进行犯罪破解检测时，实现了无监督的转移学习，并在AdvBench和HarmBench上取得了优异的结果。此外，线性回归在平均池化后Mamba-130M嵌入上的表现与S4头部相当或更好，表明有害意图已在嵌入空间中线性可分。

Conclusion: 论文的研究成果在于提供了一种基于收缩条件实现状态空间模型安全头部的确界传播认证方法，并证明了这种认证的可行性。这意味着S4安全性头部的一个重要贡献是其能够提供不依赖于探针的方法来进行形式化认证。

Abstract: Safety heads are lightweight classifiers attached to pretrained language models for flagging harmful inputs before generation. Their empirical detection performance has been studied, but their formal robustness properties remain largely unexplored. We ask when a State Space Model (SSM)-based safety head can be certified to produce the same prediction for all inputs within a bounded embedding-space perturbation. We prove that the answer turns on a single condition: the $l_\infty$ norm of the state transition matrix must satisfy $\norm{A}_\infty<1$ (the \emph{contraction condition}), which enables exact interval bound propagation (IBP) certification for linear time-invariant classifiers. When the contraction condition holds, the reachable output interval has bounded steady-state width and examples can be certified as robustly classified. When it fails, the interval grows exponentially with sequence length and certification is impossible at any practical perturbation radius. We enforce contraction with a hinge penalty and show on toxic comment data that certified fraction improves from 41\% to 59\%, with a sharp empirical phase transition at $\norm{A}_\infty=1$ matching the theory. Applying a contraction-regularized S4 head to jailbreak detection on JailbreakBench, we achieve a zero-shot transfer to AdvBench (DR=0.994) and HarmBench (DR=0.988). A logistic regression on mean-pooled Mamba-130M embeddings matches or exceeds the S4 head on every detection metric, confirming that harmful intent is already linearly separable in the embedding space. The S4 safety head's contribution is not superior discrimination but the formal certification that no probe-based approach provides.

</details>


### [227] [Harness-Aware Distillation for Small Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02858)
*Moonseok Choi,Taehong Moon,Giung Nam,Juho Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一个名为HAD的方法，该方法在模型精简时关注教师相对于代理情境增加的价值，并通过对比行动偏好和有效性检查来改进代理学习，从而在多个长期任务基准测试中取得更好的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了改善模型精简过程中的代理行为，特别是在保留教师特定能力的同时，避免直接模仿教师所有输出的问题。

Method: HAD通过将对比教师行动偏好（考虑到和不考虑到情境信息的情况）和有效性检查（排除与情境记录矛盾的偏好对）两个组件结合起来，使学生模型可以从教师中学习到比单纯模仿更多有用的信息。

Result: HAD方法在多个长期任务基准测试中表现出色，优于现有的在策略精简基线，且无需任务奖励、成功标签或未来信息。

Conclusion: HAD方法通过有效的对比方法帮助学生模型更准确地学习教师的知识，从而在实际应用中表现出更强的适应性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Language model agents are deployed with a harness, the software around the model that manages its context, tools, and feedback. When such an agent is distilled into a smaller one, the harness stays in place, so the student mainly needs the teacher-specific abilities that the harness cannot provide, such as acting correctly on harness information. Standard distillation, however, imitates the teacher's full outputs and treats the harness as part of the input. We propose Harness-Aware Distillation (HAD), which focuses distillation on what the teacher adds beyond the harness. HAD complements on-policy distillation with two components: an action preference that contrasts the same teacher's actions with and without the harness information, scored after the student's own reasoning, and a validity check that drops preference pairs whose preferred action contradicts the harness records. We show that the contrast gives the student information that imitating the teacher alone cannot provide, and HAD needs no task rewards, success labels, or future information. Across multiple long-horizon agent benchmarks and models, HAD outperforms on-policy distillation baselines with the same fixed harness. Our analysis shows that HAD enters fewer unproductive loops and recovers from errors more often than the baselines, and suggests that it adaptively keeps learnable feedback in its weights while reading state information from the harness.

</details>


### [228] [TACD: Distilling Efficient Text-to-Motion Models via Terminal Amplification Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02867)
*Wei-Jin Huang,Yuan-Ming Li,Kun-Yu Lin,Wang Luo,Yinlin Zhu,Yue Yu,Shenghao Ye,Junbin Yuan,Fa-Ting Hong,Qing Zhang,Wei-Shi Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TACD是一种通过终端放大控制蒸馏的策略，旨在提高从文本提示生成高效动作生成器的效率，特别是在少量步骤的生成中，同时减少对显存的使用和提高整体速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本到动作模型虽然在动作质量和指令遵循方面取得了进步，但由于多步骤去噪和大规模模型组件的存在，部署速度缓慢且内存密集。因此，TACD提出了一个无需使用真实动作训练数据的方法，以加速和优化模型。

Method: TACD采用了一种分段的逐策略流蒸馏方法，监督学生模型生成轨迹中的干净动作预测，并通过将最新的教师查询与学生的步长绑定来控制有效损失权重，避免在去噪终点附近过度强调错误。

Result: 在HumanML3D和KIT-ML数据集上的实验表明，使用TACD的模型在八步生成中的FID降低了58%，对于扩散教师模型，TACD能够使四步生成的动作与五零步教师模型接近或同等的文本-动作检索性能。同时，针对HY-Motion和Kimodo，TACD使得紧凑结构的学生模型相比其教师模型实现了7.7至11.9倍的端到端加速，并降低了3.8至6.7倍的峰值GPU内存使用。

Conclusion: TACD通过改进终端区域的损失权重控制策略，有效地提高了动作生成器的效率，并减轻了现有模型在少量步骤生成中的缺陷，为文本到动作领域的模型优化提供了有价值的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent text-to-motion models have improved motion quality and instruction following, yet many-step denoising and large model components make deployment slow and memory-intensive. We present Terminal-Amplification-Controlled Distillation (TACD), an on-policy approach for training efficient motion generators from text prompts and pretrained teachers, without real-motion training data. Building on segmented on-policy flow distillation, we supervise clean-motion predictions along student-generated trajectories. We identify a failure mode in which velocity matching on a fixed supervision grid repeatedly overweights errors near the denoising endpoint, degrading few-step generation. TACD ties the latest teacher query to the student's step size, bounding the effective loss weights in clean-motion space without changing inference. Experiments on HumanML3D and KIT-ML demonstrate improved few-step generation, including a 58% reduction in eight-step HY-Motion student FID relative to distillation without this bound. For diffusion teachers, the endpoint-matching form of TACD yields four-step students with lower FID and matched or improved text-motion retrieval relative to their 50-step teachers on HumanML3D. On HY-Motion and Kimodo, eight-step students with compact components achieve 7.7-11.9x end-to-end speedups and reduce peak GPU memory by 3.8-6.7x relative to their teachers. Project page: https://vkgo.github.io/TACD/

</details>


### [229] [PsyEvo: A Personalized Counseling Agent That Self-Evolves at Test Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02885)
*Yuting Yan,Shihao Xu,Junhao Yu,Mingcong Zuo,Lu Chen,Nan Xiang,Haiyang Geng,Dongjie Tao,Minghao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PsyEvo 是一种基于大语言模型的心理咨询框架，通过三种组件实现个性化干预和响应策略改进，模拟评估显示其效果优于各个组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前心理治疗师资源短缺，而基于大语言模型的咨询框架有望提供大规模的心理支持解决方案，弥补人力资源不足的问题。

Method: PsyEvo框架通过Hierarchical Bayesian Skill Policy (HBSP)、Inter-session Listwise Preference Optimization (LiPO) 和 State-conditioned Ordinal Credit Assignment (SOCA) 三个组件实现。

Result: PsyEvo在模拟评估中得分7.684，在每种匹配运行中都超过了各个组件版本的表现，移除任一组件后，其均分为0.138--0.171分。

Conclusion: PsyEvo框架为大语言模型在心理咨询服务中的应用提供了一个有效的解决方案，通过结合个性化的干预和策略优化，提高了心理咨询服务的质量和效果。

Abstract: Mental health disorders affect a substantial proportion of the global population, yet a persistent shortage of trained practitioners leaves the majority without adequate care. Large language model (LLM)-based counselors present a promising direction for delivering scalable conversational psychological support. Offline model training alone leaves limited room to adapt to individual clients or to learn from ongoing therapeutic interaction at test time. We introduce PsyEvo, an LLM-based counseling framework that enables both client-specific personalization and response-policy improvement at test time through three components: Hierarchical Bayesian Skill Policy (HBSP) personalizes what intervention to apply by maintaining a per-client skill posterior updated from session feedback; Inter-session Listwise Preference Optimization (LiPO) improves how the selected skill is expressed by updating a shared response adapter from cross-client preference evidence; and State-conditioned Ordinal Credit Assignment (SOCA) supplies candidate preferences and trajectory credit to the two components through consistency-checked comparisons and ordinal projection. In simulated-client evaluation with shared online cohort adaptation, PsyEvo obtains 7.684 Overall on PsychEval and exceeds every component variant in each of three matched runs. Removing individual components lowers mean overall score by 0.138--0.171 under the shared configuration, supporting conditional contributions within the complete scaffold. Our code is available at https://github.com/Lingxi-mental-health/PsyEvo

</details>


### [230] [Interpreting at Write Time: A Policy Ablation for Multi-Goal Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02897)
*Albert Sadowski,Jarosław A. Chudziak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 摘要的生成并不是无偏见的过程，因为决定保留什么内容是由用户未知的长远目标驱动的。我们研究了三种不同的摘要策略：无目标导向、涵盖所有目标的单个摘要以及每个目标的独立摘要，发现目标导向的摘要在相关性、完整性和准确性上更胜一筹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要策略的选择对信息的保留有着直接影响，特别是在长周期助手需要依赖总结来提供信息时。

Method: 通过比较三种不同的摘要策略在多个场景中的表现，研究它们的优缺点。

Result: 结果显示，针对具体目标生成的摘要比无目标导向的摘要更加相关、完整且准确，而综合所有目标的摘要则表现最差。

Conclusion: 研究发现，应该根据用户的实际需求来生成摘要，以提高信息的可用性和准确性。

Abstract: A long-running assistant cannot keep everything it has seen, so it summarises. Summarising is not neutral: what is kept is chosen against some notion of what the record is for, and that choice is made once, before anyone knows which of the user's standing goals will ask. Goals rarely disagree about what happened. They disagree about which parts of it were worth the space. Once the history is too long to re-read, the summary replaces the stream, and whatever it left out is gone. We ask what a memory should summarise for when it serves several standing goals at once. Three policies answer differently: summarise with no goal in view, write one summary covering every goal, or write one summary per goal and read them together. We compare them across several models and event streams, holding the read step fixed so that only the write differs. The goals do pull apart: summaries written for different goals overlap each other less than a summary overlaps a rewrite of itself. Per-goal summaries win on relevance, completeness and accuracy, and the all-goal summary loses even to the neutral one written at a fraction of its budget. Interpreting at write pays off, but only for the goal that later asks.

</details>


### [231] [LUMOS: Tracing Parametric Knowledge from Training Data to Behavioral Outputs in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02902)
*Seoyeon Ye,Gayoung Kim,Jiyoung Hong,Sookyung Kim,Hyunsoo Cho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LUMOS 框架通过验证训练数据暴露与行为输出之间的因果链，揭示模型在编码和表达罕见事实方面的表现差异，并指出即使在推理提示下，模型对未见内容的表现仍差于随机基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决当前语言模型（LLM）参数知识分析中主要依赖输出而忽视训练数据的问题，通过实证方法评估模型的真正能力。

Method: LUMOS框架使用透明的训练语料库OLMo 2跟踪知识从训练数据暴露到行为输出的过程，通过分析训练数据暴露和行为输出之间的联系来揭示模型内部存储和行为表达的差异。

Result: 研究发现，虽然模型内部高度编码（84%）了罕见事实，但在行为输出中仅部分表达（54%），且大规模模型的这一差距有所减少。模型在自我反思时，在训练内容上的表现良好（83%），但在未见内容上的表现降至随机基线水平（49%），即使在需要推理提示的情况下也是如此。

Conclusion: 通过引入 LUMOS 框架，将训练数据轴纳入 LLM 评估，使推测的诊断变为可验证的断言，模型的训练数据轴应成为知识评估的常规组成部分。

Abstract: Current analyses of LLMs' parametric knowledge are largely output-centric, drawing conclusions about what a model knows without verifying what it was actually trained on. This leaves fundamental questions, such as whether a correct response reflects genuine generalization or rote memorization, grounded in speculation rather than evidence. To resolve these ambiguities, we introduce LUMOS, a diagnostic framework that traces knowledge along the causal chain from training-data exposure to behavioral output, leveraging OLMo 2 with its fully transparent training corpus. By grounding analysis in verified exposure, we reveal that models internally encode rare facts with high separability (84%) yet fail to express them behaviorally (54%), though this retrieval gap narrows with scale. Furthermore, when models are asked to self-reflect on their own answers, they perform reliably on trained content (83%) but drop to random-baseline levels (49%) on unseen content. This collapse persists even under chain-of-thought prompting, which inflates confidence signals rather than improving calibration. Collectively, these findings demonstrate that incorporating the training-data axis into LLM evaluation transforms speculative diagnoses into verifiable claims, and we advocate that this axis should be a standard component of knowledge assessment in LLMs.

</details>


### [232] [Frequency Is Not Sensitivity Identifying Safety-Sensitive Experts in Sparse MoE LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02910)
*Md Nurul Absar Siddiky,Liuwan Zhu,Yingfei Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过抑制基于梯度敏感性的路由器而不是基于激活频率的专家，抑制策略在减少恶意提示下的拒绝次数方面更加有效，尤其是在激活专家数量相等和名义恶意路由流量相等的条件下，梯度敏感性选择比激活频率选择在24/25种情况下更有效，且具有统计显著性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在通过不同方式抑制稀疏Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）语言模型中的专家，以评估这些方法对模型安全行为的影响，特别是通过比较基于激活频率和基于路由器梯度敏感性的抑制方法。

Method: 作者通过设计实验来比较基于激活频率（activation frequency）和基于路由器梯度敏感性的抑制方法对模型安全行为的影响。实验中使用了五种MoE架构，在500个良性提示和500个恶意提示上对专家进行排名，并在两种不同的预算条件下评估抑制策略的效果。

Result: 实验结果表明，基于路由器梯度敏感性的抑制方法比基于激活频率的方法在减少恶意提示下的拒绝次数方面更为有效。在两种预算条件下，梯度敏感性选择总是比激活频率选择在24/25种情况下更有效，且在所有25种情况下，其效果也比随机选择更优。特别是在OLMoE模型中，这种抑制方法的效果最为显著。

Conclusion: 研究结论指出，在评估MoE架构的安全对策时，路由器-梯度敏感性选择比基于激活频率的选择更为有效。这种选择方法不仅可以减少恶意行为，而且不会损害生成的质量。

Abstract: Suppressing a small set of routed experts can weaken the safety behavior of a sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language model without retraining. Which experts to suppress is therefore a security question, and the usual answer is activation frequency, but frequency measures use, not influence. We test an alternative: router-gradient sensitivity, the sensitivity of the sequence loss to the gate weights that select an expert. Across five MoE architectures, we rank experts by each signal on 500 benign and 500 malicious prompts and measure refusal on 100 held-out malicious prompts under two budgets: equal expert counts and equal nominal malicious routing traffic (1%-5%). Under each of the two budgets, router-gradient selection reduces refusals more than activation in 24 of 25 conditions, and more than a ten-trial random mean in all 25. The largest effect is in OLMoE, where refusals fall from 34 to 9 of 100 prompts (73.53% relative) with no degraded outputs, indicating substantive compliance rather than broken generation. After matching expert counts in every layer, gradient selection still produces greater refusal reduction than activation in 23 of 25 conditions, with two ties. An exploratory cross-model analysis links larger malicious-versus-benign concentration gaps to greater peak gradient effects (rho = 0.90; exact two-sided p = 0.083, n = 5). Together, the results support gradient selection under the tested budgets.

</details>


### [233] [HASTE: Evolving Agent Harnesses Against Emerging Attacks Using Sparse Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02920)
*Xiqiao Xiong,Moxin Li,Zhixin Ma,Ouxiang Li,Wenjie Wang,Fuli Feng,Xiangnan He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HASTE是一个多代理框架，通过安全规格生成和攻击案例生成之间的对抗互动，从有限的威胁证据中进化代理守护程序，实现对新兴攻击的防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的手动守护程序调整速度无法应对快速变化的攻击，因此需要一个自动化的过程来进化守护程序以适应新的威胁。

Method: HASTE通过生成器对抗网络（GAN）模型，结合安全规格生成和攻击案例生成，从有限的威胁证据中自动化进化代理守护程序。

Result: 实验结果显示，HASTE在多种基础模型、攻击类型和证据形式上，能有效降低攻击成功率，同时维持正常任务的效用。

Conclusion: HASTE提供了一种自动进化代理守护程序的新方法，这种方法能够有效地针对新的攻击进行防御。

Abstract: Agent harnesses play a critical role in defenses by enforcing safety constraints to prevent unsafe actions. However, rapidly emerging attacks outpace manual harness adaptation, motivating automated harness evolution. Yet the signals available for harness evolution are often sparse, such as brief descriptions or a few attack examples in threat reports and preprints. To address this limitation, we introduce HASTE, a multi-agent framework that evolves agent harnesses from sparse threat evidence through an adversarial interplay between safety-specification generation and attack-case generation. Safety specifications guide harness updates toward addressing identified safety vulnerabilities, while attack cases probe for remaining safety vulnerabilities after each update. By feeding evaluation outcomes back into both processes, HASTE enables harness evolution against emerging attacks beyond the initially observed evidence. Experimental results across multiple backbone models, attack types, and evidence forms show that HASTE consistently reduces attack success rates while preserving benign-task utility. The code is available at https://github.com/xxiqiao/HASTE.

</details>


### [234] [Positive-Unlabeled Learning for Agent Safety False Alarm Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02925)
*Xichen Yan,Chongyang Gao,Kezhen Chen,Guangyi Zhang,Jiaqi Wu,Lixu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种缓解安全监控保守性导致的过多误警的两阶段框架，通过可信PU监督（Trust-aware PU Supervision）和可靠性门控排名精炼（Reliability-gated Rank Distillation）来改善排名，最后通过共识导向结构精炼（Consensus-guided Structural Refinement）提升排名，该方法在多种主流安全监控中表现出优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的安全监控在防范语言模型代理与外部工具和环境互动带来的风险方面发挥了重要作用，但保守性可能产生大量误警，增加资源负担并削弱警报的信任度。因此提高误警的检测效率成为关键挑战。

Method: 作者提出了一种两阶段框架，首先通过可信PU监督，调整信任安全参考并减轻误警的负面压力；其次通过可靠性门控排名精炼，整合多种PU参考模型的一致排名偏好。最后，利用共识导向结构精炼，通过层级安全参考支持、警报关系和预测参考共识来改进学生排名。框架不需要警报安全性标签，也不改变底层监控器。

Result: 该方法在主流安全监控中的表现卓越，相较于八个评价的PU基线，AUPRC提高5.27-16.98个百分点；相较于最强评价的PU基线PULDA，能以5%的审查预算恢复33.3%更多的误警。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的方法在减少误警和提高误警检测效率方面具有显著优势，为语言模型代理的安全监控提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Safety monitors help safeguard language-model agents interacting with external tools and environments, but conservative monitoring can generate many false alarms, consuming extensive review resources and weakening trust in alerts. Because false and genuine alarms often remain interleaved in native monitor scores, obtaining a reliable cutoff still requires substantial manual verification. In practice, a small set of verified-safe non-alarmed trajectories may be available while alarms remain unlabeled, naturally casting false-alarm auditing as a positive-unlabeled (PU) ranking problem. The key challenge is monitor-induced selection, since observed safe references are accepted by the monitor, while the hidden safe alarms of interest are precisely those it incorrectly flags, making the observed positives poorly representative of the positives to be recovered. To address this challenge, we propose a two-stage framework in which Trust-aware PU Supervision adapts safe references toward the alarm domain and protects plausible false alarms from excessive negative pressure, while Reliability-gated Rank Distillation consolidates consistent ordering preferences from multiple PU reference models into a single student. Consensus-guided Structural Refinement then improves the student ranking using hierarchical safe-reference support, alarm relations, and predicted reference consensus. The framework requires no alarm safety labels for fitting and leaves the underlying monitor unchanged. Across mainstream safety monitors, our method achieves a macro AUPRC of $0.6444$, outperforming eight evaluated PU baselines by 5.27--16.98 absolute percentage points; compared with PULDA, the strongest evaluated PU baseline, it recovers 33.3% more false alarms at a 5% review budget.

</details>


### [235] [When to Compile a Computer-Use Agent? Measuring Payback and Making Compilation Decisions for Token Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02932)
*Yulong Ming,Jie Xu,Zihan Wu,Xiaohua Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PACE系统通过记录编译成本和估计算法来动态评估任务编译的时间成本，确保重复使用的程序具有经济效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的重复性GUI任务手工执行成本较高，编译成程序可以节省成本，但需要解决编译成本不确定和未来任务及界面变化无法预知的问题。

Method: PACE系统采用了一种测量编译成本和执行成本的方法，并用估算的未来节约与编译成本对比，同时在线检查任务到达和编译结果来动态决定是否编译。

Result: PACE系统在不同任务集上比其他方法平均节省了17.3%的令牌成本，成功编译后的平均使用次数介于2到16次之间。

Conclusion: PACE系统能有效评估编译收益，并动态调整编译决策，从而节省了重复性GUI任务的执行成本。

Abstract: Compiling GUI procedures that agents execute repeatedly into programs can reduce their token costs. However, measuring payback and deciding when to compile have two challenges. First, compilation costs are uncertain because attempts can require repair and still fail to produce a usable program. Second, future reuse is unknown because tasks may stop arriving or GUI drift may stop the program from working. To address these challenges, we propose PACE (Payback-Aware Compilation from Experience), a system with a measurement protocol and an online compilation algorithm. The measurement protocol records successful and failed compilation costs, and compares agent and program execution costs on matched task inputs to estimate per-use savings and payback counts. Using these measurements, the online algorithm compares estimated future savings with compilation costs, including failed attempts, based on past task arrivals and compilation outcomes. It checks execution and compilation charges against a cumulative budget determined by observed task arrivals before allowing either action. Under stated action-cost assumptions, total cost after each arrival is at most $1+ε$ times the cost of running every task with the agent. For successful compilation attempts, estimated payback counts excluding source agent runs are 2-16 uses. In simulations using recorded task arrivals, PACE reduces token costs by 17.3% compared with ReAct, 24.9% with the AutoRPA adaptation, and 17.3% with the ToolPro adaptation on average ($ε=0.25$).

</details>


### [236] [Continual Graph Memory for Mathematical Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02945)
*Junyi Zhang,Jinxi Yu,Eric Hanchen Jiang,Jiachen Lu,Zhi Zhang,Xinjie He,Hyunsik Chae,Ethan Ji,Alexander K Taylor,Vigyan Sahai,Yiwen Kou,Kai-Wei Chang,Raghu Meka,Nanyun Peng,Amit Sahai,Terence Tao,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究介绍了Ansatz，一种基于持续图记忆的数学研究代理，用于组织和重用数学证明搜索过程中的中间结果，成功解决了多个开放数学研究问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决数学前沿问题需要大量并行工作的代理，这将产生大量中间证明结果。目前，组织这些结果以及重用先前探索的信息存在挑战。

Method: Ansatz使用持续图记忆系统，这是一种基于图且可进化的跨问题数学研究记忆系统，代表了所有中间探索结果，包括事实、计划和反例，以及表示它们之间关系的边。依赖感知检索提供精确的局部上下文，证据敏感的策展人更新研究前沿并提炼之前尝试的教训，以及范围内的回忆用于局部重新证明而不是无批判地重用。

Result: 实验覆盖了所有十个First Proof Second Batch问题，并且Ansatz也解决了几个新的数学问题，如Jamison毛虫猜想和Erdős问题289、348和488。 

Conclusion: Ansatz展示了其执行长期数学搜索并重用先前探索的能力，适用于解决开放的研究问题。

Abstract: Using frontier agent harnesses to tackle mathematical research problems has emerged as an effective means of advancing mathematics. However, solving frontier problems in mathematics may require a massive number of agents working in parallel for extended periods to construct proofs, thereby generating an enormous volume of intermediate proof results. Organizing these intermediate results throughout a long-horizon proof-search process and reusing knowledge gained from prior explorations remain major challenges. We present Ansatz, a mathematical research agent built around Continual Graph Memory, a graph-based, evolvable, cross-problem mathematical research memory system that explicitly organizes the entire proof search process and reuses information from exploration trajectories of previous problems. Specifically, we develop a unified graph memory that represents all intermediate exploration results, including facts, plans, and counterexamples, together with edges that explicitly represent the relationships among them; dependency-aware retrieval supplies precisely targeted local context; an evidence-sensitive curator updates the research frontier and distills lessons from prior attempts; and scoped recall surfaces earlier statements and negative findings for local re-proving rather than uncritical reuse. Experiments cover runs across all ten First Proof Second Batch problems, together with four component studies. Ansatz reports closure on all ten research tasks, demonstrating its ability to sustain and resume long-horizon mathematical search. Beyond these problems, Ansatz also produces solutions to the Jamison caterpillar conjecture and Erdős Problems 289, 348, and 488 without human intervention, and makes partial progress on several open problems, illustrating its strong ability to solve open mathematical research problems.

</details>


### [237] [Personalized Automatic Speech Recognition for a Dysarthric and Tracheostomic Speaker using Artificial Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03017)
*David Nadrchal,Monorama Swain,Florian Schmid,Gerhard Widmer,Paul Primus*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种专为永久气管造口及严重构音障碍的捷克演讲者设计的自动语音识别系统，发布了一个包含33小时标注演讲数据的公共数据集，并提出了一种多阶段训练管道，实现在接近实时场景下的表现优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鉴于严重构音障碍使演讲者言语对未训练的听众不可理解，通过创建个性化的自动语音识别系统来提高特定人群中语音的识别率。

Method: 利用Whisper Base模型进行多阶段微调，首先对标准捷克语进行微调，然后对模拟的气管造口语音进行微调，最后利用演讲者的数据进行个性化调整。

Result: 在三种接近实时的场景（事先编排的对话，问题回答，自发对话）中，相对字符错误率降低了50%，并通过定量结果和演讲者的反馈证明了即使对于严重的言语障碍，也有助于实现有用的自动语音识别。

Conclusion: 即使面对极度受限的语音，构建一种有效的自动语音识别系统是可行的，该方法为解决言语障碍人群的语音识别需求提供了一种新路径。

Abstract: This work presents an automatic speech recognition (ASR) system personalized for a Czech speaker with a permanent tracheal stoma and severe dysarthria rendering their speech unintelligible to untrained listeners. We release a public dataset containing 33 annotated hours of the speaker's speech, collected using a novel "artificial conversation" protocol designed for high engagement and dialogue realism. We propose a multi-stage training pipeline based on Whisper Base: fine-tuning on standard Czech speech, acoustically simulated tracheostomic speech, and the speaker's data. We evaluate the system across three near real-time scenarios: scripted conversations, question answering, and spontaneous dialogue, achieving a 50\% relative reduction in Character Error Rate compared to Whisper Base baseline and surpassing the average recognition accuracy of their assistants in acoustic recognition of isolated utterances. We demonstrate that even for severely impeded speech, a helpful ASR is achievable, as evidenced by the quantitative results and the feedback from the speaker.

</details>


### [238] [Reasoning with Evidence, Not Merely Rationales: Verifiable Preference Proofs for LLM-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02968)
*Yu Hou,Nathaniel Kang,Pengkai Wang,Hua Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为PROVE-REC的一般框架，用于LLM基于的推荐系统的可验证偏好推理。该框架通过两步转换历史交互记录为紧凑的偏好证明，并确保使用该证明进行推荐。通过一系列实验证明了PROVE-REC的有效性，相比现有方法提供了7.45%的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LLM的推荐系统在生成偏好推理时可能无法完全依赖真实的用户反馈，导致推荐不够准确。因此，提出了一种新的框架来增强推荐的可验证性。

Method: 该框架分为两个阶段。第一阶段将用户的完整历史交互记录转化为包含正面偏好和避开行为的简明偏好证明，与证据条目相关联。第二阶段依据偏好证明和选择的证据进行推荐，确保推荐过程按推理路径进行。并通过比较掩盖的选择证据和控制项的效应来验证证据对接地，通过移除偏好断言检查推荐影响，同时也保持排序质量。

Result: 在广泛的实际数据集上进行全面实验表明，PROVE-REC框架在各种基线方法上表现优异，尤其是在顺序模型，生成模型和LLM增强模型。在一些数据集上，PROVE-REC的改进率高达7.45%。

Conclusion: 研究得出结论，新的PROVE-REC框架通过提供一种可验证的偏好推理，可以显著提高推荐系统的性能，同时确保推荐结果的准确性和相关性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can infer user preferences from interaction histories and reviews, yet the rationales they generate may not reflect the information actually used for recommendation. A preference claim may be weakly supported by its selected evidence, or may have little effect on the final ranking. We refer to these two failures as the grounding-influence gap. We introduce PROVE-REC, a general framework for verifiable preference reasoning in LLM-based recommendation. Pass A converts the complete pre-target history into a compact preference proof consisting of positive and avoidance claims linked to selected evidence entries. Pass B predicts the next item using only the proof and its selected evidence, preventing the recommender from bypassing the reasoning path. To verify evidence-to-proof grounding, we compare the effect of masking selected evidence with masking a comparable control entry. To verify proof-to-recommendation influence, we remove a preference claim and measure the resulting decrease in the target item's ranking margin. A ranking-preservation objective further retains useful information from the complete history. Comprehensive experiments on wide-ranging real-world datasets demonstrate that PROVE-REC consistently outperforms strong sequential, generative, and LLM-enhanced baselines, with improvements of up to 7.45%. Controlled ablations confirm the effectiveness of the two-pass architecture and verification objectives. Moreover, PROVE-REC produces claims that are more strongly grounded in historical evidence and more influential to recommendation while preserving ranking quality.

</details>


### [239] [CreateScore: Domain-Theory-Informed Bayesian Routing for LLM-Based CV Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02972)
*Rupsa Roy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CreateScore 是一个基于贝叶斯网络的系统，利用领域理论对大型语言模型进行分类任务的路由，减少高能力模型的使用，并提高评分的一致性，但未能识别出 8B 模型的错误决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过利用领域知识优化高能力模型的使用，以减少代价并提升评分的一致性。

Method: CreateScore 系统采用一种手工指定的有向无环图，结合狄利克雷-多项分布条件概率表，将简历证据转化为后验不确定性，并根据低不确定性决策由局部的 8B 模型处理，高不确定性决策则由参考的 120B 模型处理。

Result: 系统在处理 200 份合成数据科学简历的 5 个评价标准时，有 77.7% 的决策由 8B 模型本地解决，使用令牌量减少了 65.2%，评分一致性提高了 10%。

Conclusion: 尽管系统在资源使用和评分一致性方面有所改进，但在识别 8B 模型错误决策方面的表现不如随机。系统不作为独立招聘系统，但对于审计和成本降低具有作用。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can support rubric-based screening of CVs, but applying a high-capability model to every candidate and criterion is costly. We present CreateScore, a domain-theory-informed Bayesian network for criterion-level LLM routing. A hand-specified directed acyclic graph with Dirichlet-multinomial conditional probability tables converts CV evidence into posterior uncertainty; low-uncertainty decisions are resolved by a local 8B model and uncertain ones are escalated to a 120B reference model. The graph is causally motivated, but the system performs standard Bayesian conditioning, not causal inference. The escalation threshold is calibrated on a training fold (target: 70% resolved locally) and then frozen. On 200 synthetic Data Science CVs (139 training and 61 test candidates, five criteria), 77.7% of criterion decisions were resolved locally (237 of 305). Relative to a reference condition in which the 120B model adjudicated every criterion, routed escalation reduced token use by 65.2% and raised exact score agreement from 32.8% (8B alone) to 42.6% (95% CI 31.0-55.1%); at n = 61 the gain was not statistically distinguishable. The uncertainty signal did not, however, identify the decisions on which the 8B model erred: disagreement with the reference was 16.2% among escalated and 19.4% among locally resolved decisions (AUROC 0.47, 95% CI 0.39-0.56), no better than random selection. We also document how an earlier evaluation was invalidated when truncated reasoning-model outputs were silently replaced by local labels, and we recommend safeguards for cascade evaluation. CreateScore is supported as an auditable cost-reduction mechanism, not yet as a targeted error detector, and is not an autonomous hiring system.

</details>


### [240] [Verifiable, Articulable, and Tacit Components of Preference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03025)
*Alexander Spangher,Sheldon Huang,Andreas Haupt,Noah D. Goodman,Diyi Yang,Daniel E. Ho,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究创建了CreativePreferences数据集，包含280万文本和31.7亿人类偏好判断，覆盖7个创意领域。通过可执行程序、评分表和密集训练模型来建模偏好标签。研究发现各领域普遍存在可表达性和可验证性缺口，并提出测量这些缺口的方法。当更多人参与判断时，可表达性缺口会扩大。研究指出了这一现象的实际后果，并提供了关于任务提示、学习机制改进和保留人类判断等方面的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了弥合AI决策与人类偏好之间的差距，研究强调了对不可言喻和不可验证的偏好维度的关注，并通过构建CreativePreferences数据集，探索这些难以量化和验证的偏好特性。

Method: 该研究构建了一个大规模的标注偏好数据集CreativePreferences，并使用可执行程序、评分表和密集训练模型来建模数据集中的标签。研究采用了一个新颖的测量方法，以评估可表达性缺口（VAT-VA）和可验证性缺口（VAT-V），并在不同领域的判断中观察这些缺口.

Result: 研究发现了各领域存在显著的可表达性和可验证性缺口。在不同的领域和任务中，这些缺口大小不同，特别是在创造性和知识密集型领域中更为突出。研究还发现，随着更多人类参与判断，可表达性缺口会扩大，符合Collins关于集体潜知识的概念。

Conclusion: 研究表明，尽管在某些领域通常认为这些特性是完全可验证的，但在实践中仍然存在显著的可表达性和可验证性缺口。这些结论对理解和改善AI模型在需要考虑人类偏好和直觉的任务方面的表现具有重要意义。“说话”偏好与“理性”偏好的差距可能会影响模型行为，而人类生成和AI生成的偏好会表现出不同的模式，说明了在特定任务中优先考虑人类判断的重要性。

Abstract: What makes a short story gripping; a news article newsworthy; or a math proof elegant? These constructs resist articulation or verification; their meaning is at least partially tacit. However, modern AI models are improved primarily via articulated constitutions, rubrics and verifiers (i.e. in RLAIF and RLVR); tacit components of preferences are typically understudied. We introduce a large, labeled preference dataset CreativePreferences, containing 2.8M texts labeled by 317M human preference judgments across 7 creative domains, with 42 benchmark tasks. We model these labels with executable programs, rubric banks and densely trained models (V, A and VAT, respectively). We observe robust articulability gaps, VAT-VA; and verifiability gaps, VAT-V; we estimate upper and lower bounds for each gap with a novel measurement approach that discovers articulable and verifiable metrics, identifies spurious variables and estimates the value of undiscovered metrics using capture-recapture. These gaps occur across all domains, even in domains traditionally treated as fully verifiable: correctness-centered domains (i.e. mathematics and software engineering) and claim- and novelty-centric domains (i.e. news, patents, peer review). The size of the gap varies based on domain (e.g. peer review and creative writing have the largest articulability gaps) and widens as more people take part in the judgment, consistent with Collins' collective tacit knowledge. We show two consequences: (1) on human generations, the full model more closely matches human preferences, often in disagreement with articulated criteria, and (2) in an analogy to Goodhart's law, articulating preference shifts it away from the tacit dimension. Articulability and verifiability gaps are consequential; we give recommendations on when tasks can be prompted; how learning mechanisms might improve; and when to leave judgments with humans.

</details>


### [241] [Reliable Self-Evolution with Imperfect Proxy Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02975)
*Kangjun Noh,Soyu Kim,Kyungwoo Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Conformal Interval-Driven Self-Evolution (CISE)的方法，通过构建候选物特定的奖励区间，使用条件卷式推断和迭代在线密度比估计，解决了基于大语言模型的自演进搜索中的不确定性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自演进搜索系统在科学发现中具有潜力，但由于某些领域中的高保真度评估成本极高的问题，这些系统依赖于低费用但不完美的代理奖励。这可能导致不切实际的候选物获得高分，从而污染最终输出和反馈指导。

Method: 本文提出CISE方法，通过使用条件卷式推断和迭代在线密度比估计，为每个候选物构建特定的奖励区间，并仅在所有必需的属性区间完全包含在各自的可行区域内时返回候选物。

Result: 研究在材料科学中的三个自演进搜索任务上进行评估，结果显示CISE返回的所有候选物在高保真评估中均为真阳性，而基线则返回了更多的候选物，其中包括误报。这些结果表明，当下游验证预算有限时，较少而更精确的候选物名单的价值。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了使用条件卷式推断和在线密度比估计构建候选物特定奖励区间的有效性和可靠性，为自演进搜索提供了更可靠的方法。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based self-evolving search is a promising approach to scientific discovery. However, high-fidelity evaluation of every candidate is prohibitively expensive in some domains. Self-evolving systems in such settings therefore rely on low-cost but imperfect proxy rewards, which may assign high scores to infeasible candidates. These false positives may contaminate both the final output and the feedback used to guide subsequent generations. This motivates statistically calibrated reward intervals for more reliable self-evolving search. We propose Conformal Interval-Driven Self-Evolution (CISE), which constructs candidate-specific reward intervals using conditional conformal inference and iteration-wise online density-ratio estimation. CISE uses conservative interval-based rewards for evolutionary feedback and returns candidates only when all required property intervals lie entirely within their respective feasible regions. We derive fixed-iteration coverage results under explicit assumptions of independence and covariate shift. We evaluate CISE on three self-evolving search tasks in materials science. In our experiments, all candidates returned by CISE are true positives under high-fidelity evaluation, whereas the baselines return more candidates but include false positives. These results highlight the value of a smaller, more precise shortlist when downstream validation budgets are limited. Our repository is available at https://github.com/MLAI-Yonsei/CISE.git.

</details>


### [242] [Peer Influence across Heterogeneous AI Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03095)
*Frida Nøhr Laustsen,Marie Haahr Petersen,Victoria Popa,Ariel Flint,Romualdo Pastor-Satorras,Andrea Baronchelli,Luca Maria Aiello*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现，当两个AI代理意见不一时，接收方通常会改变初始判断。这种说服力并不依赖于模型的规模或确定性，而是与听众的可说服性密切相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着多代理系统中不同语言模型的融合，理解哪些判断在交互中得到保留变得重要。因此，该研究探讨了在模型交互中哪一方更具说服力。

Method: 通过测试七种开放权重模型在三个语言理解任务中的表现来评估模型在互动中的说服效应。

Result: 研究结果显示，当模型之间产生分歧时，接收方常常放弃自己的初始判断。然而，这种说服力并不受模型个体确定性或规模的影响，反而取决于接收者的可说服性。此外，说服的效果在不同模型组合中表现出异质性。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示，交互模型的行为无法仅仅通过其个体属性来预测，而必须在它们实际运行的组合中进行评估。

Abstract: When two AI agents disagree, who persuades whom? As multi-agent systems increasingly combine language models of different families and sizes, the answer can determine which judgments survive interaction. Measuring persuasion as the probabilistic shift in an agent's decision after a single exchange with a dissenting peer, we test seven open-weight models across three language understanding tasks. We find that persuasion is strong: when models disagree, receivers often abandon their initial judgment after seeing a peer's answer and explanation. Surprisingly, however, neither standalone certainty nor model scale reliably predicts persuasion dynamics. Models producing almost perfectly consistent decisions in isolation can be among the most susceptible to persuasion, and small models can match larger ones as persuaders and resist their influence just as effectively. Furthermore, we show that the size of the shift depends more on the susceptibility of the listener than on the persuasiveness of the speaker. Persuasion patterns are therefore specific to each model pairing, with heterogeneity amplifying persuasion in some combinations and suppressing it in others, allowing a dissenting agent running a small model to overturn the judgments of a much larger one. These findings show that the behavior of interacting models cannot be inferred from their individual properties but must be evaluated in the combinations in which they will operate.

</details>


### [243] [Relevant Evidence Decoding for Audio-Visual Hallucination Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02976)
*Hyunjae Ra,Aecheon Jung,Jungin Park,Sungeun Hong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了 Relevance Evidence Decoding (RED)，一种无需训练的方法，能够识别与问题相关的证据，并增强其贡献。经过在三种 AV-LLMs 上的测试，在三个音频-视觉幻觉基准上的准确率分别提高了 7%、6.3% 和 3.8%，并且相较于标准解码算法，其首个预测词的时间开销为 1.5 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的解码方法无法处理音频-视频大语言模型中的跨模态幻觉问题，主要因为不同问题需要不同的感知证据，而现有方法难以充分识别和利用这种证据。

Method: RED 方法通过点式互信息（PMI）来量化单模态以外所提供的预测支持，并将音频和视频贡献分解成单独的音频、视频及其交互项。在仅问题推理过程中确定所需证据类型后，方法将其与原音频-视频预测结合。

Result: 在三种音频-视觉幻觉基准上，RED 方法在 CMM、AVHBench 和 SVHalluc 上分别提高了 7%、6.3% 和 3.8% 的准确率，并且相较于标准解码算法，首个预测词的时间开销为 1.5 倍。

Conclusion: RED 方法为改善音频-视频大语言模型中的跨模态幻觉提供了有效的方法，并展示了其在多个 AV-LLMs 和多任务上的应用潜力。

Abstract: Audio-Visual Large Language Models (AV-LLMs) remain prone to cross-modal hallucinations, where one modality incorrectly affects predictions about another. Although contrastive decoding reduces hallucinations in vision-language models, its direct extension to AV-LLMs overlooks a key challenge: different questions require different perceptual evidence, including audio, video, or their interaction. Notably, we observe that joint audio-visual inference can weaken the prediction even when a model can recover the correct answer from a single informative modality. For example, when asked which instrument is heard, a model may correctly predict violin from the audio alone. Once a video showing a guitar is added, its confidence in violin may drop. In this paper, we introduce Relevant Evidence Decoding (RED), a training-free method that identifies question-relevant evidence and selectively strengthens its contribution. RED uses pointwise mutual information to quantify the predictive support provided by audio and video beyond the question alone. It decomposes their joint contribution into audio, video, and residual interaction components. A question-only inference pass determines the required evidence type, after which the model augments the original audio-visual prediction with the corresponding PMI contribution. Across three audio-visual hallucination benchmarks and three AV-LLMs, RED improves accuracy over standard decoding by up to 7% on CMM, 6.3% on AVHBench, and 3.8% on SVHalluc, with an average relative time to first token of 1.5x standard decoding.

</details>


### [244] [Gains and Collapse in On-Policy Distillation:A Reinforcement Learning Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03185)
*Han Cui,Jianhao Yan,Yun Luo,Hongbo Zhang,Zhizhang Fu,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OPD在提高语言模型性能的同时，可能导致生成过长和重复的问题，这取决于教师模型的奖励机制是否可靠。通过掩码不良响应和使用SFT初始化可以缓解这些问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD虽然能提升语言模型的性能，但也可能产生过度生成和重复的问题，本文通过奖励学习的视角来探讨这一现象背后的原因。

Method: 通过奖励学习的视角分析OPD过程，实验验证了OPD在不同的情境下对模型性能的影响。

Result: 实验表明，当隐式奖励模型可靠时，OPD可以更有效地生成正确响应；当偏好与质量不匹配时，会引发奖励作弊，放大过长和重复的生成。

Conclusion: 研究发现OPD通过放大教师模型偏好的学生行为转移到了评估学生的生成质量上，提出了掩码不良响应和使用SFT初始化等改进策略。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has become an important approach to language model post-training. However, despite its performance gains, OPD can also collapse into excessively long and repetitive generation, and the mechanism underlying these divergent outcomes remains poorly understood. We explain these outcomes through a reinforcement learning perspective: the teacher implicitly rewards student behaviors, even those it rarely exhibits itself. From this perspective, our experiments show that OPD improves performance without expanding the student's capabilities. When the implicit reward model is reliable, OPD makes correct responses easier to sample. In contrast, when the preference misaligns with quality, reward hacking happens: the implicit reward model amplifies overlong, repetitive student rollouts, even though it rarely generates such text itself. Guided by this diagnosis, we find that masking unhealthy responses during training and using SFT initialization can each effectively mitigate the collapse. Together, these findings show that OPD amplifies student behaviors favored by the teacher's implicit feedback, shifting the focus from how well the teacher generates to how reliably it evaluates student rollouts. Our code is available at https://github.com/HancCui/opd_hacking.

</details>


### [245] [RASPER: Reward-Aligned Summarization of Clinical Notes for EHR Outcome Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02979)
*Arya Hadizadeh Moghaddam,Mohsen Nayebi Kerdabadi,Chen Chen,Dongjie Wang,Zijun Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RASPER 是一个直接针对下游临床任务优化笔记总结的方法，通过奖励学习的方式提高预测质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在电子健康记录（EHR）中，未结构化的出院笔记包含大量患者特有的证据，这些证据不能仅靠标准的临床代码捕捉。现有的通用总结方法可能忽略关键证据，保留不必要的细节。

Method: RASPER 使用可调参数的语言模型（LLM）来从出院笔记中提取与任务相关的信息，并通过基于预测反馈的强化学习进行训练。此外，通过一个纵向编码器将结构化代码转化为软提示来整合每个患者的临床背景。

Result: RASPER 在Readmission prediction 和Medication recommendation 任务上表现优于强大的基线模型。

Conclusion: RASPER 通过直接优化总结与下游预测任务的一致性，有效地保留了补充患者特征的临床证据，进一步提高了预测性能。

Abstract: Unstructured discharge notes in Electronic Health Records (EHRs) often carry signal complementary to structured medical codes, holding patient-specific evidence that standardized cohort-level codes alone cannot capture. However, this evidence in notes is frequently buried in lengthy, noisy text that is not intentionally written with any specific clinical prediction in mind. Summarization is an obvious mitigation, but generic summaries, tuned for fluency rather than the outcome, routinely omit decisive evidence while retaining plausible but uninformative detail. To this end, we propose RASPER, a Reward-Aligned Summarizer for Prediction in EHR, that optimizes note summarization directly against the downstream clinical task. RASPER employs a tunable LLM-based summarizer to extract task-relevant evidence from discharge notes and trains it via reinforcement learning from prediction feedback, using a reward derived from the downstream predictor's loss. To ground the summarizer, a longitudinal encoder converts structured codes into soft prompts that incorporate each patient's clinical context into note summarization. By rewarding the quality of the resulting multimodal prediction, RASPER encourages the summarizer to retain patient-specific evidence that complements, rather than duplicates, information captured by structured codes. RASPER consistently outperforms strong baselines on both readmission prediction and medication recommendation across MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV.

</details>


### [246] [KV$^2$: A Self-Refining KV Cache](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03198)
*Johannes Wesch,Danni Liu,Jan Niehues*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KV²是一种基于选择性重建的查询无关KV缓存压缩方法，通过轻量级代理评分器识别信息性上下文令牌，仅重新处理这些部分来计算最终淘汰分数。该方法在RULER、Needle-in-a-Haystack和LongBench上的表现优于基线，在预算紧张的情况下具有显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的长上下文模型受到键值缓存内存足迹的限制，当一个预填好的上下文需要为许多不同的查询提供服务时，缓存成本会非常高。KV²旨在解决这一问题，通过提供一种基于选择性重建的查询无关KV缓存压缩方法，减少不必要的上下文重处理，提高利用率。

Method: KV²的方法首先是使用一种轻量级代理评分器来识别上下文中的信息性令牌，然后仅重新处理这些部分来计算最终的淘汰分数。这种方法在资源有限的情况下，能够有效地减少上下文重处理的需求，从而节约内存和处理时间。

Result: 在RULER、Needle-in-a-Haystack和LongBench等基准测试上，KV²的性能优于所有基线方法。特别是在RULER 16K中，2%的KV缓存预算下，KV²比第二好的基线方法平均提高了超过40个百分点。此外，KV²在2%-10%的预算范围内仍保持最佳表现，同时压缩阶段运行时间和峰值内存占用低于完全上下文重建。

Conclusion: KV²为重复使用的键值缓存环境提供了一种有效的方法，它可以显著提高效率，而不必重新处理整个上下文。这一方法证明了在资源有限的环境中仍然可以有效管理缓存，提高了大规模模型在实际应用中的可用性。

Abstract: The memory footprint of the key-value (KV) cache constrains the practical use of long-context models, and it dominates cost when one prefilled context must later serve many different queries. In this reusable setting, query-agnostic compression trades cost against quality: lightweight estimators are cheap but less accurate, whereas full-context reconstruction scoring is more accurate yet reprocesses the entire prompt. We introduce KV$^2$, a query-agnostic KV-cache compression method based on selective reconstruction. KV$^2$ first uses a lightweight proxy scorer to identify informative in-context tokens, then reprocesses only this subset to compute final eviction scores. On RULER, Needle-in-a-Haystack, and LongBench, KV$^2$'s margin over baselines widens as the budget tightens: on RULER 16K at a 2% KV-cache budget it improves the average score over the next-best baseline by more than 40 percentage points, and on LongBench it attains the highest average across 2%-10% budgets at lower compression-stage runtime and peak memory than full-context reconstruction. Reusable KV-cache compression thus does not require reprocessing the full context. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/KVsquared-0B97.

</details>


### [247] [Safeguarding Mutual Correction in Source-Free Domain Adaptation via Cut Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02981)
*Seongjun Lee,Changhee Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SafeCut 是一种新颖的方法，利用剪切统计作为无监督的预测可靠性指标来控制跨模型的监督，在目标领域中动态调整监督的方向和强度，以确保真正的纠正信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单一模型和早期方法在无需原始源域标签的情况下难以纠正系统性错误，而引入ViL模型作为外部知识源的方法则主要采取单向监督，忽视了两个模型在预测上的互补性质。

Method: SafeCut 方法使用剪切统计作为预测可靠性的无监督指标来控制跨模型的监督，基于相对可靠性动态调整监督的方向和强度，选择性地放大真实的矫正信号并抑制错误的矫正信号。

Result:  SAFECut 在多种SFDA基准测试中的表现优于现有方法，证明了通过剪切统计进行相互校正的保护机制的有效性。

Conclusion: SafeCut 方法提供了一种新的策略，通过可靠性的动态管理实现模型间的相互校正，在无标签的设置中显著提高了源无域适应的效果。

Abstract: Source-Free Domain Adaptation (SFDA) aims to adapt a source-pretrained model to an unlabeled target domain without access to the original source domain. While early single-model approaches rely on self-refinement, they are inherently susceptible to confirmation bias and struggle to correct their own systematic errors. To overcome this limitation, recent methods introduce Vision-Language (ViL) models as external knowledge sources. However, these approaches operate in a largely unidirectional paradigm, using the ViL model primarily to supervise the source-pretrained model. This overlooks a key structural property: the two models exhibit distinct failure modes -- where one produces an incorrect prediction, the other may produce a correct one, creating a natural opportunity for mutual correction within the target domain. Yet, without ground-truth labels, identifying which model is correct on any given sample is non-trivial, and naively exchanging predictions risks propagating errors across models. To address this challenge, we propose SafeCut, a novel approach that leverages the cut statistic as a label-free measure of prediction reliability to gate cross-model supervision. Our approach dynamically controls both the direction and strength of supervision based on relative reliability, selectively amplifying true corrections while suppressing miscorrections on a per-sample basis. We further provide theoretical justification showing that this reliability-gated mechanism guarantees a net-positive correction signal. Extensive experiments across diverse SFDA benchmarks demonstrate that SafeCut achieves state-of-the-art performance, highlighting the effectiveness of safeguarding mutual correction in SFDA via cut statistics.

</details>


### [248] [Multilingual GSM-Symbolic: What determines capability transfer across languages?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03367)
*Kenneth Enevoldsen,Riley Herchert,Sofie Mosegaard,Dan Saattrup Smart,Simon Enni,Isaac Chung,Sofie Bruun,Ayush Sunil Munot,Max Müller-Eberstein,Adnan El-Assadi,Elisa Bassignana,Gianluca Barmina,Hafsteinn Einarsson,Iben Nyholm Debess,Linda Freienthal,Lukas Galke Poech,Mike Zhang,Nicolas Legrand,Vladimir Salnikov,Yevhen Kostiuk,Zafar Hussain,Sagandeep Kaur,Agnes Toftgård,Marie Mattson,Kristoffer Nielbo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究利用多元 GSM-Symbolic 数据集评估跨语言能力转移，并量化了模型大小、语言资源、推理和类型学距离等因素对未来语言性能的影响，解释了92%的跨语言变化，但只有23%的模型-语言变化，并能够预测未知语言的性能偏差在6.0pp之内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前评估跨语言能力转移的数据集存在不足，缺乏共同可比的数据资源，同时对转移的决定因素研究不够全面。因此，引入多元 GSM-Symbolic 数据集，并量化了影响因素，旨在改进开发者可以忽略所有语言对进行全面评估并针对性地改进低资源语言。

Method: 研究团队开发了多元 GSM-Symbolic，一个跨15种语言的数学问题集，包含3万个项目匹配的问题-答案对。该集利用符号模板来防止过拟合并确保泛化，通过从单个样本生成数百万高质量的变化来实现。使用此数据集来评估跨语言能力转移，并通过统计模型量化关键因素对性能的影响。

Result: 研究表明，模型大小、语言资源、推理和类型学距离是能力转移的关键决定因素，模型大小和推理能缩小低资源语言与高资源语言之间的性能差距，但对类型学距离差异影响不大。整体来说，该分析框架解释了92%的跨语言变异，但只有23%的模型-语言变异，能够预测未知语言的性能偏差在6.0pp之内。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，通过减少评估语言对的数量，可以直接通过模型大小和推理提升低资源语言的性能。此外，只需要10个模板的测量就能减少偏差至4.19pp。总体而言，本研究提供了一种有效的方法来评估和提高跨语言模型的性能。

Abstract: We understand little about how capabilities acquired in one language carry over to another, or what governs this transfer: evaluations rely on incomparable, saturation-prone datasets and rarely examine its determinants jointly. Identifying what predicts transfer would let us avoid exhaustive evaluation across all language pairs and let developers target the factors that limit performance in low-resource languages. To evaluate cross-lingual capability transfer, we introduce Multilingual GSM-Symbolic, an extensible multilingual mathematical dataset covering 30,000 item-matched question-answer pairs and spanning 15 languages. It utilises symbolic templates to prevent overfitting and ensure generalisation by allowing generation of millions of high-quality variations from a single sample. Using Multilingual GSM-Symbolic, we quantify the largest determinants of capability as model size ($β= 1.77$), language resource level ($β= 0.77$), reasoning ($β= 0.67$) and typological distance ($β= -0.25$). This joint estimation allows these determinants to be expressed in terms of one another: a 32B model evaluated in Marathi performs like a 10B model in English. Our findings have important implications for model developers, showing that model size and reasoning narrow the performance gap between low- and high-resource languages ($β= -0.27$ and $β= -0.20$, respectively), while similar levers have little or no effect on typologically distant languages.
  Overall, our analysis framework explains 92% of between-language variation, but only 23% of the model-by-language variation, and predicts a model's performance on an unseen language within 6.0pp (r=.96). Incorporating measurements from just 10 templates in the target language reduces this to 4.19pp, enabling reasonable estimates of performance with little or no downstream dataset.

</details>


### [249] [PLCWorld: Benchmarking LLM-Generated PLC Programs in Closed-Loop Plant Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02982)
*Yunji Kim,Yunseok Lee,Hyunwoo Seo,Jaerim Choi,Woojin Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PLCWorld 提供了一个用于评估大型语言模型生成的 PLC 程序的环境和基准，通过模拟实际工控环境中的设备和传感器反馈来验证程序的正确性和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发 PLCWorld 的动机是为了解决随着工业自动化向智能化方向发展而带来的挑战，特别是需要确保由大型语言模型自动生成的 PLC 程序能够满足实际应用中的任务要求和安全标准。

Method: PLCWorld 通过建立一个闭环执行环境，将结构化文本（Structured Text, ST）执行与模拟的生产设备响应和传感器反馈相结合。该系统设置了100个合成任务和473个任务条件对，涵盖运动控制和物料搬运领域，难度指标基于控制依赖范围。系统通过专家审查、参考程序、替代程序、针对性反例以及独立 ST 运行时的比较来进行验证。

Result: 经过验证，PLCWorld 可以为评估基于大语言模型的 PLC 程序的成功率和安全性提供新的基准。GPT-5.5 直接生成的 PLC 程序在容易的案例中达到82.70%的成功率，在困难的案例中仅为25.10%。此外，研究还揭示了六种不同的大语言模型和四个适应性生成-验证工作流之间的差异，特别是在完成任务、确保安全性和生成效率方面。

Conclusion: PLCWorld 为评估大型语言模型生成的 PLC 程序的具体成效提供了一个系统的方法，有助于在智能工业自动化领域制定更加安全和可靠的规范。

Abstract: Programmable logic controllers (PLCs) coordinate industrial equipment by reading sensor inputs and issuing control commands. Evaluating whether large language model (LLM)-generated PLC programs satisfy task requirements and safety constraints requires observing how their commands affect device and workpiece states. We introduce PLCWorld, a common closed-loop execution environment and benchmark that couples Structured Text (ST) execution with simulated plant responses and sensor feedback. Grounded in control relations identified in industrial PLC programs and engineering documentation, PLCWorld contains 100 synthetic tasks and 473 registered task-condition pairs across Motion Control and Material Handling, with difficulty defined by control-dependency scope. A common protocol reports Task Success and Safety Violation separately. Validation combines practitioner review, reference and alternative programs, targeted counterexamples, specification-evaluator alignment checks, and comparisons with independent ST runtimes. Reference and alternative programs satisfy their applicable cases, while all 542 targeted counterexamples activate their designated evaluator rules under at least one registered condition. Execution Gap relates submission-profile acceptance to subsequent task failure or observed Safety Violation. Across the constructed task groups, direct GPT-5.5 achieves 82.70% Task Success on Easy cases but 25.10% on Hard cases. Evaluations of six LLMs and four adapted generation-and-verification workflows further expose differences between completion, safety, and generation cost.
