</details>


### [286] [Credit Where It Matters: Dependency-Aware Policy Optimization for Terminal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03634)
*Yu Li,Guangfeng Cai,Long-Fei Li,Shuo Han,Shengtian Yang,Han Luo,Kaibing Yang,Lei Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个名为Dependency-Aware Group Policy Optimization (DepGPO)的方法，通过识别命令间的执行依赖关系来改进强化学习中的信用分配，从而在复杂终端任务中提高性能和学习稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的轨迹级和步骤级的信用分配方法无法明确跟踪命令之间的读写依赖关系，这可能导致不相关操作被错误地赋予学习信号，从而削弱从相关步骤中学到的知识。

Method: 该方法首先从执行轨迹构建命令依赖图，然后从任务验证器检查的资源反向追踪，将信用分配给相关写操作及其支持的读操作，并用于在步骤间重新分配轨迹优势。

Result: 广泛的比较实验和消融研究证明，DepGPO在复杂终端任务中提高了任务性能和训练稳定性。

Conclusion: 实验表明，该方法有效提高了基于终端的代理的任务完成能力和学习的鲁棒性，为复杂任务中的强化学习信用分配提供了一种新的视角。

Abstract: Terminal-using agents benefit from reinforcement learning (RL) in coding, debugging, and other multi-step terminal tasks. In these tasks, later commands often depend on information or intermediate results produced by earlier commands. However, existing trajectory-level and step-level credit assignment methods do not explicitly trace the read-write dependencies through which commands affect the final outcome. Consequently, training signals could still be assigned to irrelevant operations, weakening learning from relevant steps. In this paper, we propose Dependency-Aware Group Policy Optimization (DepGPO), which uses execution dependencies between commands to guide credit assignment for terminal agents. Specifically, we construct a command dependency graph from execution traces and trace backward from the resources inspected by the task verifier. We then assign credit to relevant writes and their supporting reads along these paths, and use it to redistribute trajectory advantages across steps. Extensive comparative experiments and ablation studies demonstrate that DepGPO improves task performance and training stability on complex terminal tasks.

</details>


### [287] [Do Large Language Models Know Colombian Law? A Reliability Benchmark for the Colombian Legal System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03639)
*Rubén Manrique,Michelle Castellanos,Jorge Morales,Juan David Gutiérrez,Antonio Barreto Rozo,Joaquín Vélez Navarro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究引入了一个针对哥伦比亚法律系统的专家验证基准，评估了15个当代模型在不同问题类型和法律领域的表现，发现模型的答案相关性与正确性存在较大偏差，尤其是在自由文本答案中的事实正确率较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估大型语言模型（LLMs）在哥伦比亚法律系统中的可靠性，填补了相关研究的空白。

Method: 研究人员构建了一个包含1,042个项目的基准，涵盖十个法律领域和三种问题格式，通过人类在环（human-in-the-loop）流程进行多阶段专家审阅。评价了15个当代的私有和开放权重模型，使用格式适应的指标来衡量准确率。

Result: 闭合问题的准确率范围广泛，从0.905（Gemini 3.1 Pro）到0.577，而对于自由文本的答案，事实正确率从未超过0.45。评估显示，模型在回答相关性与正确性之间存在较大差异，尤其在非专家用户群体中。闭合问题准确性和自由文本正确性高度相关，但闭合问题的排名可以通过廉价的多项选择筛选预测，但不能完全反映绝对可靠性。独立的评分框架和盲评人类专家都再现了自由文本排名。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，当前的LLMs需要专家监督才能用于哥伦比亚法律任务，并提出将答案与权威来源联系起来作为提高可靠性的前景路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to support legal practice, education, and research, yet their reliability in national legal systems outside the United States remains largely undocumented. We introduce an expert-validated benchmark for evaluating LLM reliability on the Colombian legal system. The benchmark comprises 1,042 items spanning ten areas of law and three question formats (closed multiple-choice, semi-open, and open-ended IRAC), built through a human-in-the-loop pipeline with multi-stage expert review. We evaluate 15 contemporary proprietary and open-weight models with format-appropriate metrics. Accuracy on closed questions ranges widely, from 0.905 (Gemini 3.1 Pro) to 0.577, but on free-text legal answers factual correctness never exceeds 0.45 (on a 0-1 scale) for any model. We find a dissociation between answer relevancy and correctness (Spearman rho = -0.46): models reliably sound responsive while frequently being wrong, a pattern of particular concern for non-expert users. Closed-question accuracy and free-text correctness are strongly rank-correlated (rho = 0.94), so cheap multiple-choice screening predicts model ranking but overstates absolute reliability. An independent rubric-based LLM judge and blind human expert scoring both reproduce the free-text ranking (rho >= 0.88). The judge further reveals that only about half of the norms models cite are correct; the rest are wrong or non-existent. Reliability varies systematically by legal area and follows an inverted-U across question complexity. Our results indicate that current LLMs require expert supervision for Colombian legal tasks, and that grounding answers in authoritative sources is a promising path to higher reliability. We release the benchmark construction pipeline to support reproducible evaluation.

</details>


### [288] [MRVQ: One Resident Index for Dimension- and Rate-Elastic Vector Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03651)
*Sean Culatana,Shang-En Huang,Kang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为Matryoshka Residual Vector Quantization（MRVQ）的新量化方法，旨在减少内存使用同时在不同的嵌入维度和索引码率之间切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集检索服务需要根据延迟、质量和内存预算调整嵌入前缀维度和索引位率。单独为每种速率调优量化器可以提供最优的效果，但检索阶段需要同时持有多个码流和量化器状态。研究动机是开发一种可以在不同嵌入维度和速率为检索任务提供支持的解决方案。

Method: 研究提出了一种名为MRVQ的后置残差量化器，其最大速率代码可以有两种方式被截断：减少残留阶段的数量以降低速率，或者减少嵌入坐标以降低维度。MRVQ设计的目标是在保留质量的同时大幅减少内存消耗。

Result: 研究结果表明，在FiQA和NFCorpus数据集上的不同嵌入家族和码率组合下，MRVQ是最低RAM使用量的设计。相比于三个独立训练的QINCo2索引，MRVQ节省了17.8-22.0倍的内存，相比于一个轻量共享模型的假象，则节省了1.89-2.02倍的内存。

Conclusion: 总之，尽管MRVQ在某些情况下比每速率独立训练的QINCo2稍逊色一点性能，但它是在匹配代码大小下击败PQ，OPQ和AdANNS-OPQ的方法。此外，还提出了一种低构建成本的PCA标量设计，其性能可媲美RaBitQ及其扩展版本，并且具有420倍更快的介质查询速度。最后，研究也指出QINCo2在高率下训练时会崩溃，这表明MRVQ更适合用于弹性检索中的低内存运行点。

Abstract: Dense-retrieval services must switch among embedding-prefix dimensions and index bit rates as latency, quality, and memory budgets change. Tuning a quantizer separately for each rate gives the best quality, but the retrieval tier then holds several code streams and quantizer states at once. We introduce Matryoshka Residual Vector Quantization (MRVQ), a post-hoc residual quantizer for frozen embeddings. Its maximum-rate code can be truncated two ways: dropping residual stages lowers the rate, and dropping embedding coordinates lowers the dimension. One resident artifact therefore serves every (dimension, rate) pair we evaluate. Across FiQA and NFCorpus, four embedding families, and {4, 8, 16}-byte codes, MRVQ is the lowest-RAM design we evaluate. It uses 17.8-22.0x less memory than three separately trained QINCo2 indices, and 1.89-2.02x less than a lean shared-model steelman. The saving is not free: per-rate QINCo2 is 0.026-0.107 nDCG@10 better on FiQA. But MRVQ beats PQ, OPQ, and AdANNS-OPQ at matched code size. We also evaluate a low-build-cost PCA-scalar design that attains quality comparable to RaBitQ and its extension while fitting 420x faster at the median. Finally, we report two negative results: QINCo2 collapses when trained at high rates, and a ranking-bound hypothesis misses its pre-specified acceptance criteria. MRVQ is therefore a low-memory operating point for elastic retrieval, not a universal quality winner.

</details>


### [289] [Transcriptome-informed multi-modal AI for predicting neoadjuvant therapy response from breast cancer biopsies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03693)
*Jungkyu Park,Dhruva Biswas,Joseph Cappadona,Cerise Tang,Ken G. Zeng,Bartosz Machura,Chuwen Liu,Paolo Tarantino,Coral Omene,Francisco J. Esteva,Rohit Bhargava,Marcin Braun,Kamila Paździerz,Jakub Czerwiński,Hanna Romańska-Knight,Albert Grinshpun,Bareket Daniel,Michele Buchinger,Frederick Howard,Piotr Wysocki,Brie Chun,Freya Schnabel,Rich Caruana,Jan Witowski,Krzysztof J. Geras*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个两阶段AI模型来预测乳腺癌新辅助治疗后的病理完全缓解（pCR），通过转录组从病理学中学习，并简化了从推断出的表达和临床变量预测pCR的第二阶段模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限的带标签数据限制了在肿瘤学中开发深度学习生物标志物的进步。本文的动机是克服这一限制，开发一个能够准确预测新辅助治疗后病理完全响应（pCR）的两阶段AI模型。

Method: 该研究包含两个阶段：第一阶段通过从32种癌症类型共8,742名患者的病理学数据中学习转录组来进行；第二阶段则根据推断出的表达和临床变量预测pCR。模型在五个组中的1,080名患者数据上进行开发，并在九个组中的1,412名患者数据上进行评估。

Result: 该模型在所有患者数据中达到了0.79（95%置信区间：0.73-0.85）的AUROC值，能够在分子亚型内区分应答者。与组织学生物标志物相比，该模型表现更优，并且在肿瘤内部采样不同和最少组织取材情况下保持稳定。此外，模型的实证结果证明，转录组级别的推断比单独使用临床变量或单阶段的病理学模型更能提高区分能力，并且具有更强的鲁棒性，避免了基因选择对基因组学检测的约束。

Conclusion: 这些研究结果表明，生物信息学启发的数据压缩可能在精准肿瘤学中的数据稀缺应用中普遍有效。

Abstract: Scarcity of labeled data limits development of deep learning biomarkers in oncology. We develop a two-stage AI model predicting pathological complete response (pCR) to neoadjuvant therapy in breast cancer. The first stage learns the transcriptome from histopathology using 8,742 patients across 32 cancer types, corroborated by pathologist review and spatial agreement with measured expression. This simplifies the second stage to predicting pCR from inferred expression and clinical variables. Developed using 1,080 patients (five cohorts) and evaluated in 1,412 patients (nine cohorts), the model achieves a pooled AUROC of 0.79 (95% CI, 0.73-0.85), discriminating responders within molecular subtypes. It outperforms histopathological biomarkers, remaining stable across intratumoral sampling and with minimal biopsy tissue. Ablations show transcriptome-wide inference improves discrimination over clinical variables alone or one-stage pathology models, and robustness by avoiding genomic assays' gene selection constraints. These results indicate that biologically informed compression may generalize to data-sparse applications in precision oncology.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [290] [A Compression Tree Generation Method for High Performance VLSI Datapaths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02228)
*Christophe Giacomotto*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于加权位堆的延迟感知压缩树方法，该方法通过重新排序位并在减少阶段使用Dadda风格的高带宽拓扑优化延迟。与Wallace风格的最大压缩树相比，该方法在Radix-4布斯乘法器中表现出较低的延迟和逻辑体积。研究还展示了如何评估由于保留单独产品的冗余格式而产生的微体系结构约束成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大矩阵乘法结构在计算领域占据更重要的地位，人们对压缩树的理解需要重新定义。本文旨在从加权位堆的角度分析压缩树，不仅仅将其视为乘法器的一部分，而是一种更通用的计算结构。

Method: 该研究提出了一种基于加权位堆的延迟感知压缩树方法。它通过每个位的重要性和相对到达时间来描述位，并使用技术和独立的归一化延迟单位来估算布尔逻辑压力。最后，该方法利用加法的可交换性在每一列内重新排序位，并使用Dadda风格的高带宽拓扑和灵活的CSA3:2运算符。

Result: 对于11到64位的Radix-4布斯乘法器，该方法产生的树模型延迟更低，逻辑体积更小，优于Wallace风格的最大压缩树。在融合四重整数点乘中，这种表示方法也能评估微体系结构约束成本，例如保持个体产品的冗余格式。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了加权位堆生成提供了一种实际的技术独立基础，可用于探索超过独立乘法器的压缩树。

Abstract: Compression trees have been historically a key component of several arithmetic units, most notably multipliers. The latter have been for the most part standalone units and in common situations synthesis tools can generate very close to optimal solutions. With large matrix multiply structures taking more of a central role in the compute industry, the need for a better understanding of these compression trees is apparent. Furthermore, the boundary of its usage needs to be redefined: dot products, polynomial fitting of non-linear operators, are all sums of products. And since products are sums of partial products, in the end, we are just adding many things together as bit heaps; i.e. the fundamental role of a compression tree. Therefore, we must see them as more than just a component of a multiplier. This paper presents a delay-aware compression-tree methodology based on a weighted bit heap. Each bit is described by its significance and relative arrival time, and the generator uses technology-independent normalized delay units to estimate Boolean logical pressure. By exploiting the commutativity of addition within each column, the method reorders bits across reduction stages and uses a Dadda-style maximum-bandwidth topology with flexible CSA3:2 operators. For Radix-4 Booth multipliers from 11 to 64 bits, the resulting trees show lower modeled delay and estimated logic volume than the evaluated Wallace-style maximum-compression trees. Fused four-way integer dot products further demonstrate how the same representation can evaluate the cost of microarchitectural constraints, such as preserving individual product redundant formats. These results show that weighted bit-heap generation provides a practical technology-independent basis for exploring compression trees beyond standalone multiplication.

</details>


### [291] [Efficient FlashAttention on Blackwell via Fixed-Shift Softmax and Persistent Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02229)
*Oleksandr Stashuk,Hongtao Yu,Jay Shah*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种FA4风格的流水线实现，并在Triton TLX中使用固定偏移密集softmax和保存逆分子，以提高注意力核的吞吐量。通过具体优化算子，该实现相比FA4在B200上提供了在32,768令牌和128个头维度的24种情况下，总体几何平均吞吐量提高了7.3%，前向和后向的密集计算分别提高了13.0%和10.4%，而因果后向计算提高了8.0%，但因果前向计算略慢1.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提升基于注意力机制的语言模型推理时的计算效率，在不改变原有模型框架的基础上，通过流水线优化、算子优化等手段来提高实际算子的执行效率。

Method: 作者在Triton TLX中实现了FA4风格的流水线，包括固定偏移密集softmax、保存逆分子、有选择的回收路径、跳过全掩码对角收缩等技术，并利用BF16格式和并行dQ发布减少每项工作量和芯片内部数据迁移。

Result: 该实现方案在B200上通过了准备好的启动并在1000瓦功耗下，相对于FA4在24种情况下的总体几何平均吞吐量提高了7.3%，其中前向和后向密集计算分别提高了13.0%和10.4%，因果后向计算提高了8.0%，但因果前向计算略慢1.5%。

Conclusion: TritonTlx在多个方面进行的优化在实际应用中表现出色，特别是在大规模训练和推理中能够显著提升计算效率。

Abstract: On Blackwell, normalization and operand movement can limit attention kernels whose matrix multiplications are already deeply pipelined. We implement an FA4-style pipeline in Triton TLX with fixed-shift dense softmax and a saved inverse denominator for backward. The fixed shift removes recurrent accumulator corrections; the saved reciprocal moves row normalization from the quadratic backward loop into linear preprocessing. The guarded causal path uses a first-block anchor with selective recovery, and fully masked diagonal contractions are skipped. A packed BF16 exponential approximation and pipelined dQ publication reduce per-element work and on-chip movement. On B200 at 1,000 W, using prepared launches and the original FA4 timer, TLX improves geometric-mean throughput by 7.3 percent across the 24 cases in FA4's BF16 variable-batch grid through 32,768 tokens at head dimension 128. Dense forward and backward improve by 13.0 and 10.4 percent, respectively. Causal backward improves by 8.0 percent, while causal forward is 1.5 percent slower. The TritonBench comparison has geometric- mean gains of 13.4 percent in forward and 24.4 percent in backward

</details>


### [292] [Budgeted Cache Repair for Cross-Context KV-Cache Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02233)
*Haeyong Kang,Chang D. Yoo*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 研究表明跨上下文Key-Value缓存重用带来的不仅仅是质量损失，还存在隐藏的成本和决策单位的不当选择。通过有选择地重新计算部分缓存，可以有效减少错误。Budgeted Cache Repair (BCR) 在特定单元级别上选择性地重新计算缓存，能够恢复GSM8K的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究发现，尽管跨上下文Key-Value缓存重用能够预测共享段的键值，但在实际应用中会损失准确性。为了解决这个问题，提出了Budgeted Cache Repair (BCR) 方法，旨在减轻这些成本。

Method: BCR 方法通过从缓存中抽取两个标记，按这些标记关注程度对缓存行进行排序，并在3种布局中精确计算固定预算的缓存行。这种方法可以在不预测成本的情况下支付成本，并且能够超越随机选择的性能。

Result: BCR 方法在GSM8K数据集上恢复了稠密填充的准确性，并且在最好的布局下，其性能超过了所有重用基线方法的平均值。同时，在相同预算下，抽样方式也优于随机选择。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，选择性地重新计算部分缓存行可以有效地改善准确性，并且BCR 方法在降低重用成本方面的表现超过了现有的重用基线方法。

Abstract: Cross-context KV-cache reuse predicts a shared segment's keys and values under a new prefix instead of recomputing them, and has been reported to do so without quality loss. We find otherwise, and identify two problems. (1) A hidden cost: on MMLU and GSM8K, reuse costs substantial accuracy. (2) A decision at the wrong unit: no rule for deciding whether to reuse a cache removes that cost. What does help is choosing which parts of the cache to recompute, and the value of choosing well falls as the unit of choice grows: informed selection removes 49.5% of the cache error beyond chance at single rows (one token's keys and values), 10.6% at 64-token chunks, and nothing at the level of whole calls. Budgeted Cache Repair (BCR) acts at the unit where selection still pays. It drafts two tokens from the assembled cache, ranks cache rows by the attention those tokens pay them, and recomputes a fixed budget of rows exactly, in one of three layouts. The cost is paid rather than predicted away, and the draft that fails as a gate succeeds as a selector. BCR restores GSM8K to dense-prefill accuracy while still serving most calls from cache, and its best layout outperforms every reuse baseline's mean in the reference grid. The draft also beats a coin-flip selector at the same budget - a control prior evaluations lack.

</details>


### [293] [CORE: COverage CAlibration and Evicted-Mass REdistribution for KV Cache](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02235)
*Shuxin Liu,Qing Liu,Yi Du,Ou Wu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的KV缓存压缩方法CORE，通过精确分解移除误差，引入了覆盖校准和移除质量重分布策略，优化缓存分配和内存写入方式，从而提升了压缩率和解码效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着长上下文解码对关键值缓存性能的限制，现有方法的局限性导致了压缩效率和准确性的权衡。本研究目的是通过改进缓存管理和内存写入策略来解决这一问题。

Method: CORE方法通过精确分解移除误差，设计了覆盖校准和移除质量重分布策略，并且在一个轻量级的缓存感知索引中结合了查询效用和对数值覆盖度。在推理时，一个校准分布驱动了两个通道：Top-B顺序保留互补的KV状态，而排除的分配质量和条件权重参数化潜忆写入，无须额外的写权预测器或在线对数值重评估。

Result: 在三种不同的骨干网络上，CORE方法在90%压缩率下超过了最强的RULER基线3.78点；长基准和多次移除评估进一步证实了其强大有效性和解码效率。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种新的KV缓存压缩方法CORE。该方法显著提升了压缩率和解码效率，并通过实验证明其有效性和效率。

Abstract: Long-context decoding is increasingly constrained by key--value (KV) cache memory and bandwidth. Existing fixed-budget compression methods typically separate retention from compensation, while a retention ranking specifies neither discarded attention mass nor the direction of induced output error. We start from an exact factorization: eviction error equals evicted attention mass times the directional gap between the evicted centroid and retained output, highlighting the importance of set-level coverage in retention and mass-preserving memory writing. We introduce CORE COverage Calibration and Evicted-Mass REdistribution for KV Cache, which distills an offline allocation combining query utility and log-determinant coverage into a lightweight cache-aware indexer. At inference, one calibrated distribution drives both channels: its Top-$B$ ordering retains complementary KV states, while its excluded allocation mass and conditional weights parameterize latent-memory writes without a separate write-weight predictor or online log-determinant evaluation. Our analysis provides a four-term pre-compensation error certificate, characterizes non-additive coverage interactions, and establishes mass-independent write stability with hierarchical bounds through recurrent and query-adaptive normalization. Across three backbones, CORE exceeds the strongest RULER baseline by up to 3.78 points at 90\% compression; LongBench and repeated-eviction evaluations further demonstrate strong effectiveness and decoding efficiency.

</details>


### [294] [An Adaptive Heterogeneous Architecture for High-Ratio, High-Throughput Lossless Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02236)
*Zeyu Jia*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: GPX是一种适应性异构压缩架构，适用于结构化数据流，可以透明地处理任意长度的流（从小于1千字节到大块），实现了高性能压缩和解压缩，特别是在企业级和科学工作负载中。它在同一基准测试中表现出色，并超越了标准配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的压缩算法要么在压缩密度上占优势但速度较慢，要么速度快但压缩效果较差。GPX旨在弥合这一差距，通过整合高速内部决策探针、GPU原生逆变换、多假设序列优化和自适应熵边界，提供了一种高性能的压缩解决方案。

Method: GPX结合了低至15微秒的内部结构决策探针，基于GPU的逆变换，AVX2多假设序列优化，以及自适应熵边界。它不仅在企业级和科学工作负载下表现出色，还能处理小至256字节的输入数据。

Result: 在Silesia基准测试中，GPX Track A在保持高标准解压缩速度的同时实现了56.27 MiB的压缩，超过所有RFC 8878兼容配置。 GPX Track B进一步优化了结果，达到了54.49 MiB和259.76 MiB/s的速度，严格胜过现有标准配置。

Conclusion: GPX提供了一种高效的压缩方法，特别适用于企业级和科学应用中的大块数据流，同时保持了高质量的压缩效果。其性能优于现有标准配置，并且在不同基准测试中展示了良好的稳健性。

Abstract: Modern lossless data compression enforces an acute dichotomy: industrial streaming codecs (e.g., Zstandard) prioritize throughput (10 to 1000 MiB/s) at the expense of compression density, while context-mixing algorithms achieve superior ratios at unusable serial speeds (0.1 to 1 MiB/s). We present GPX, an adaptive heterogeneous compression architecture for structured data streams. While optimized for high-throughput enterprise and scientific large-block workloads (>= 4 MiB), GPX operates transparently across arbitrary stream lengths down to sub-kilobyte inputs (N >= 256 B). GPX combines a sub-15-microsecond native structural decision probe, GPU-native reversible domain transforms, AVX2 multi-hypothesis sequence optimization, and adaptive entropy boundaries. On the canonical Silesia benchmark (202.12 MiB) under a preregistered 7-repeat Median+IQR protocol (W = 6 workers), GPX Track A produces standard RFC 8878-compliant .zst streams that compress to 56.27 MiB at 235.41 MiB/s with native line-speed decompression (3074.27 MiB/s on unmodified libzstd), establishing non-domination among evaluated RFC 8878-compatible configurations. GPX Track B achieves 54.49 MiB at 259.76 MiB/s (CI [250.7, 287.6]), establishing an empirical Pareto frontier point and dominating Stock Levels 8-14. GPX Track A+B achieves 54.46 MiB at 191.90 MiB/s (CI [177.8, 206.7]), strictly dominating official Stock Zstandard Levels 10-14 under non-overlapping 95% Bootstrap confidence intervals (182.18 KB smaller than Level 14 and 1.15x to 18.70x faster than Levels 10-14). Zero-tuning out-of-sample generalization is confirmed across Canterbury (-0.650%), Calgary (-0.124%), and a 102.45 MB modern compiled binary (-0.246%), while enwik8 (+0.084%) documents the authentic failure mode of splitter over-segmentation. All outputs are 100% SHA-256 bit-exact round-trip verified.

</details>


### [295] [Calibrating the Checksum: An Empirical False-Positive Bound for One-Sided ABFT on bfloat16 GEMM, and What It Fails to Catch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02240)
*Gautam Khosla*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该研究通过使用广义帕累托分布来调整阈值，以适应bfloat16数据类型的矩阵乘法检查和修正方法，并发现单个指数位翻转的检出率为76%，未能达到预设的90%标准，而低尾数翻转则全部未被检测到。研究表明，检出能力与位的位置相关，而不是与运算长度相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在解决在bfloat16数据类型下经典阈值不适用的问题，通过重新校准阈值来提高算法检查和修正方法的有效性，尤其是在运算长度较长的情况下更为重要。

Method: 研究通过生成大约7.99e7个清洁的单行残差，拟合广义帕累托分布，并基于该分布来推导阈值。另外，该研究使用了数据和代码公开的方式，并对预注册文档做了时间戳记录。

Result: 研究发现，在bfloat16数据类型下，单个指数位翻转的检出率为76%，低尾数翻转则全部未能被检测到。检测能力与位的位置相关，而与运算长度无关。此外，研究还未能在其研究设计中的预设标准内达到预期的检出率。

Conclusion: 虽然该研究成功地为bfloat16数据类型下的矩阵乘法检查和修正方法提出了适用的阈值，但在某些情况下（如低尾数翻转）的检出率仍然不足。未来的研究需要进一步提高检测的准确性。

Abstract: Algorithm-based fault tolerance detects corrupted matrix multiplications by comparing a checksum of the product against a checksum recomputed from the factors, flagging a fault when the residual exceeds a threshold. The classical threshold is tau = Ku, derived from a dot-product rounding bound that requires Ku <= 0.01. At bfloat16 with contraction length K = 4096, Ku is approximately 32, so the bound does not hold and the threshold is not defined. Prior work therefore evaluated checksum ABFT only at float32. We supply the missing calibration. This paper is a characterization of a detector under an output-level, post-GEMM perturbation model; it is not a characterization of faults in silicon, and no rate reported here estimates fault incidence in deployed hardware. We fit a Generalized Pareto distribution to 7.99e7 clean per-row residuals and extrapolate a threshold at a per-GEMM false-positive rate of 1e-6, obtaining 3.1445e-6, which sits between the two clean sample maxima we observed. The independence assumption behind the per-GEMM conversion is checked empirically rather than asserted, at a variance ratio of 0.985 against a binomial reference. Against this threshold the detector catches 304 of 400 single exponent-bit flips (76%), failing a bar of 90% that was registered before any data was collected, and catches 0 of 600 low-mantissa flips. Detectability is invariant in K across a 16x range even though the clean noise floor falls as K^-0.4999: signal and floor scale together, so bit position governs detectability and contraction length does not. This falsified the mechanism the study was designed around. We report four registered predictions that failed, including that one, and four retractions of our own earlier findings, two of which were caught by pre-publication audit rather than by prediction. Code, data, and timestamped pre-registration documents are released.

</details>


### [296] [Hardware-Native Joint Sparse-Quantization for Trillion-Scale Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02241)
*Kwanhee Lee,Namhoon Lee,Dan Alistarh*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种端到端的硬件-软件协同设计框架，将专家权重压缩成硬件原生的低精度稀疏表示，并在稀疏张量核心（SpTCs）上加速其实现。结果显示，该框架在保持较高模型性能的同时提升了联合稀疏-量化的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）架构虽然可以处理大规模的语言模型，但由于需要巨大的内存和带宽资源，限制了其实际部署。虽然现代加速器提供了一种名为稀疏张量核心（SpTCs）的技术来减少权重存储并提高计算效率，但在MoE架构中应用仍然具有挑战性。

Method: 研究提出了一个端到端的硬件-软件协同设计框架，包括算法层面的连续重构和系统层面的定制组稀疏GEMM内核。算法层面通过连续重构放松了离散的半结构化支持选择，实现了带有路由器权重重建目标的量化权重的可微联合优化。系统层面则开发了一个针对SpTCs上的低精度稀疏MoE推理的定制化组稀疏GEMM内核。

Result: 实验结果表明，该框架在从300亿到1万亿参数的MoE模型中，将最新的联合稀疏-量化精度提高了最多4.35个百分点，同时保持了96.09%的原始模型性能。在NVIDIA B200 GPU上，该内核在服务吞吐量上提高了1.18倍，在端到端延迟上降低了最多4.03倍，相比厂商基线提升了1.65倍。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了硬件-软件协同设计框架在MoE模型部署的实用性，提供了一条走向大规模和高效MoE部署的可行路径。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures allow frontier language models to scale to trillions of parameters, but their deployment is constrained by massive memory footprints and memory-bandwidth limitations. Although modern accelerators provide Sparse Tensor Cores (SpTCs) that reduce weight storage and increase throughput through low-precision semi-structured sparsity, exploiting them for MoEs remains challenging because of substantial model-quality degradation and the lack of grouped sparse GEMM primitives. We present an end-to-end hardware-software co-design framework that compresses expert weights into hardware-native, low-precision sparse representations and accelerates their execution on SpTCs. Algorithmically, our framework relaxes discrete semi-structured support selection through continuous reparameterization, enabling differentiable joint optimization with quantized weights under a router-weighted reconstruction objective and scalable expert-parallel compression. Systemically, we develop a custom grouped sparse GEMM kernel tailored to low-precision sparse MoE inference on SpTCs. Across MoE models ranging from 30 billion to one trillion parameters, our framework improves state-of-the-art joint sparse-quantization accuracy by up to 4.35 percentage points while preserving 96.09% of the original model's performance. On NVIDIA B200 GPUs, our kernel outperforms the vendor baseline by up to $1.65\times$, increasing serving throughput by $1.18\times$ and reducing end-to-end latency by up to $4.03\times$. These results establish hardware-software co-design as a practical path toward scalable and efficient MoE deployment.

</details>


### [297] [AdaptViT: Runtime-Adaptive Vision Transformer Deployment on Custom RISC-V](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02288)
*Vishnu PS,Ajay Kumar M,Yike Li,Robert Bogdan Staszewski,Deepu John*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端部署管道，将预训练的Vision Transformers (ViTs)转换为单个运行时可配置的二进制文件，实现了嵌入式CPU上不同稀疏度级别的动态计算预算切换，减少了存储开销，并通过硬件对齐的块剪枝策略和ISA扩展提高了效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于高计算需求，将Vision Transformers部署在低功耗边缘设备上具有挑战性。传统的剪枝框架需要为每个稀疏性级别生成单独的编译二进制文件，增加了存储开销并限制了运行时的适应性。

Method: 提出了一种端到端部署管道，通过重新结构化生成的C内核，修改循环界限和二进制掩码控制逻辑，使执行可以在不同的稀疏度级别之间切换。引入了硬件对齐的块剪枝策略和ISA扩展，以最大化剪枝效率和性能。

Result: 与多二进制部署方法相比，提出的运行时自适应方法可以在设备上减少多达4.86倍的存储开销，仅需163 MB就能处理ViT-Base，而非近800 MB。此外，在Synopsys TRV32P3FX RISC-V处理器上，全系统在65%的MLP和50%的注意力头剪枝时，实现了2.8倍的加速，并降低了33%的推断能耗。

Conclusion: 这项研究提出了一种有效的方法来解决Vision Transformers在低功耗边缘设备上的部署挑战，通过减少存储开销和提高效率，展示了在嵌入式处理器上的性能提升。

Abstract: Deploying Vision Transformers (ViTs) on low-power edge devices is challenging due to high computational demands. Conventional pruning frameworks require a separate compiled binary for each sparsity level, increasing storage overhead and limiting runtime adaptability. This paper presents an end-to-end deployment pipeline that transforms pretrained ViTs into a single runtime-configurable binary, enabling dynamic compute-budget switching on embedded CPUs. This is achieved by restructuring generated C kernels with modified loop bounds and binary-mask control logic, allowing execution to switch across discrete sparsity levels via compact external configuration files. Compared to multi-binary deployment, the proposed runtime-adaptive approach reduces on-device storage by up to 4.86x, requiring only 163 MB for ViT-Base instead of nearly 800 MB. To maximize pruning efficiency, we introduce a hardware-aligned block pruning strategy for Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP) layers. In addition, a custom ISA extension is proposed to exploit input-reuse patterns in linear projection kernels. On a Synopsys TRV32P3FX RISC-V processor, the full system achieves up to 2.8x speedup at 65% MLP and 50% attention-head pruning for ViT-Base. The ISA extension alone provides a 1.56x speedup and 33% lower inference energy, with a 24.7% area overhead in a TSMC 28 nm implementation.

</details>


### [298] [Design Space Exploration of Backside Clock Meshes for 2 nm GAAFET BSPDN Technology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02401)
*Wajid Ali,Muhammad Hadir Khan,Dalton Gaddy,Matthew Guthaus*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 后侧时钟网格在GT2N 2纳米纳米片技术中对四个不同规模的基准进行探索，结果显示，与正面网格相比，后侧网格能够显著降低时序偏斜、接收下冲、功耗和正面时钟布线面积，且其时序偏斜的变异系数显著低于正面网格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的时钟网格在高-performance VLSI设计中依赖稀缺的工艺金属层，造成资源浪费。后侧电源传输网络提供了一种使用剩余金属层创建后侧时钟网格的新途径，绕过正面的翻转触发器，只经过硅通孔。

Method: 在OpenROAD平台上使用多目标贝叶斯优化方法探索四种不同规模的基准设计，每个设计点通过晶体管级SPICE仿真验证，避免了静态时序分析无法评价循环网格的问题。

Result: 所有四种设计均证明后侧网格性能优于等效的正面网格，平均时序偏斜降低45%，接收下冲降低25%，功率降低4.5%，正面时钟布线面积减少28%。通过10,000样本蒙特卡洛模拟，后侧网格的时序偏斜变异系数约为正面网格的三分之一。

Conclusion: 后侧时钟网格在多种基准设计中展现出优越性，提供了一种更有效的时钟分配方案。

Abstract: Clock meshes are used in high-performance VLSI designs to minimize skew and tolerate on-chip variation, but they spend scarce routing resources on premium metal layers. Backside power delivery creates a new option: it adds thick, low-resistance metal layers on the back of the wafer, and the power grid does not consume all of them. Flip-flops remain on the frontside; a backside mesh therefore cannot drive them directly, and every connection passes through a through-silicon via. We present the first design-space exploration of backside clock meshes, implemented in OpenROAD on GT2N, a 2 nm nanosheet technology. Four benchmarks (1,938 to 15,311 flip-flops) are explored with multi-objective Bayesian optimization, and every design point is verified by transistor-level SPICE simulation, since the cyclic mesh cannot be evaluated by static timing analysis. Across all four designs, the backside mesh consistently outperforms an identical frontside mesh, with on average 45% lower skew, 25% lower sink slew, 4.5% lower power, and 28% less frontside clock wiring, and its skew spread under 10,000-sample Monte Carlo is a third of the frontside mesh's.

</details>


### [299] [RAPID: Row-Parallel Arithmetic Processing in DRAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02502)
*William C. Tegge,João Paulo Cardoso de Lima,Shouzhi Fang,Jeronimo Castrillon,Alex K. Jones*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: RAPID通过在DRAM主体中添加迁移单元和反转单元，能够直接操作行并行、位并行的数据，保持CPU兼容的数据布局的同时利用DRAM阵列的巨大并行性。这减少了数据布局转换的成本，提高了乘法运算的并行归约效率，同时保持了DRAM阵列的常规组织结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的DRAM-处理架构需要将数据重新组织成列对齐、位串行的形式才能执行计算，但这与传统处理器和加速器使用的行对齐、字并行布局不兼容，导致在PUM和常规执行之间转换时需要昂贵的数据布局转换成本。为了降低成本并提高性能，提出了能够直接处理行并行数据的RAPID架构。

Method: 通过在DRAM子阵列中添加迁移单元和反转单元，使DRAM能够直接处理行并行、位并行的数据。这种设计使得DRAM可以实现局部水平通信，减少计算延迟，同时保持与CPU兼容的数据布局。

Result: 实验表明，与SIMDRAM在DDR4 PUM执行中的表现相比，RAPID在19个MLPerf基准测试中实现了5.9倍的端到端性能提高。

Conclusion: RAPID架构通过减少数据布局转换的成本，提高了乘法运算的并行归约效率，并保持了DRAM阵列的常规组织结构，从而实现了更高的性能。

Abstract: Processing-using-memory (PUM) architectures perform computation directly within DRAM to reduce costly data movement between memory and processors. Because charge-sharing operations are confined to individual bitlines, existing DRAM-PUM architectures reorganize data into column-oriented, bit-serial representations. This organization is fundamentally incompatible with the row-oriented, word-parallel layouts used by conventional processors and accelerators, requiring expensive data-layout transformations whenever computation transitions between PUM and conventional execution. In this paper, we present RAPID, a Row-parallel Arithmetic Processing-In-DRAM architecture. RAPID augments the DRAM subarray with two lightweight extensions: migration cells that enable localized horizontal data movement between neighboring bitlines and inversion cells that provide efficient in-array logical inversion. These primitives enable RAPID to operate directly on row-parallel, bit-parallel data, preserving CPU-compatible layouts while exploiting the massive parallelism of the DRAM subarray. In particular, RAPID demonstrates that localized horizontal communication is sufficient to realize shallow arithmetic networks and efficient parallel reduction for multiplication, reducing arithmetic latency while preserving throughput and eliminating costly data-layout transformations, all while maintaining the conventional DRAM array organization. We demonstrate the feasibility and overhead of augmenting DRAM subarrays with migration and inversion cells through detailed transistor-level layout and SPICE-validated circuit simulations. Using the RAPID compiler it is possible to evaluate the performance and data reorganization tradeoffs to ensure the best execution across combined CPU and PUM. Evaluating RAPID on 19 MLPerf benchmarks, there is a 5.9x higher end-to-end performance compared to SIMDRAM for DDR4 PUM execution.

</details>


### [300] [PEEK: Heterogeneous Parallelism for Privileged Error Detection in Safety-Critical Processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03045)
*Tinglue Wang,Zhenghui Guo,Bing Guo,Jiapeng Guan,Renshuang Jiang,Jie Zhang,Jing Li,Xin Si,Sam Ainsworth,Zhe Jiang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: .peek引入了一种新的高级别并行错误检测架构，旨在保护高级执行模式，该架构在硬件开销较低的情况下实现了对Linux的全面保护，且性能损失可以忽略不计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的异构并行错误检测体系结构在保护超标量处理器的非特权模式执行方面表现出卓越效果，但它们在真实部署中无法保护特权模式执行，.peek的设计就是为了弥补这一缺陷。

Method: 通过深入分析特权执行过程，.peek重新设计了验证管道，确保能够处理特权模式保护中遇到的所有瓶颈和问题。

Result: 在真实系统中使用多种指标评估，.peek在RTL级别实现了对Linux的全面特权保护，同时性能损失可以忽略，硬件开销也在可接受范围。

Conclusion: 该研究通过发布.PEEK设计及其实现，为安全关键系统的高级执行模式保护开启了新的可能性，适用于未来的硬件开发和系统安全性增强。

Abstract: Heterogeneous parallel error detection architecture has been widely studied for safeguarding OoO superscalar processors in safetycritical systems, as it achieves significantly lower hardware overhead compared to traditional LockStep, by exploiting the parallelism that exists in a secondary execution. However, previous works do not cover the protection of privileged-mode execution, impeding their effectiveness in real-world deployment. Moreover, naive extension to privileged-mode can cause a litany of issues, from abysmal performance due to high synchronization costs, to full deadlocks. Here, we present PEEK, the first privileged parallel error detection architecture. Based on a deep analysis of privileged execution, we redesign the verification pipeline, addressing all the bottlenecks and bugs identified in privileged-mode protection. Evaluated using various metrics on an RTL-level full system running Linux, PEEK achieves full-privilege protection on Linux with negligible performance slowdown and affordable hardware overhead. PEEK has been taped out using a 28nm process, and its source is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/PEEK-3000.

</details>


### [301] [Divide and conquer: Scalable performance and energy in MCM GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03061)
*Mario Ibáñez Bolado,Borja Pérez Pavón,Jose Luis Bosque Orero,Julio Ramón Beivide*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了多芯片模块（MCM）GPU上的计算和内存能力分布是否提供了比资源集中更具扩展性的替代方案。16个芯片的Torus配置在相同计算能力下实现了2.40倍的性能提升，并减少了4.45倍的能量消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前对多芯片模块（MCM）GPU的分解在性能可扩展性和能源消耗方面的影响了解不足。现有的研究没有明确的指导，因此进行这项研究旨在探讨是否可以利用分解来实现性能与能效的优化。

Method: 研究团队量化了分解对性能、能源和效率的影响，并通过研究不同规模系统中芯片间拓扑结构对可扩展性的影响来得出结论。

Result: 研究结果表明，使用包含256个SM的16个芯片的Torus配置，实现了与最先进的MCM架构相同的计算能力，但性能提升了2.40倍，能量消耗减少了4.45倍。

Conclusion: 研究团队的发现证明，分解是衡量下一代GPU性能和能源效率的关键因素，对于释放GPU的性能和能效潜力至关重要。

Abstract: Multi-chip-module (MCM) GPUs offer a promising path to scale compute capability beyond monolithic designs by integrating multiple chiplets on a common package. However, the impact of disaggregation on performance scalability and energy consumption remains underexplored. The design space grows rapidly across dimensions such as SMs per chiplet, chiplet count, and interconnection network. The inter-chiplet network is particularly critical, as it determines whether additional compute resources translate into performance gains. This limited understanding leaves industry and research without clear guidance on the performance and energy trade-offs of MCM GPU scaling. In this work, we investigate whether distributing compute and memory capability across multiple chiplets offers a more scalable alternative to concentrating resources. We quantify their effects on performance, energy, and efficiency and examine how inter-chiplet topology influences scalability at different system sizes. Our results demonstrate that a 16 chiplet Torus configuration with 256 SMs delivers a remarkable $2.40\times$ performance improvement over a state-of-the-art MCM architecture with the same compute capability, while simultaneously reducing energy consumption by $4.45\times$. These substantial gains provide evidence that disaggregation is a first-order architectural factor and will be critical to unlocking the performance and energy-efficiency potential of next-generation GPUs.

</details>


### [302] [Evidence-Guided Repository-Level RTL Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03219)
*Yuxin Du,Juxin Niu,Zhe Jiang,Nan Guan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个基于证据的框架，包括失败定位、波形导向的故障定位以及一致性修复验证三个模块，以解决跨文件和时钟周期的RTL修复问题，实验结果优于baseline。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在修复跨文件和时钟周期的RTL故障时存在问题，它们只能揭示潜在行为而难以捕捉错误执行，也无法验证局部修复是否被一致地传播。

Method: 研究提出了一个包含三个模块的框架：失败定位、波形导向故障定位、以及一致性修复验证。失败定位将故障描述转换为再现失败运行和故障锚点；波形导向故障定位利用工具箱将观察到的故障窄化为候选机制和证据轨迹；一致性修复验证则扩展种子为协调修补，并重播场景以确认故障消失。

Result: 在HWE-Bench上的实验表明，该方法比基线方法性能更优。

Conclusion: 研究提出的方法可以有效解决跨文件和时钟周期的RTL修复问题，并通过实验验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Repository-level RTL repair must localize a failure that spans files, modules, and clock cycles, then propagate the fix consistently. Existing methods reason over source code, which reveals possible behaviors but not the failed execution, and cannot tell whether a local fix was propagated consistently. We therefore present an evidence-guided framework with three modules. Failure grounding converts a problem statement into a reproduced failing run and a failure anchor. Waveform-guided localization uses a localization toolbox to narrow the observed violation into a candidate mechanism and an evidence trail. Consistency-aware repair and validation then expand that seed into a coordinated patch and replay the same scenario to check that the violation disappears. We conducted experiments on HWE-Bench and achieved better performance than the baseline.

</details>
